        lambda: BrickwallLimiter(threshold_db=ceiling_db, release_ms=100.0, lookahead_ms=3.0),
        lambda: BrickwallLimiter(threshold_db=ceiling_db, release_ms=100.0),
        lambda: BrickwallLimiter(threshold_db=ceiling_db),
        lambda: BrickwallLimiter(ceiling_db=ceiling_db),
        lambda: BrickwallLimiter(),
    )
    for f in attempts:
        try:
            return f()
        except Exception:
            continue
    return None


def limit(x, sr, ceiling_db=CEILING_DB):
    """Master limiter. Priority order:
       1. numpy sidechain-HPF limiter (bass-preserving, from limiter.py)
       2. numpy lookahead limiter (fallback, also preserves crest)
       3. pedalboard BrickwallLimiter (last resort, clips flat)
    """
    y = None

    # Primary: ProductionMasteringLimiter (user-designed, sidechain HPF, M/S, ARC)
    try:
        from limiter import ProductionMasteringLimiter as _NewLim
        _lim = _NewLim(sample_rate=sr)
        y = _lim.process(x, input_gain_db=0.0, ceiling_db=ceiling_db,
                         release_ms=80.0, sc_hpf_freq=120.0, ms_link=0.85)
    except Exception:
        y = None

    # Fallback: numpy lookahead limiter (transparent, preserves crest)
    if y is None:
        try:
            y, _ = _lookahead_limiter(x, sr, ceiling_db,
                                      lookahead_ms=5.0, release_ms=80.0)
        except Exception:
            y = None

    # Fallback 2: pedalboard brickwall
    if y is None:
        b = _brick(ceiling_db)
        if b is not None:
            try:
                y = Pedalboard([b])(x, sr).astype(np.float32)
            except Exception:
                y = None
        if y is None:
            try:
                y = Pedalboard([Limiter(threshold_db=ceiling_db, release_ms=100.0)])(x, sr).astype(np.float32)
            except Exception:
                y = x.copy()

    # Safety trim: true peak enforcement
    tp = _true_peak_db(y)
    lim = 10.0 ** (ceiling_db / 20.0)
    if tp > ceiling_db:
        y = (y * (lim / (10.0 ** (tp / 20.0)))).astype(np.float32)
        tp = float(ceiling_db)
    return y, float(tp), True


# ---------- main ----------

def _band_carve(beat, lead, sr):
    import wratty_fix_v2
    ducked, _ = wratty_fix_v2.carve_beat(beat.T, lead.T, sr, fallback=False)
    return ducked.T

def mix(groups, sr, loud="MEDIUM"):
    beats = list(groups.get("beat") or [])
    others = list(groups.get("other") or [])
    rep = {"sr": int(sr)}
    _fx_plans = {}
    _fx_dets = {}
    try:
        rep["arrived"] = {}
        for _k in ("beat", "lead", "adlib", "backing", "response", "intro", "outro", "feature", "other"):
            _g = groups.get(_k) or []
            if _g:
                _s = _sum(_g)
                rep["arrived"][_k] = {"n": len(_g),
                                      "sec": round(_s.shape[1] / float(sr), 2),
                                      "rms_db": round(_rms_db(_s), 1)}
                if _k == "beat":
                    rep["arrived"][_k]["lufs"] = integrated_lufs(_s, sr)
    except Exception as _e:
        rep["arrived"] = {"error": str(_e)[:120]}

    beat = _sum(beats) if beats else None
    # Shift BEAT later (pad with silence) so vocal lands on the beat.
    ro_map = {}
    for r in ROLE_VOCALS:
        arrs = groups.get(r) or []
        if arrs:
            ro_map[r] = _sum(arrs)
            if len(arrs) > 1:
                try:
                    _ref = arrs[0]
                    _d = []
                    for _i, _a in enumerate(arrs):
                        _mm = min(_a.shape[1], _ref.shape[1])
                        _x = _a[:, :_mm].mean(0); _y = _ref[:, :_mm].mean(0)
                        _c = float(np.corrcoef(_x, _y)[0, 1]) if _i else 1.0
                        _d.append({"i": _i, "rms_db": round(_rms_db(_a), 1),
                                   "corr_vs_1": round(_c, 3) if np.isfinite(_c) else None})
                    _d.append({"sum_rms_db": round(_rms_db(ro_map[r]), 1)})
                    rep.setdefault("bus_diag", {})[r] = _d
                except Exception as _e:
                    rep.setdefault("bus_diag", {})[r] = str(_e)[:120]
    _oth = groups.get("other") or []
    if (beat is None and _oth
            and not any(groups.get(r) for r in ROLE_VOCALS)):
        rep["mode"] = "master"
        rep["note"] = "single full-mix stem - master bus, no vocal chain"
        return _master(_sum(_oth), sr, rep, loud)
    if _oth:
        _o = _sum(_oth)
        if _o is not None:
            beat = _o if beat is None else _sum([beat, _o])
            rep["other_folded_into_beat"] = len(_oth)
    voc = _sum(list(ro_map.values())) if ro_map else None
    
    # --- SAVE TRUE RAW SUM FOR A/B PREVIEW ---
    try:
        import soundfile as sf
        _raw_voc = voc if voc is not None else np.zeros((2, 1))
        _raw_beat = beat if beat is not None else np.zeros((2, 1))
        
        # Match lengths
        _max_len = max(_raw_voc.shape[1], _raw_beat.shape[1])
        if _raw_voc.shape[1] < _max_len: _raw_voc = np.pad(_raw_voc, ((0,0),(0,_max_len-_raw_voc.shape[1])))
        if _raw_beat.shape[1] < _max_len: _raw_beat = np.pad(_raw_beat, ((0,0),(0,_max_len-_raw_beat.shape[1])))
            
        _raw_mix = (_raw_voc + _raw_beat).astype(np.float32)
        sf.write('/tmp/raw_mix.wav', _raw_mix.T, sr)
    except Exception as _rwe:
        print('raw sum error:', _rwe)
    # -----------------------------------------

    if beat is None and voc is None:
        rep["mode"] = "sum"
        return _headroom(_sum(others)), rep

    if voc is None:
        rep["mode"] = "passthrough"
        rep["note"] = "no vocal stem - beat returned without reprocessing"
        return _headroom(beat * (10.0 ** (-1.5 / 20.0))), rep

    if beat is None:
        rep["mode"] = "vocal_only"
        st, _f = analyze(voc, voc, sr)
        parts, moves = [], {}
        for r in list(ro_map.keys()):
            # --- SMART FX DETECTOR ---
            try:
                if ro_map[r].shape[-1] > sr * 12:
                    _det = vocal_fx_detect.analyze(ro_map[r], sr, bpm=bpm)
                    _plan = vocal_fx_detect.engine_plan(_det)
                    _fx_plans[r] = _plan
                    _fx_dets[r] = _det
            except Exception as _fx_e:
                rep.setdefault("fx_detect_error", {})[r] = str(_fx_e)[:80]
            # --------------------------
            y, mv = _role_chain(ro_map[r], sr, st, r)
            parts.append(y); moves[r] = mv
        core = _sum(parts)
        if others:
            ex = _sum(others)
            n = max(core.shape[1], ex.shape[1])
            core = (_pad(core, n) + _pad(ex, n) * (10.0 ** (-4.0 / 20.0))).astype(np.float32)
        core, ms, pk = _ambience(_exciter(_parallel(_glue(core, sr), sr), sr), sr, detect_tempo(core, sr))
        rep["vocal_chain"] = moves
        rep["slap_ms"] = ms
        rep["plate"] = pk
        rep["plate_error"] = plate_error()
        try:
            rep["reverb_last"] = dict(_REVERB_LAST)
        except Exception:
            rep["reverb_last"] = {}
        try:
            rep["delay_last"] = dict(_DELAY_LAST)
        except Exception:
            rep["delay_last"] = {}
        try:
            rep["parallel_last"] = dict(_PAR_LAST)
            rep["exciter_last"] = dict(_EXC_LAST)
        except Exception:
            pass
        return _headroom(core), rep

    # ---- optional declip: only fires on genuinely damaged stems ----
    if _declip is not None:
        for _r in list(ro_map.keys()):
            try:
                _v = ro_map[_r]
                import wratty_fix_v2
                if not wratty_fix_v2.needs_declip(_v.T):
                    continue
                _fixed, _drep = _declip.declip(_v, sr)
                if _drep.get("repaired", 0) > 0:
                    ro_map[_r] = _fixed
                    rep.setdefault("declip", {})[_r] = _drep
            except Exception as _e:
                rep.setdefault("declip_error", {})[_r] = str(_e)[:120]

    # --- ADAPTIVE PHASE ALIGNMENT ---
    try:
        import vocal_align
        _v_stems = [ro_map[r] for r in ('lead', 'adlib', 'backing') if r in ro_map and ro_map[r] is not None]
        _lead_idx = 0 if 'lead' in ro_map else None
        if _v_stems and beat is not None and _lead_idx is not None:
            _aligned, _align_rep = vocal_align.align_group(_v_stems, beat, sr, lead_index=_lead_idx)
            rep['vocal_alignment'] = _align_rep
            _idx = 0
            for r in ('lead', 'adlib', 'backing'):
                if r in ro_map and ro_map[r] is not None:
                    ro_map[r] = _aligned[_idx]
                    _idx += 1
    except Exception as _ae:
        rep['vocal_alignment_error'] = str(_ae)[:150]

    _match_role_levels(ro_map, sr, rep)
    rep["mode"] = "two_track" if len(beats) == 1 else "stems"
    rep["roles"] = {r: len(groups.get(r) or []) for r in ROLE_VOCALS if groups.get(r)}
    bpm, _bpm_conf = detect_tempo(_mono(beat), sr, with_conf=True)
    if _bpm_conf < 0.15:
        bpm = 100.0
    rep["bpm"] = round(bpm, 1)

    freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(N, 1.0 / sr)
    Bst = _stft(_mono(beat))
    st = _measures(Bst, _stft(_mono(voc)), freq)
    rep["measured"] = {k: round(float(v), 2) for k, v in st.items()}

    parts, moves, _pre = [], {}, {}
    for r in list(ro_map.keys()):
        # --- SMART FX DETECTOR ---
        try:
            if ro_map[r].shape[-1] > sr * 12:
                _det = vocal_fx_detect.analyze(ro_map[r], sr, bpm=bpm)
                _plan = vocal_fx_detect.engine_plan(_det)
                _fx_plans[r] = _plan
                _fx_dets[r] = _det
        except Exception as _fx_e:
            rep.setdefault("fx_detect_error", {})[r] = str(_fx_e)[:80]
        # --------------------------
        # ── V2 NOISE CLEANER ──
        try:
            import noise_clean
            ro_map[r], _nc_rep = noise_clean.clean(ro_map[r], sr, role=r)
            rep.setdefault('noise_clean', {})[r] = _nc_rep
        except Exception as _e:
            rep.setdefault('noise_clean', {})[r] = 'failed: ' + str(_e)[:80]
        # ──────────────────────
        y, mv = _role_chain(ro_map[r], sr, st, r)
        if r == "lead":
            pass  # dry lead stays mono/centered. Width lives on the wet return.
        if ROLE_TREAT[r]["width"] != 1.0:
            y = _widen(y, sr, ROLE_TREAT[r]["width"])
        _pre[r] = y
        parts.append(y); moves[r] = mv
    core = _sum(parts)   # dry sum: polish, raise and _dry_voc all read this
    if polish is not None:
        try:
            _sh = core.shape
            _y, _pst = polish.dynamic_eq(core, sr, get_stats=True)
            if _y.shape != _sh or not np.isfinite(_y).all(): raise ValueError("bad audio")
            core = _y
            rep["dynamic_eq"] = [{"band": "%g-%g" % (float(b[0]), float(b[1])),
                "avg_db": round(float(b[4]), 2), "max_db": round(float(b[5]), 2),
                "active_pct": round(float(b[6]), 1)} for b in _pst]
        except Exception as _e:
            rep["dynamic_eq_error"] = str(_e)[:200]
    rep["vocal_chain"] = moves
    rep["clarity_before"] = round(float(st["clarity"]), 2)

    raised = 0.0
    level_passes = 0
    while (level_passes < 3 and st["clarity"] < (CLARITY_MIN - 0.3)
           and raised < RAISE_CAP):
        add = min(RAISE_PER_PASS, (CLARITY_MIN - st["clarity"]) * 0.7, RAISE_CAP - raised)
        if add <= 0.05:
            break
        raised += add
        core = (core * (10.0 ** (add / 20.0))).astype(np.float32)
        st = _measures(Bst, _stft(_mono(core)), freq)
        level_passes += 1
    del Bst
    rep["vocal_raise_db"] = round(raised, 2)
    rep["level_passes"] = level_passes

    _dry_voc = core.copy()
    core, _info = _role_buses(_pre, sr, bpm, raised, beat=beat, fx_plans=_fx_plans)

    # --- LIVE SONG SECTION DETECTION ---
    try:
        import song_sections
        _sec_res = song_sections.analyze(beat, core, sr)
        rep["song_sections"] = _sec_res
        if _sec_res.get("strength", 0.0) > 0:
            _curves = song_sections.control_curves(_sec_res, core.shape[1], sr)
            core = song_sections.apply_gain_db(core, sr, _curves["vocal_db"])
            rep["section_curves_applied"] = True
    except Exception as _se:
        rep["song_sections_error"] = str(_se)[:150]
    # -----------------------------------


    # Parallel compression on the summed vocal bus (two-track path)
    if _NEW.get("parallel_ny", False):
        try:
            core = _parallel(core, sr)
        except Exception as _pe:
            _PAR_LAST["error"] = str(_pe)[:200]
    try:
        import beat_punch
        beat, _kp_rep = beat_punch.process(beat, sr, preset=_PRESET.get("_name", "afrobeats"))
        rep["kick_punch"] = _kp_rep
    except Exception as _e:
        rep["kick_punch_error"] = str(_e)[:120]
    ms, pk = _info if _info else (0, "none")
    rep["bus"] = {r: ROLE_BUS.get(r, {}) for r in _pre}
    rep["slap_ms"] = ms
    rep["plate"] = pk
    rep["plate_error"] = plate_error()
    try:
        rep["reverb_last"] = dict(_REVERB_LAST)
    except Exception:
        rep["reverb_last"] = {}
    try:
        rep["delay_last"] = dict(_DELAY_LAST)
    except Exception:
        rep["delay_last"] = {}
    try:
        rep["parallel_last"] = dict(_PAR_LAST)
        rep["exciter_last"] = dict(_EXC_LAST)
    except Exception:
        pass

    _DOUBLE_ON = True    # ADT detune copy — widens the vocal (refs sit corr 0.85-0.92)
    if _fx_plans.get("lead", {}).get("skip_adt") or _fx_plans.get("feature", {}).get("skip_adt"):
        _DOUBLE_ON = False
        rep["fx_detect_adt"] = "skipped (vocal already wide/doubled)"
    side_ids = [r for r in ("lead", "adlib") if r in ro_map] if _DOUBLE_ON else []
    if side_ids:
        dbl = _double(_sum([ro_map[r] for r in side_ids]), sr)
        if dbl is not None:
            n = max(core.shape[1], dbl.shape[1])
            core = (_pad(core, n) + _pad(dbl, n)).astype(np.float32)
            rep["double"] = True
            rep["double_kind"] = _DBL.get("kind", "")
        else:
            rep["double"] = False
            rep["double_error"] = _DBL.get("error", "")
    else:
        rep["double"] = False
        rep["double_error"] = "side_ids empty"

    # ── CLAUDE'S VOCAL POLISH (rider + gap cleaner + 4-band adaptive carve) ──
    try:
        import vocal_polish
        core, beat, rep["vocal_polish"] = vocal_polish.polish(
            core, beat, sr, genre=_PRESET.get("_name", "afrobeats")
        )
        ducked = beat  # The beat is now already carved
    except Exception as _pol_err:
        rep["vocal_polish_error"] = str(_pol_err)[:120]
        ducked = _band_carve(beat, _dry_voc, sr)  # Fallback to old carve
    # ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    plan, vpres, post = {}, None, None
    tries = 1

    rep["duck_db"] = {("%d-%d" % b): round(v, 2) for b, v in plan.items()}
    rep["clarity_after_raise"] = round(float(st["clarity"]), 2)
    rep["clarity_after"] = None if post is None else round(float(
        _avg(_stft(_mono(core)), PRES, freq) - _avg(_stft(_mono(ducked)), PRES, freq)), 2)
    rep["duck_passes"] = tries

    n = max(ducked.shape[1], core.shape[1])
    ducked = _pad(ducked, n)
    core = _pad(core, n)
    try:
        import vocal_fix
        core, _vg = vocal_fix.forward_guard(core, ducked, sr)
        rep["vocal_forward_guard"] = _vg
    except Exception as _e:
        rep["vocal_forward_guard_error"] = str(_e)[:120]
    mixed = (ducked + core).astype(np.float32)
    if others:
        ex = _sum(others)
        n2 = max(mixed.shape[1], ex.shape[1])
        mixed = (_pad(mixed, n2) + _pad(ex, n2) * (10.0 ** (-4.0 / 20.0))).astype(np.float32)

    _w = float(_PRESET.get("width") or 1.0)
    try:
        if mixed.shape[0] >= 2:
            _m = (mixed[0] + mixed[1]) * 0.5
            _s = (mixed[0] - mixed[1]) * 0.5
            _rm = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(_m**2)))
            _rs = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(_s**2)))
            _sm = 20.0 * np.log10(max(1e-9, _rs) / max(1e-9, _rm))
            rep["widen_probe"] = {"width_wanted": _w, "sm_db": round(_sm, 2),
                                  "gate_would_fire": bool(_sm < -14.0)}
    except Exception as _we:
        rep["widen_probe_error"] = str(_we)[:120]
    mixed = _widen(mixed, sr, _w)   # AUDIO PATH UNCHANGED
    mixed = _headroom(mixed)
    rep["check"] = verify(mixed, sr, core, ducked)
    if "_vocal_space_used" in _PRESET:
        rep["vocal_space_used"] = True
    if "vocal_space_rep" in _PRESET:
        rep["vocal_space_rep"] = _PRESET["vocal_space_rep"]
    rep["fx_detect"] = _fx_dets
    rep["fx_plan"] = _fx_plans
    return mixed, rep


def _glue(x, sr, thr=-10.0, ratio=1.5, atk=30.0, rel=250.0):
    """Vocal-bus glue: slow, gentle, 1-2 dB of gain reduction.
    Softens peaks and seats lead + backups together - the 'polished record' step."""
    for kw in ({"threshold_db": thr, "ratio": ratio, "attack_ms": atk, "release_ms": rel},
               {"threshold_db": thr, "ratio": ratio}):
        try:
            return np.asarray(Pedalboard([Compressor(**kw)])(x, sr), dtype=np.float32)
        except Exception:
            continue
    return x


# ---------- full-mix master bus ----------

WIDEN_MASTER = 1.0


def _master(x, sr, rep, loud="MEDIUM"):
    """Master a finished stereo mix. No vocal EQ, no role chain, no low-cut
    below 20 Hz. Rumble guard -> tone polish -> modest width. mix.py adds the
    glue, the LUFS normalise and the true-peak limiter on top."""
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float32)
    if a.ndim == 1:
        a = a[None, :]
    moves = []
    try:
        a = Pedalboard([HighpassFilter(cutoff_frequency_hz=20.0)])(a, sr).astype(np.float32)
        moves.append(["hpf", 20.0])
    except Exception:
        pass
    # polish removed from master mode: a finished mix was already EQ'd
    # by its producer/mastering engineer. Re-applying polish cut -2.97 dB
    # at 2.5-4.5 kHz and -2.5 dB at 5.5-9 kHz on every upload, which made
    # finished beats sound lean and light. Master mode is now transparent:
    # HPF -> width -> loudness -> true-peak limiter.
    try:
        a = _widen(a, sr, WIDEN_MASTER)
        moves.append(["width", WIDEN_MASTER])
    except Exception:
        pass
    rep["master_chain"] = moves
    return _headroom(a, -1.0), rep


# ---------- polish additions ----------

def _parallel(x, sr):
    if _NEW["parallel_ny"]:
        try:
            import parallel_comp
            out = parallel_comp.industry_parallel_compressor(x, sr, blend_amount=0.30)
            _PAR_LAST["ran"] = True
            _PAR_LAST["mode"] = "ny 6:1 8ms hpf120 sub30"
            return out
        except Exception as _e:
            _PAR_LAST["error"] = str(_e)[:200]
            return x
    try:
        a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float32)
        if a.ndim != 2 or a.shape[0] not in (1, 2) or a.shape[1] == 0:
            return x
        crushed = Pedalboard([
            Compressor(threshold_db=-24.0, ratio=6.0, attack_ms=8.0, release_ms=90.0),
            Gain(gain_db=3.0),
        ])(a, int(sr)).astype(np.float32)
        y = (a * 0.70 + crushed * 0.30).astype(np.float32)
        _PAR_LAST["ran"] = True
        return _headroom(y, -1.0).astype(np.float32)
    except Exception as _e:
        _PAR_LAST["error"] = str(_e)[:200]
        return x


def _exciter(x, sr):
    if _NEW["exciter_t1"]:
        try:
            import dsp_tier1
            a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float32)
            if a.ndim != 2 or a.shape[1] == 0:
                return x
            y = dsp_tier1.exciter(a, int(sr), drive_db=8.0, blend=0.08)
            _EXC_LAST["ran"] = True
            return _headroom(y, -1.0).astype(np.float32)
        except Exception as _e:
            _EXC_LAST["error"] = str(_e)[:200]
            return x
    try:
        from pedalboard import Pedalboard as _PB, HighpassFilter as _HP, LowpassFilter as _LP, Distortion as _D, Gain as _G
        a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float32)
        if a.ndim != 2 or a.shape[0] not in (1, 2) or a.shape[1] == 0:
            return x
        air = _PB([_HP(cutoff_frequency_hz=5000.0),
                   _D(drive_db=0.8),
                   _LP(cutoff_frequency_hz=10000.0),
                   _G(gain_db=-9.0)])(a, int(sr)).astype(np.float32)
        y = (a + air * 0.08).astype(np.float32)
        _EXC_LAST["ran"] = True
        return _headroom(y, -1.0).astype(np.float32)
    except Exception as _e:
        _EXC_LAST["error"] = str(_e)[:200]
        return x


def _match_role_levels(ro_map, sr, rep):
    """Lift quiet vocal roles toward the lead so they survive the mix."""
    try:
        if ro_map.get("lead") is None:
            rep["level_match"] = "no lead - skipped"
            return
        lead = float(_rms_db(ro_map["lead"]))
        target = {"adlib": lead - 8.0, "backing": lead - 4.0}
        rep["level_match"] = {"lead_rms": round(lead, 1)}
        for r, tgt in target.items():
            v = ro_map.get(r)
            if v is None or getattr(v, "shape", (0, 0))[1] == 0:
                continue
            cur = float(_rms_db(v))
            if cur < -60.0:
                rep["level_match"][r] = "SILENT (%.1f dB) - bad file" % cur
                continue
            if (lead - cur) > 18.0:
                rep["level_match"][r] = "TOO QUIET (%.1f dB under lead) - not boosted" % (lead - cur)
                pass
            pk = float(np.max(np.abs(v))) or 1e-9
            head = 20.0 * np.log10(0.9 / pk)
            gain = float(np.clip(min(tgt - cur, max(head, 8.0)), -12.0, 20.0))
            if abs(gain) >= 0.1:
                ro_map[r] = (v * (10.0 ** (gain / 20.0))).astype(np.float32)
            rep["level_match"][r] = {"from": round(cur, 1), "gain": round(gain, 2)}
    except Exception as e:
        rep["level_match_error"] = str(e)[:300]


def soft_clip(x, sr, knee=0.70, oversample=4):
    """Transparent soft clipper. Linear below `knee`, smooth tanh bend above.
    4x FFT oversampling keeps the new harmonics from aliasing back down.
    Quiet material passes through untouched."""
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float32)
    if a.ndim == 1:
        a = a[None, :]
    n = a.shape[1]
    k = int(oversample)
    if k < 2 or n < 64:
        return x
    up = k * n
    y = np.empty((a.shape[0], up), dtype=np.float64)
    for c in range(a.shape[0]):
        F = np.fft.rfft(a[c].astype(np.float64))
        F2 = np.zeros(up // 2 + 1, dtype=np.complex128)
        F2[: F.shape[0]] = F * k
        y[c] = np.fft.irfft(F2, up)
    t = float(knee)
    m = np.abs(y)
    over = m > t
    if np.any(over):
        y[over] = np.sign(y[over]) * (t + (1.0 - t) * np.tanh((m[over] - t) / (1.0 - t)))
    out = np.empty((a.shape[0], n), dtype=np.float64)
    for c in range(a.shape[0]):
        F = np.fft.rfft(y[c])
        out[c] = np.fft.irfft(F[: n // 2 + 1] / k, n)
    return out.astype(np.float32)

# --- WRATTY DELAY HOOK v1 BEGIN (python3 install_delay.py --remove restores the original delay) ---
try:
    try:
        import delay_fx as _dfx
    except ImportError:
        from . import delay_fx as _dfx
    import os as _os_dfx
    _pingpong_legacy = _pingpong

    def _pingpong(voc, sr, bpm, note=0.75):
        old = _pingpong_legacy(voc, sr, bpm, note=note)
        # Claude's delay only for afrobeats — every other genre uses the original engine delay
        if _PRESET.get("_name", "neutral") != "afrobeats":
            return old
        if _os_dfx.environ.get("WRATTY_DELAY", "1") == "0":
            return old
        try:
            ref = np.asarray(old)
            if ref.ndim == 2 and ref.shape[0] <= 8:
                lay, n_ref = "cf", ref.shape[1]
            elif ref.ndim == 2:
                lay, n_ref = "cl", ref.shape[0]
            else:
                lay, n_ref = "m", ref.shape[0]
            r_old = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(ref, dtype=np.float64))))
            if r_old <= 0.0:
                return old
            # level-match DISABLED — play at preset level, not old-delay level
            wet, _info = _dfx.render(voc, sr, bpm, _PRESET.get("_name", "neutral"),
                                     note=note, target_rms=r_old)
            n = wet.shape[1]
            if n < n_ref:
                wet = np.pad(wet, ((0, 0), (0, n_ref - n)))
            elif n > n_ref:
                wet = wet[:, :n_ref]
            if lay == "cl":
                wet = wet.T
            elif lay == "m":
                wet = wet.mean(axis=0)
            return np.ascontiguousarray(wet, dtype=np.float32)
        except Exception as _e:
            print("[delay_fx] fallback to legacy delay:", _e)
            return old
except Exception as _e:
    print("[delay_fx] not installed, using legacy delay:", _e)
# --- WRATTY DELAY HOOK v1 END ---

_REVERB_LAST = {}
_DELAY_LAST = {}
_PAR_LAST = {}
_EXC_LAST = {}
# --- WRATTY REVERB HOOK v1 BEGIN (python3 install_reverb.py --remove restores the original) ---
try:
    try:
        import reverb_fx as _rvx
    except ImportError:
        from . import reverb_fx as _rvx
    import os as _os_rvx
    _plate_legacy = _plate
    _hall_legacy = _hall
    _REV_NOTE_CACHE = {"bpm": None}

    def _reverb_swap(voc, sr, kind, legacy_fn):
        old = legacy_fn(voc, sr)
        if old is None:
            return None
        # Claude's reverb only for afrobeats — other genres use the original engine reverb
        if _PRESET.get("_name", "neutral") != "afrobeats":
            return old
        if _os_rvx.environ.get("WRATTY_REVERB", "1") == "0":
            return old
        try:
            ref = np.asarray(old)
            n_ref = ref.shape[-1] if ref.ndim >= 2 else ref.shape[0]
            r_old = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(ref, dtype=np.float64))))
            if r_old <= 0.0:
                return old
            # song bpm is stored by the delay hook; fall back to 100
            bpm = _PRESET.get("_bpm", 100.0)
            wet, _info = _rvx.render(voc, sr, bpm, _PRESET.get("_name", "neutral"),
                                     target_rms=r_old, out_len=n_ref)
            try:
                _REVERB_LAST[kind] = dict(_info) if _info else {}
            except Exception:
                pass
            if wet.shape[-1] < n_ref:
                wet = np.pad(wet, ((0, 0), (0, n_ref - wet.shape[-1])))
            elif wet.shape[-1] > n_ref:
                wet = wet[:, :n_ref]
            return np.ascontiguousarray(wet, dtype=np.float32)
        except Exception as _e:
            print("[reverb_fx] fallback to legacy %s: %s" % (kind, _e))
            return old

    def _plate(voc, sr):
        return _reverb_swap(voc, sr, "plate", _plate_legacy)

    def _hall(voc, sr):
        return _reverb_swap(voc, sr, "hall", _hall_legacy)
except Exception as _e:
    print("[reverb_fx] not installed, using legacy reverb:", _e)

==============================================================================
FILE: fx-api/api/beat_punch.py   (173 lines)
==============================================================================

"""beat_punch.py -- adaptive KICK PUNCH for the beat bus (kick only: no saturator, no tone change).

The beat's crest factor and kick are measured BEFORE any punch is applied. The measurement decides how much punch
(0-3 dB, often 0) is added, and guards make sure the kick is never made worse: beat power is matched, peaks are
held at the original peak + 0.3 dB, and nothing is added if there are no real kicks.

  process(beat, sr, preset="afrobeats") -> (beat_out, report)
  measure_beat(beat, sr) / decide(meas, preset) / kick_punch(...) can be used on their own.
Pure numpy/scipy. beat: (2, n) or (n,) float32.
"""
import numpy as np
import scipy.signal as sg

PRESETS = {
    "afrobeats": dict(max_punch_db=3.0, click_ratio=0.35),
    "club":      dict(max_punch_db=4.0, click_ratio=0.40),
    "streaming": dict(max_punch_db=2.0, click_ratio=0.30),
}
LIMITS = dict(rms_rise_db=0.3, peak_rise_db=0.3, min_kicks=8)


def _mono(x):
    a = np.asarray(x, np.float32)
    return a.mean(axis=0) if a.ndim == 2 else a


def _sos(kind, f, sr, order=4):
    return sg.butter(order, f, kind, fs=sr, output="sos")


def _zp(x, sos):
    return sg.sosfiltfilt(sos, x).astype(np.float32)


def _db(v):
    return 20.0 * np.log10(np.asarray(v, np.float64) + 1e-12)


def _smooth(x, sr, tau):
    a = float(np.exp(-1.0 / (tau * sr)))
    return sg.lfilter([1.0 - a], [1.0, -a], x).astype(np.float32)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------- 1. measure (BEFORE any punch)
def measure_beat(beat, sr):
    a = np.asarray(beat, np.float32)
    m = _mono(a)
    peak_db = float(_db(np.abs(a).max()))
    rms_db = float(10 * np.log10((m.astype(np.float64) ** 2).mean() + 1e-20))
    out = {"peak_db": round(peak_db, 2), "rms_db": round(rms_db, 2), "crest_db": round(peak_db - rms_db, 2)}
    w = int(0.4 * sr); k = len(m) // w
    if k > 4:
        fr = m[:k * w].reshape(k, w); r = np.sqrt((fr ** 2).mean(1) + 1e-14); p = np.abs(fr).max(1)
        loud = _db(r) > np.percentile(_db(r), 50)
        out["crest_short_db"] = round(float(np.median((_db(p) - _db(r))[loud])), 2)
    kb = _zp(m, _sos("bandpass", [40, 120], sr, 3))
    low_share = float(10 * np.log10((kb.astype(np.float64) ** 2).mean() / ((m.astype(np.float64) ** 2).mean() + 1e-20) + 1e-20))
    out["low_share_db"] = round(low_share, 1)
    ab = np.abs(kb)
    fast, slow = _smooth(ab, sr, 0.004), _smooth(ab, sr, 0.08)
    cond = (fast > 2.0 * slow) & (fast > 0.6 * np.percentile(fast, 85))
    edges = np.flatnonzero(cond[1:] & ~cond[:-1]) + 1
    if low_share < -20.0:                       # almost no 40-120 Hz energy: nothing to punch (also rejects noise)
        edges = edges[:0]
    ev, last = [], -10 ** 9
    for i in edges:
        if i - last >= int(0.15 * sr) and i > int(0.01 * sr) and i + int(0.2 * sr) < len(kb):
            ev.append(int(i)); last = int(i)
    kc, kp = [], []
    for i in ev:
        s = kb[i - int(0.005 * sr): i + int(0.15 * sr)]
        pk = np.abs(s).max(); rm = np.sqrt((s.astype(np.float64) ** 2).mean() + 1e-20)
        kc.append(float(_db(pk) - _db(rm))); kp.append(float(_db(pk)))
    mid = _zp(m, _sos("bandpass", [300, 3000], sr, 3))
    h = int(0.1 * sr); kk = len(mid) // h
    mid_pk = float(np.percentile(np.abs(mid[:kk * h].reshape(kk, h)).max(1), 95)) if kk > 4 else 1e-6
    out.update({"kick_count": len(ev), "events": ev})
    if ev:
        out["kick_crest_db"] = round(float(np.median(kc)), 2)
        out["kick_to_mid_db"] = round(float(np.median(kp) - _db(mid_pk)), 2)
    return out


# ----------------------------------------------------------------- 2. decide
def decide(meas, preset="afrobeats", strength=1.0):
    P = PRESETS[preset] if isinstance(preset, str) else preset
    n = meas.get("kick_count", 0)
    if n < LIMITS["min_kicks"] or "kick_crest_db" not in meas:
        return {"punch_db": 0.0, "click_db": 0.0, "need": 0.0, "reason": "fewer than %d kicks found, no punch added" % LIMITS["min_kicks"]}
    # reference masters (Boi Chase, Burna, Davido, Fola, Rema): kick crest 8.4-10.5 dB (median 9.7), kick-to-mid -0.3..+5.0 dB (median +1.0)
    need_c = float(np.clip((16.5 - meas["crest_db"]) / 6.5, 0, 1))          # squashed beat (low crest) -> needs more
    need_k = float(np.clip((9.7 - meas["kick_crest_db"]) / 3.0, 0, 1))       # kick flatter than the references -> needs more
    need_f = float(np.clip((1.0 - meas["kick_to_mid_db"]) / 4.0, 0, 1))      # kick buried under the mids -> needs more
    need = float(np.clip((0.5 * need_k + 0.3 * need_c + 0.2 * need_f) * strength, 0, 1))
    punch = round(need * P["max_punch_db"], 2)
    return {"punch_db": punch, "click_db": round(punch * P["click_ratio"], 2), "need": round(need, 2),
            "need_parts": {"crest": round(need_c, 2), "kick_crest": round(need_k, 2), "kick_level": round(need_f, 2)},
            "reason": "adaptive from crest factor and kick crest"}


# ----------------------------------------------------------------- 3. kick punch
def _event_window(n, sr, events, pre_s, post_s, ramp_s):
    w = np.zeros(n, np.float32)
    for i in events:
        s0, e0 = max(0, i - int(pre_s * sr)), min(n, i + int(post_s * sr))
        seg = np.ones(e0 - s0, np.float32); r = min(len(seg) // 2, int(ramp_s * sr))
        seg[:r] = np.linspace(0, 1, r); seg[-r:] = np.linspace(1, 0, r)
        w[s0:e0] = np.maximum(w[s0:e0], seg)
    return w


def kick_punch(beat, sr, events, punch_db, click_db, cross_hz=120.0):
    """Transient shaping on the kick only: the 40-120 Hz body gets a fast-attack lift inside each kick window, plus a
    very small 1.5-4.5 kHz click. Gains are smoothed (2 ms), so no clicks. The rest of the beat is untouched."""
    a = np.asarray(beat, np.float32); n = a.shape[-1]
    if punch_db <= 0.01 or not events:
        return a
    m = _mono(a)
    low_m = _zp(m, _sos("bandpass", [40, cross_hz], sr, 3))
    ab = np.abs(low_m)
    fast, slow = _smooth(ab, sr, 0.0015), _smooth(ab, sr, 0.025)
    r = fast / (slow + 1e-9)
    G = 10 ** (punch_db / 20.0)
    g = 1.0 + (G - 1.0) * np.clip((r - 1.0) / 1.0, 0.0, 1.0)
    win = _event_window(n, sr, events, 0.010, 0.080, 0.008)
    g = _smooth(1.0 + (g - 1.0) * win, sr, 0.002)
    body_add = low_m * (g - 1.0)
    out = a + body_add[None, :] if a.ndim == 2 else a + body_add
    if click_db > 0.01:
        click = _zp(m, _sos("bandpass", [1500, 4500], sr, 2))
        cwin = _event_window(n, sr, events, 0.002, 0.025, 0.005)
        add = click * (10 ** (click_db / 20.0) - 1.0) * cwin
        out = out + add[None, :] if a.ndim == 2 else out + add
    return out.astype(np.float32)


def _peak_guard(x, ceiling, sr, look_ms=3.0):
    """Lookahead peak guard: gain drops smoothly only around samples above `ceiling`; everything else untouched."""
    from scipy.ndimage import maximum_filter1d, uniform_filter1d
    a = np.asarray(x, np.float32)
    pk = np.abs(a).max(axis=0) if a.ndim == 2 else np.abs(a)
    L = max(3, int(look_ms / 1000.0 * sr))
    env = maximum_filter1d(pk, size=2 * L + 1)
    g = np.minimum(1.0, ceiling / (env + 1e-12)).astype(np.float32)
    g = uniform_filter1d(g, size=2 * L + 1).astype(np.float32)
    g = np.minimum(g, np.minimum(1.0, ceiling / (pk + 1e-12)))
    return (a * g[None, :] if a.ndim == 2 else a * g).astype(np.float32)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------- 4. process (kick only, with guards)
def process(beat, sr, preset="afrobeats", strength=1.0):
    P = PRESETS[preset] if isinstance(preset, str) else preset
    a = np.asarray(beat, np.float32)
    before = measure_beat(a, sr)                                   # crest factor measured BEFORE punch
    dec = decide(before, P, strength)
    out = a
    if dec["punch_db"] > 0:
        out = kick_punch(out, sr, before["events"], dec["punch_db"], dec["click_db"])
        rms0 = 10 * np.log10((a.astype(np.float64) ** 2).mean() + 1e-20); pk0 = float(np.abs(a).max())
        rms1 = 10 * np.log10((out.astype(np.float64) ** 2).mean() + 1e-20)
        gain = float(np.clip(rms0 - rms1, -LIMITS["rms_rise_db"], LIMITS["rms_rise_db"]))
        out = (out * np.float32(10 ** (gain / 20.0))).astype(np.float32)
        out = _peak_guard(out, pk0 * 10 ** (LIMITS["peak_rise_db"] / 20.0), sr)
    after = measure_beat(out, sr)
    rep = {"preset": preset if isinstance(preset, str) else "custom",
           "before": {k: v for k, v in before.items() if k != "events"}, "decision": dec,
           "after": {k: v for k, v in after.items() if k != "events"},
           "delta": {"rms_db": round(after["rms_db"] - before["rms_db"], 2), "peak_db": round(after["peak_db"] - before["peak_db"], 2),
                     "crest_short_db": round(after.get("crest_short_db", 0) - before.get("crest_short_db", 0), 2)}}
    if "kick_crest_db" in before and "kick_crest_db" in after:
        rep["delta"]["kick_crest_db"] = round(after["kick_crest_db"] - before["kick_crest_db"], 2)
        rep["delta"]["kick_to_mid_db"] = round(after["kick_to_mid_db"] - before["kick_to_mid_db"], 2)
    return out, rep

FILE: fx-api/api/blobstore.py   (93 lines)

"""Byte storage for mix outputs. R2 primary, Modal volume fallback."""
import os
import pathlib

MOUNT = pathlib.Path(os.environ.get("MIX_MOUNT", "/mixes"))
BASE = os.environ.get("FILES_BASE_URL",
                      "https://wrattyg--wratty-files-web.modal.run").rstrip("/")
VOLUME = os.environ.get("MIX_VOLUME", "wratty-mixes")

R2_ACCOUNT = os.environ.get("CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID", "")
R2_KEY = os.environ.get("CLOUDFLARE_R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID", "")
R2_SECRET = os.environ.get("CLOUDFLARE_R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY", "")
R2_BUCKET = os.environ.get("R2_BUCKET_NAME", "")
R2_HOST = "r2.wratty.internal"

_r2_client = None


def _r2():
    global _r2_client
    if _r2_client is None:
        import boto3
        from botocore.config import Config
        _r2_client = boto3.client(
            "s3",
            endpoint_url="https://%s.r2.cloudflarestorage.com" % R2_ACCOUNT,
            aws_access_key_id=R2_KEY,
            aws_secret_access_key=R2_SECRET,
            region_name="auto",
            config=Config(
                signature_version="s3v4",
                s3={"addressing_style": "path"},
                retries={"max_attempts": 3, "mode": "standard"},
            ),
        )
    return _r2_client


def r2_enabled():
    return bool(R2_ACCOUNT and R2_KEY and R2_SECRET and R2_BUCKET)


def volume_enabled():
    return MOUNT.is_dir()


def _content_type(name):
    n = name.lower()
    if n.endswith(".mp3"): return "audio/mpeg"
    if n.endswith(".flac"): return "audio/flac"
    if n.endswith(".wav"): return "audio/wav"
    return "application/octet-stream"


def put(blob, name):
    if isinstance(blob, (bytes, bytearray)):
        data = bytes(blob)
    elif hasattr(blob, "read"):
        data = blob.read()
    else:
        data = pathlib.Path(blob).read_bytes()

    if r2_enabled():
        try:
            key = "mixes/%s" % name
            _r2().put_object(Bucket=R2_BUCKET, Key=key, Body=data,
                             ContentType=_content_type(name))
            return "https://%s/%s" % (R2_HOST, key)
        except Exception as _e:
            print("R2 put failed, falling back to volume: %s" % str(_e)[:200], flush=True)

    if not volume_enabled():
        raise RuntimeError("no R2 creds and volume not mounted at %s" % MOUNT)
    dst = MOUNT / name
    dst.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    tmp = dst.with_suffix(dst.suffix + ".part")
    tmp.write_bytes(data)
    os.replace(tmp, dst)
    _commit_volume()
    return "%s/f/%s" % (BASE, name)


def _commit_volume():
    try:
        import modal
        modal.Volume.from_name(VOLUME).commit()
    except Exception as e:
        print("blobstore commit failed: %s" % e, flush=True)


def commit():
    if not r2_enabled():
        _commit_volume()


==============================================================================
FILE: fx-api/api/clip_gain.py   (105 lines)
==============================================================================

"""Relative clip-gain rider — level-neutral vocal dynamics pre-stage.

Rides loud words down and quiet words up, targets the vocal's OWN average
(not a fixed dB), and stays level-neutral on average. This is what closes
the dynamic-range gap that compressors cannot touch (they only act above
threshold; quiet words pass through untouched).
"""
import numpy as np
from numba import njit


@njit(cache=True)
def _asym_solver(src, sr, active_mask, local_rms_db, avg_rms_db,
                 max_gain_db, attack_ms, release_ms):
    samples = len(src)
    out_sig = np.zeros(samples, dtype=np.float32)

    alpha_atk = 1.0 - np.exp(-1.0 / ((attack_ms / 1000.0) * sr))
    alpha_rel = 1.0 - np.exp(-1.0 / ((release_ms / 1000.0) * sr))

    current_gain = 1.0

    for s in range(samples):
        if active_mask[s]:
            ideal_db = avg_rms_db - local_rms_db[s]
            if ideal_db > max_gain_db:
                ideal_db = max_gain_db
            elif ideal_db < -max_gain_db:
                ideal_db = -max_gain_db
            target = 10.0 ** (ideal_db / 20.0)
        else:
            target = 1.0

        if target < current_gain:
            current_gain += alpha_atk * (target - current_gain)
        else:
            current_gain += alpha_rel * (target - current_gain)

        out_sig[s] = src[s] * current_gain

    return out_sig


def clip_gain_ride(x, sr, window_ms=100.0, max_gain_db=3.0,
                   silence_threshold_db=-32.0):
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float32)
    if a.ndim == 1:
        a = a[None, :]
    channels, samples = a.shape
    if samples < 256:
        return x

    sil_lin = 10.0 ** (silence_threshold_db / 20.0)
    win = int((window_ms / 1000.0) * sr)
    if win % 2:
        win += 1
    half = win // 2

    attack_ms  = 30.0
    release_ms = 250.0

    # Pass 1: local RMS per channel
    local_rms_db_list = []
    active_masks = []
    for ch in range(channels):
        src = a[ch]
        pad = np.pad(src, half, mode="reflect")
        sq  = pad.astype(np.float64) ** 2
        cs  = np.cumsum(sq)
        energy = cs[win:] - cs[:-win]
        rms = np.sqrt(np.maximum(energy / win, 1e-10))
        if len(rms) < samples:
            rms = np.pad(rms, (0, samples - len(rms)), mode="edge")
        elif len(rms) > samples:
            rms = rms[:samples]
        mask = rms > sil_lin
        rms_db = 20.0 * np.log10(rms)
        local_rms_db_list.append(rms_db)
        active_masks.append(mask)

    # Pass 2: one linked global average (linear-domain, no dB bias)
    pooled = np.concatenate([local_rms_db_list[ch][active_masks[ch]]
                             for ch in range(channels)])
    if pooled.size == 0:
        return a if a.ndim == 2 else a[0]
    pooled_lin = 10.0 ** (pooled / 20.0)
    global_avg_db = 20.0 * np.log10(np.mean(pooled_lin) + 1e-12)

    # Pass 3: solve each channel against the same global target
    out = np.zeros_like(a)
    for ch in range(channels):
        out[ch] = _asym_solver(
            a[ch], float(sr), active_masks[ch], local_rms_db_list[ch],
            float(global_avg_db), float(max_gain_db),
            float(attack_ms), float(release_ms),
        )

    # Peak safety
    pk = float(np.max(np.abs(out)))
    if pk > 0.95:
        out = (out * (0.95 / pk)).astype(np.float32)

    if x.ndim == 1:
        return out[0]
    return out


==============================================================================
FILE: fx-api/api/declip.py   (123 lines)
==============================================================================

from scipy.interpolate import CubicSpline
"""
declip.py - conservative de-clipper. numpy only.

Repairs flat-topped clipped regions by band-limited (Lanczos-sinc)
interpolation using surrounding samples as anchors.

Design rules:
  * CONSERVATIVE: skip runs longer than max_run (too damaged).
  * ADVISORY: reports what it did; caller decides to apply.
  * numpy only, no state, no globals.

CLI:
    python3 declip.py input.wav output.wav
"""
import sys
import numpy as np


def _lanczos_interp(xp, fp, xi, a=4):
    """Lanczos-a sinc interpolation. Vectorised over xi."""
    xp = np.asarray(xp, dtype=np.float64)
    fp = np.asarray(fp, dtype=np.float64)
    xi = np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(xi, dtype=np.float64))
    out = np.empty_like(xi)
    for i, x in enumerate(xi):
        d = x - xp
        m = np.abs(d) < a
        if not m.any():
            out[i] = 0.0
            continue
        dd = d[m]
        k = np.sinc(dd) * np.sinc(dd / a)
        s = k.sum()
        out[i] = (k * fp[m]).sum() / s if abs(s) > 1e-12 else 0.0
    return out


def detect_clips(x, threshold=0.998):
    """Return list of (start, end, sign) for each clipped run."""
    a = np.asarray(x)
    if a.ndim == 2:
        hit = (np.abs(a) >= threshold).any(axis=0)
        # per-sample sign of the loudest channel — proper fancy indexing
        rows = np.argmax(np.abs(a), axis=0)
        cols = np.arange(a.shape[1])
        sign = np.sign(a[rows, cols]).astype(np.int8)
    else:
        hit = np.abs(a) >= threshold
        sign = np.sign(a).astype(np.int8)
    d = np.diff(np.concatenate([[0], hit.astype(np.int8), [0]]))
    starts = np.where(d == 1)[0]
    ends = np.where(d == -1)[0]
    out = []
    for s, e in zip(starts, ends):
        sgn = int(sign[s]) if s < len(sign) else 1
        out.append((int(s), int(e), sgn))
    return out


def declip(x, sr, threshold=0.998, context=8, max_run=16):
    """
    Repair short clipped runs by Lanczos interpolation.
    x: (channels, samples) or (samples,) float array.
    Returns (repaired, report).
    """
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float32)
    single = a.ndim == 1
    if single:
        a = a[None, :]
    out = a.copy()
    clips = detect_clips(a, threshold=threshold)
    report = {
        "clip_count": len(clips),
        "repaired": 0,
        "skipped_long": 0,
        "skipped_edge": 0,
        "max_run_repaired": 0,
    }
    for s, e, sgn in clips:
        run = e - s
        if run > max_run:
            report["skipped_long"] += 1
            continue
        lo = s - context
        hi = e + context
        if lo < 0 or hi > a.shape[1]:
            report["skipped_edge"] += 1
            continue
        xp = np.concatenate([np.arange(lo, s), np.arange(e, hi)])
        for ch in range(a.shape[0]):
            fp = np.concatenate([a[ch, lo:s], a[ch, e:hi]])
            xi = np.arange(s, e)
            # Claude's Proper Fix: CubicSpline
            yi = CubicSpline(xp, fp)(xi)
            lvl = np.abs(a[ch, s:e])
            yi = sgn * np.maximum(sgn * yi, lvl)
            yi = np.clip(yi, -1.6, 1.6)
            out[ch, s:e] = yi.astype(np.float32).astype(np.float32)
        report["repaired"] += 1
        report["max_run_repaired"] = max(report["max_run_repaired"], run)
    if single:
        out = out[0]
    return out, report


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) < 3:
        print(__doc__)
        sys.exit(1)
    import subprocess, json
    src, dst = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
    raw = subprocess.run(["ffmpeg","-v","error","-i",src,"-f","f32le",
                          "-acodec","pcm_f32le","-ac","2","-ar","44100","-"],
                         stdout=subprocess.PIPE, check=True).stdout
    a = np.frombuffer(raw, dtype=np.float32).reshape(-1,2).T.astype(np.float32)
    out, rep = declip(a, 44100)
    # write back
    p = subprocess.Popen(["ffmpeg","-y","-v","error","-f","f32le",
                          "-ac","2","-ar","44100","-i","-","-c:a","pcm_s16le",dst],
                         stdin=subprocess.PIPE)
    p.communicate(out.T.astype(np.float32).tobytes())
    print(json.dumps(rep, indent=2))

FILE: fx-api/api/delay_fx.py   (150 lines)

"""
delay_fx.py - professional vocal delay for WrattyGstudio.  numpy only.
"""
import numpy as np

DELAY_PRESETS = {
    "afrobeats": dict(fb=0.30, taps=5, hp=280.0, lp_bright=5000.0, lp_dark=2500.0, duck_db=10.0, width=0.70, level_db=-16.0),
    "amapiano":  dict(fb=0.24, taps=4, hp=320.0, lp_bright=4500.0, lp_dark=2200.0, duck_db=10.0, width=0.60, level_db=-19.0),
    "highlife":  dict(fb=0.20, taps=4, hp=250.0, lp_bright=5500.0, lp_dark=3000.0, duck_db=8.0,  width=0.50, level_db=-18.0),
    "pop":       dict(fb=0.22, taps=4, hp=250.0, lp_bright=6000.0, lp_dark=3000.0, duck_db=8.0,  width=0.70, level_db=-18.0),
    "rnb":       dict(fb=0.34, taps=5, hp=250.0, lp_bright=4000.0, lp_dark=2000.0, duck_db=8.0,  width=0.80, level_db=-16.0),
    "rap":       dict(fb=0.15, taps=3, hp=320.0, lp_bright=5000.0, lp_dark=2500.0, duck_db=12.0, width=0.50, level_db=-21.0),
    "neutral":   dict(fb=0.20, taps=3, hp=250.0, lp_bright=5000.0, lp_dark=2500.0, duck_db=10.0, width=0.50, level_db=-20.0),
}
SUGGESTED_NOTE = {"afrobeats": 0.75, "amapiano": 1.0, "highlife": 0.5, "pop": 0.5, "rnb": 0.75, "rap": 0.5, "neutral": 1.0}
DELAY_MIN_MS = 180.0
DELAY_MAX_MS = 640.0
MAX_TAIL_MS = 3000.0
ACTIVE_GATE_DB = -45.0


def fold_bpm(bpm, lo=70.0, hi=140.0):
    try: b = float(bpm)
    except (TypeError, ValueError): return 100.0
    if not np.isfinite(b) or b <= 0: return 100.0
    while b < lo: b *= 2.0
    while b > hi: b /= 2.0
    return b


def delay_ms(bpm, note=0.75):
    beat = 60000.0 / fold_bpm(bpm)
    t = max(float(note), 0.0625) * beat
    while t > DELAY_MAX_MS: t /= 2.0
    while t < DELAY_MIN_MS: t *= 2.0
    return t


def _mono(voc):
    a = np.asarray(voc)
    if a.ndim == 1: return a.astype(np.float64)
    return a.mean(axis=0, dtype=np.float64) if a.shape[0] <= a.shape[1] else a.mean(axis=1, dtype=np.float64)


def _rms(a, chunk=1 << 20):
    flat = np.asarray(a).reshape(-1)
    acc = 0.0
    for i in range(0, flat.size, chunk):
        c = flat[i:i + chunk].astype(np.float64)
        acc += float(np.dot(c, c))
    return float(np.sqrt(acc / max(flat.size, 1)))


def _shape(x, sr, hp, lp, order=2, block=1 << 17, pad=4096):
    n = len(x)
    out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float32)
    nfft = 1 << int(np.ceil(np.log2(block + 2 * pad)))
    f = np.fft.rfftfreq(nfft, 1.0 / sr)
    f[0] = 1e-3
    H = np.ones_like(f)
    if hp: H = H / np.sqrt(1.0 + (hp / f) ** (2 * order))
    if lp: H = H / np.sqrt(1.0 + (f / lp) ** (2 * order))
    for s in range(0, n, block):
        e = min(n, s + block)
        a, b = max(0, s - pad), min(n, e + pad)
        y = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(x[a:b], nfft) * H, nfft)[:b - a]
        out[s:e] = y[s - a:e - a]
    return out


def _duck_gain(x, sr, depth_db, release_ms):
    n = len(x)
    blk = max(1, int(sr * 0.001))
    m = n // blk
    if m < 10 or depth_db <= 0: return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float32)
    pk = np.abs(x[:m * blk]).reshape(m, blk).max(axis=1)
    att = np.exp(-1.0 / 3.0)
    rel = np.exp(-1.0 / max(release_ms, 20.0))
    env = np.empty(m); e = 0.0
    for i in range(m):
        c = att if pk[i] > e else rel
        e = c * e + (1.0 - c) * pk[i]
        env[i] = e
    env_db = 20.0 * np.log10(env + 1e-9)
    live = env > 1e-4
    ref_db = float(np.percentile(env_db[live], 75)) if live.any() else -20.0
    amt = np.clip((env_db - (ref_db - 20.0)) / 14.0, 0.0, 1.0)
    g_db = -depth_db * amt
    g = 10.0 ** (g_db / 20.0)
    out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float32)
    xs = (np.arange(m) + 0.5) * blk
    for i in range(0, n, 1 << 20):
        j = min(n, i + (1 << 20))
        out[i:j] = np.interp(np.arange(i, j), xs, g)
    return out


def _pan_gains(pan):
    th = (pan + 1.0) * np.pi / 4.0
    return float(np.cos(th)), float(np.sin(th))


def render(voc, sr, bpm, preset="neutral", note=0.75, overrides=None, target_rms=None, start_side=1.0):
    p = dict(DELAY_PRESETS.get(str(preset).lower(), DELAY_PRESETS["neutral"]))
    if overrides: p.update(overrides)
    x = _mono(voc); n = len(x)
    info = {"preset": str(preset), "taps": 0, "time_ms": None, "bpm_used": None, "density": None, "fb": None, "duck_db": None, "tail_s": 0.0}
    wet = np.zeros((2, n), dtype=np.float32)
    if n < sr // 2 or float(np.max(np.abs(x))) < 1e-6: return wet, info
    w = int(0.05 * sr); k = n // w
    fr = 20.0 * np.log10(np.sqrt((x[:k * w].reshape(k, w) ** 2).mean(axis=1)) + 1e-12)
    act_frames = fr > ACTIVE_GATE_DB
    dens = float(act_frames.mean()) if k else 0.0
    fb = float(p["fb"]); lvl = float(p["level_db"]); duck = float(p["duck_db"])
    if dens > 0.75: fb *= 0.85; lvl -= 2.0; duck = min(14.0, duck + 2.0)
    elif dens < 0.45: fb = min(0.45, fb * 1.10); lvl += 1.5
    fb = float(np.clip(fb, 0.0, 0.45))
    T = delay_ms(bpm, note); d = int(round(T * sr / 1000.0))
    bright = _shape(x, sr, p["hp"], p["lp_bright"])
    dark = _shape(x, sr, p["hp"], p["lp_dark"])
    sample_mask = np.repeat(act_frames, w)
    sample_mask = np.concatenate([sample_mask, np.zeros(n - sample_mask.size, dtype=bool)])
    ref = bright[sample_mask]
    ref_rms = _rms(ref) if ref.size else 1e-6
    del ref
    base = ref_rms * 10.0 ** (lvl / 20.0)
    taps = int(min(p["taps"], max(1, MAX_TAIL_MS // T)))
    last = 0
    for i in range(1, taps + 1):
        off = i * d
        if off >= n: break
        a = 0.0 if taps == 1 else (i - 1) / float(taps - 1)
        g = base * (fb ** (i - 1)) / max(ref_rms, 1e-9)
        seg = (bright[:n - off] * (1.0 - a) + dark[:n - off] * a) * g
        side = start_side if i % 2 == 1 else -start_side
        pan = side * float(p["width"]) * (0.0 if i == 1 else (0.65 if i == 2 else 1.0))
        gl, gr = _pan_gains(pan)
        wet[0, off:] += seg * gl
        wet[1, off:] += seg * gr
        last = i
    release = float(np.clip(0.50 * T, 150.0, 400.0))
    gain = _duck_gain(x, sr, duck, release)
    wet *= gain[None, :]
    if target_rms is not None and target_rms > 0:
        cur = _rms(wet)
        if cur > 1e-12: wet *= np.float32(target_rms / cur)
    peak = float(np.abs(wet).max())
    if peak > 0.5: wet *= np.float32(0.5 / peak)
    info.update({"taps": last, "time_ms": round(T, 1), "bpm_used": round(fold_bpm(bpm), 1), "density": round(dens, 3), "fb": round(fb, 3), "duck_db": round(duck, 1), "tail_s": round(last * T / 1000.0, 2), "release_ms": round(release)})
    return wet, info


==============================================================================
FILE: fx-api/api/delay_spec.py   (181 lines)
==============================================================================

"""Spec-compliant stereo ping-pong delay for the vocal bus.

Follows afrobeats_default_preset_v1.json -> "delay" section.

  - auto-pick note value so delay_ms lands in [250, 500]
  - stereo ping-pong, feedback 0.25 (max 0.35)
  - HPF 400 / LPF 5000 on the return
  - light tape saturation
  - stereo width 0.8
  - 0.4 Hz / 2 ms chorus modulation
  - duck 6 dB / 5 ms attack / 150 ms release, keyed from source
  - send level -18 dB
"""
import numpy as np

try:
    from pedalboard import (
        Pedalboard, HighpassFilter, LowpassFilter,
        Chorus, Distortion, Gain,
    )
except Exception:
    Pedalboard = None


PRESETS = {
    "afrobeats": dict(
        feedback=0.25, feedback_max=0.35,
        send_db=-14.0,
        hpf_hz=500.0, lpf_hz=5000.0,
        saturation_drive_db=1.0,
        width=0.80,
        modulation_rate_hz=0.40, modulation_depth_ms=0.8,
        duck_db=6.0, duck_atk_ms=5.0, duck_rel_ms=150.0,
        note_factor_pref=(0.75, 0.5, 0.25, 1.0),
    ),
    "neutral": dict(
        feedback=0.25, feedback_max=0.35,
        send_db=-20.0,
        hpf_hz=400.0, lpf_hz=5000.0,
        saturation_drive_db=0.0,
        width=0.80,
        modulation_rate_hz=0.40, modulation_depth_ms=2.0,
        duck_db=6.0, duck_atk_ms=5.0, duck_rel_ms=150.0,
        note_factor_pref=(0.75, 0.5, 0.25, 1.0),
    ),
}


def _pick_note_factor(bpm, pref):
    try:
        bpm = float(bpm)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        bpm = 100.0
    if not (bpm > 0) or bpm != bpm:
        bpm = 100.0
    beat_ms = 60000.0 / bpm
    for f in pref:
        d_ms = beat_ms * f
        if 100.0 <= d_ms <= 600.0:
            return float(f), float(d_ms)
    best = min(pref, key=lambda f: abs(beat_ms * f - 350.0))
    return float(best), float(beat_ms * best)


def _envelope(x, sr, atk_ms, rel_ms):
    atk = max(1, int(sr * atk_ms / 1000.0))
    rel = max(1, int(sr * rel_ms / 1000.0))
    ax = np.abs(x).astype(np.float32)
    ka = np.ones(atk, dtype=np.float32) / float(atk)
    kr = np.ones(rel, dtype=np.float32) / float(rel)
    e = np.convolve(ax, ka, mode="same")
    e = np.convolve(e, kr, mode="same")
    return e.astype(np.float32)


def render(voc, sr, bpm, preset="afrobeats"):
    if Pedalboard is None:
        return None, {"error": "pedalboard unavailable"}
    p = PRESETS.get(preset, PRESETS["neutral"])
    nf, delay_ms = _pick_note_factor(bpm, p["note_factor_pref"])
    delay_s = delay_ms / 1000.0

    a = np.asarray(voc, dtype=np.float32)
    if a.ndim == 1:
        a = np.stack([a, a])
    if a.shape[0] < 2:
        a = np.stack([a[0], a[0]])
    a = a[:2]
    n = a.shape[1]
    src = ((a[0] + a[1]) * 0.5).astype(np.float32)

    d = int(round(sr * delay_s))
    if d < 1:
        return None, {"error": "delay too short"}

    fb = min(float(p["feedback"]), float(p["feedback_max"]))
    pad = d * 12
    sp = np.zeros(n + pad, dtype=np.float32)
    sp[:n] = src
    out = np.zeros((2, n + pad), dtype=np.float32)
    g = 1.0
    off = d
    if off < n + pad:
        out[0, off:] += sp[:n + pad - off] * g * 0.5
        out[1, off:] += sp[:n + pad - off] * g * 0.5
        g *= fb
    side = 1
    for tap in range(2, 12):
        off = d * tap
        if off >= n + pad:
            break
        out[side, off:] += sp[:n + pad - off] * g
        side = 1 - side
        g *= fb

    out = Pedalboard([
        HighpassFilter(cutoff_frequency_hz=float(p["hpf_hz"]) / 1.96),
        HighpassFilter(cutoff_frequency_hz=float(p["hpf_hz"]) / 1.96),
        HighpassFilter(cutoff_frequency_hz=float(p["hpf_hz"]) / 1.96),
        LowpassFilter(cutoff_frequency_hz=float(p["lpf_hz"]) / 0.644),
        LowpassFilter(cutoff_frequency_hz=float(p["lpf_hz"]) / 0.644),
    ])(out, sr).astype(np.float32)

    try:
        depth_n = float(p["modulation_depth_ms"]) / 25.0
        out = Pedalboard([
            Chorus(rate_hz=float(p["modulation_rate_hz"]),
                   depth=depth_n, mix=0.15),
        ])(out, sr).astype(np.float32)
    except Exception:
        pass

    drive = float(p["saturation_drive_db"])
    if drive > 0.01:
        try:
            out = Pedalboard([
                Distortion(drive_db=drive),
                Gain(gain_db=-drive),
            ])(out, sr).astype(np.float32)
        except Exception:
            pass

    mid = (out[0] + out[1]) * 0.5
    sd = (out[0] - out[1]) * 0.5 * float(p["width"])
    out = np.stack([mid + sd, mid - sd]).astype(np.float32)

    try:
        env = _envelope(src, sr, p["duck_atk_ms"], p["duck_rel_ms"])
        ep = float(env.max())
        if ep > 0:
            env = env / ep
        duck_lin = 10.0 ** (-(float(p["duck_db"]) * env) / 20.0)
        out = out * duck_lin[None, :]
    except Exception:
        pass

    send_lin = 10.0 ** (float(p["send_db"]) / 20.0)
    out = (out * send_lin).astype(np.float32)
    out = out[:, :n]

    try:
        _bpm_out = round(float(bpm), 1) if (isinstance(bpm, (int, float)) and bpm == bpm and bpm > 0) else 100.0
    except Exception:
        _bpm_out = 100.0

    info = {
        "preset": str(preset),
        "note_factor": round(nf, 3),
        "delay_ms": round(delay_ms, 1),
        "bpm_used": _bpm_out,
        "feedback": round(fb, 3),
        "send_db": float(p["send_db"]),
        "hpf_hz": float(p["hpf_hz"]),
        "lpf_hz": float(p["lpf_hz"]),
        "width": float(p["width"]),
        "duck_db": float(p["duck_db"]),
        "saturation_drive_db": drive,
        "modulation_rate_hz": float(p["modulation_rate_hz"]),
        "modulation_depth_ms": float(p["modulation_depth_ms"]),
    }
    return out, info


==============================================================================
FILE: fx-api/api/dsp_delay.py   (133 lines)
==============================================================================

"""Tier-1 stereo tape delay: circular buffer, tempo sync, cross-feedback
ping-pong, feedback filters, LFO modulation, envelope duck, tape sat, Numba."""
import numpy as np
from numba import njit


@njit(cache=True)
def _delay_core(xL, xR, sr, delay_samples, mod_depth_samps, mod_rate,
                fb, mix, ahp, alp, atk, rel, duck_depth, width):
    n = len(xL)
    max_delay = delay_samples + int(mod_depth_samps) + 4
    bufL = np.zeros(max_delay, dtype=np.float32)
    bufR = np.zeros(max_delay, dtype=np.float32)

    outL = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float32)
    outR = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float32)

    hp_prevL = 0.0; hp_prevR = 0.0
    x_prevL = 0.0;  x_prevR = 0.0
    lp_L = 0.0;     lp_R = 0.0
    env = 0.0

    two_pi = 6.283185307179586
    ptr = 0
    for i in range(n):
        # LFO modulation, fractional
        mod = mod_depth_samps * np.sin(two_pi * mod_rate * i / sr)
        f = (ptr - delay_samples + mod) % max_delay
        if f < 0.0: f += max_delay
        i0 = int(f)
        fr = f - i0
        i1 = (i0 + 1) % max_delay
        dL = (1.0 - fr) * bufL[i0] + fr * bufL[i1]
        dR = (1.0 - fr) * bufR[i0] + fr * bufR[i1]

        # Feedback filters
        hL = ahp * (hp_prevL + dL - x_prevL)
        hp_prevL = hL; x_prevL = dL
        lp_L += alp * (hL - lp_L)

        hR = ahp * (hp_prevR + dR - x_prevR)
        hp_prevR = hR; x_prevR = dR
        lp_R += alp * (hR - lp_R)

        # Envelope-follower duck (peak of dry L+R)
        rL = xL[i] if xL[i] >= 0.0 else -xL[i]
        rR = xR[i] if xR[i] >= 0.0 else -xR[i]
        rr = rL if rL >= rR else rR
        if rr > env: env += atk * (rr - env)
        else:        env += rel * (rr - env)
        dg = 1.0 - duck_depth * env

        # Tape sat + cross-feedback write
        sL = lp_R
        sR = lp_L
        bufL[ptr] = xL[i] + fb * sL
        bufR[ptr] = xR[i] + fb * sR

        # Wet only widened; dry untouched
        wL = lp_L * mix * dg
        wR = lp_R * mix * dg
        mid = (wL + wR) * 0.5
        side = (wL - wR) * 0.5 * width
        outL[i] = xL[i] + mid + side
        outR[i] = xR[i] + mid - side

        ptr += 1
        if ptr >= max_delay: ptr = 0

    return outL, outR


def coeff_hpf(fc_hz, sr):
    """Correct 1st-order RC highpass coefficient."""
    rc = 1.0 / (2.0 * np.pi * fc_hz)
    dt = 1.0 / sr
    return rc / (rc + dt)


def coeff_tau(tau, sr):
    return 1.0 - np.exp(-1.0 / (tau * sr))


def delay_render(voc, sr, bpm=100.0, preset="afrobeats",
                 note_div=0.75, feedback=0.30, mix=1.0, width=0.80,
                 hp_cut=400.0, lp_cut=5000.0,
                 mod_rate=0.4, mod_depth_ms=0.0,
                 duck_depth=0.3, tape=True):
    """Drop-in for delay_spec.render — same return (wet_2ch, info)."""
    a = np.asarray(voc, dtype=np.float32)
    if a.ndim == 1:
        a = np.stack([a, a])
    if a.shape[0] == 1:
        a = np.stack([a[0], a[0]])

    try: bpm = float(bpm)
    except Exception: bpm = 100.0
    if not (bpm > 0.0): bpm = 100.0

    delay_sec = (60.0 / bpm) * float(note_div)
    delay_samples = int(delay_sec * sr)
    mod_depth_samps = (mod_depth_ms / 1000.0) * sr

    ahp = np.float32(coeff_hpf(hp_cut, sr))
    alp = np.float32(coeff_tau(1.0 / (2.0 * np.pi * lp_cut), sr))
    atk = np.float32(coeff_tau(0.005, sr))
    rel = np.float32(coeff_tau(0.150, sr))

    L, R = _delay_core(
        a[0].astype(np.float32), a[1].astype(np.float32),
        float(sr), int(delay_samples), float(mod_depth_samps),
        float(mod_rate), float(feedback), float(mix),
        float(ahp), float(alp), float(atk), float(rel),
        float(duck_depth), float(width),
    )

    wet = np.stack([L - a[0], R - a[1]]).astype(np.float32)
    pk = float(np.max(np.abs(wet)))
    if pk > 0.95: wet *= 0.95 / pk

    info = {
        "preset": preset,
        "note_factor": float(note_div),
        "delay_ms": round(delay_sec * 1000.0, 1),
        "bpm_used": round(bpm, 1),
        "feedback": float(feedback),
        "send_db": round(20.0 * np.log10(max(1e-6, mix)), 2),
        "hpf_hz": float(hp_cut), "lpf_hz": float(lp_cut),
        "width": float(width), "duck_db": float(duck_depth * 20.0),
        "modulation_rate_hz": float(mod_rate),
        "modulation_depth_ms": float(mod_depth_ms),
    }
    return wet, info


==============================================================================
FILE: fx-api/api/dsp_pro.py   (413 lines)
