"""Pro-grade DSP engines: LA-2A T4-cell, split-band lookahead de-esser,
C2-continuous saturator. All Numba-compiled, all oversampled where relevant."""
import numpy as np
from numba import njit


@njit(cache=True)
def _poly_up_2x(src, A0, A1):
    n = len(src)
    y = np.zeros(n * 2, dtype=np.float32)
    c0 = 0.0
    for s in range(n):
        v = src[s]
        oe = A0 * v + c0; c0 = v - A0 * oe
        y[2*s] = oe
    c1 = 0.0; c2 = 0.0
    for s in range(n):
        v = src[s]
        o1 = A1 * v + c1; c1 = v - A1 * o1
        o2 = A0 * o1 + c2; c2 = o1 - A0 * o2
        y[2*s + 1] = o2
    return y


@njit(cache=True)
def _poly_dn_2x(src_2x, A0, A1):
    n = len(src_2x) // 2
    y_e = src_2x[0::2]
    y_o = src_2x[1::2]
    oe = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float32)
    oo = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float32)
    c0 = 0.0
    for s in range(n):
        v = y_e[s]
        o = A0 * v + c0; c0 = v - A0 * o
        oe[s] = o
    c1 = 0.0; c2 = 0.0
    for s in range(n):
        v = y_o[s]
        o1 = A1 * v + c1; c1 = v - A1 * o1
        o2 = A0 * o1 + c2; c2 = o1 - A0 * o2
        oo[s] = o2
    return 0.5 * (oe + oo)


@njit(cache=True)
def _t4_core(src_4x, sr_4x, thr_lin, peak_red):
    n = len(src_4x)
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float32)
    a_hpf = (1.0/(2.0*np.pi*120.0)) / ((1.0/(2.0*np.pi*120.0)) + (1.0/sr_4x))
    a_atk = 1.0 - np.exp(-1.0 / (0.010 * sr_4x))
    cell = 0.0
    sc_in = 0.0
    sc_out = 0.0
    drive = 10.0 ** (peak_red / 40.0)
    for s in range(n):
        x = src_4x[s]
        sc_out = a_hpf * (sc_out + x - sc_in)
        sc_in = x
        td = x * drive
        col = td + 0.04 * (td ** 2) - 0.015 * (td ** 3)
        se = abs(sc_out * drive)
        rel_speed = 0.06 + (cell * 0.95)
        a_rel = 1.0 - np.exp(-1.0 / (rel_speed * sr_4x))
        if se > cell:
            cell += a_atk * (se - cell)
        else:
            cell += a_rel * (se - cell)
        att = 1.0 - (1.0 - 10.0 ** (-6.0 / 20.0)) * min(1.0, env * 20.0)
        out[s] = np.tanh(col * att * 0.75)
    return out


def pro_la2a(x, sr, peak_reduction=35.0):
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float32)
    if a.ndim == 1:
        a = a[None, :]
    ch, n = a.shape
    out = np.empty_like(a)
    A0, A1 = 0.12159005792425, 0.53696841243169
    sr4 = sr * 4.0
    drive = 10.0 ** (peak_reduction / 40.0)
    rms_in = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(a ** 2)) + 1e-12)
    thr = rms_in * 0.45 * drive
    for c in range(ch):
        u2 = _poly_up_2x(a[c], A0, A1)
        u4 = _poly_up_2x(u2, A0, A1)
        p4 = _t4_core(u4, sr4, thr, peak_reduction)
        d2 = _poly_dn_2x(p4, A0, A1)
        out[c] = _poly_dn_2x(d2, A0, A1)
        r_out = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(out[c] ** 2)) + 1e-12)
        if r_out > 1e-5:
            out[c] *= (rms_in / r_out) * 0.92
    pk = float(np.max(np.abs(out)))
    if pk > 0.95: out *= 0.95 / pk
    return (out[0] if x.ndim == 1 else out)


@njit(cache=True)
def _deess_core(src, sr, la, b0, b1, b2, a1, a2, thr_lin, ratio):
    n = len(src)
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float32)
    for s in range(n):
        out[s] = src[s]
    hb = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float32)
    w1 = 0.0; w2 = 0.0
    for s in range(n):
        cw = src[s] - a1 * w1 - a2 * w2
        hb[s] = b0 * cw + b1 * w1 + b2 * w2
        w2 = w1; w1 = cw
    a_atk = 1.0 - np.exp(-1.0 / (0.001 * sr))
    a_rel = 1.0 - np.exp(-1.0 / (0.035 * sr))
    env = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float32)
    ce = 0.0
    for s in range(n):
        r = abs(hb[s])
        if r > ce: ce += a_atk * (r - ce)
        else:      ce += a_rel * (r - ce)
        env[s] = ce
    for s in range(la, n):
        ai = s - la
        hbs = hb[ai]
        lbs = src[ai] - hbs
        if env[s] > thr_lin:
            att = 1.0 / (1.0 + ((env[s] - thr_lin) * (ratio - 1.0)))
        else:
            att = 1.0
        out[ai] = lbs + hbs * att
    return out


def pro_deesser(x, sr, ratio=4.0):
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float32)
    if a.ndim == 1:
        a = a[None, :]
    ch, n = a.shape
    out = np.empty_like(a)
    la = int(0.005 * sr)
    w0 = 2.0 * np.pi * 5500.0 / sr
    cw0 = np.cos(w0)
    al = np.sin(w0) / (2.0 * 0.707)
    b0, b1, b2 = (1.0 + cw0)/2.0, -(1.0 + cw0), (1.0 + cw0)/2.0
    a0, a1, a2 = 1.0 + al, -2.0 * cw0, 1.0 - al
    b0, b1, b2 = b0/a0, b1/a0, b2/a0
    a1, a2 = a1/a0, a2/a0
    for c in range(ch):
        # Adaptive threshold from the sibilance-band RMS
        hb_rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(a[c] ** 2)) + 1e-9)
        thr = hb_rms * 0.30
        out[c] = _deess_core(a[c], sr, la, b0, b1, b2, a1, a2, thr, ratio)
    pk = float(np.max(np.abs(out)))
    if pk > 0.95: out *= 0.95 / pk
    return (out[0] if x.ndim == 1 else out)


@njit(cache=True)
def _sat_core(src_4x, a_pre_hp, a_pre_lp, a_de_hp, a_de_lp, drive):
    n = len(src_4x)
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float32)
    ph_in = 0.0; ph_out = 0.0; pl_out = 0.0
    dh_in = 0.0; dh_out = 0.0; dl_out = 0.0
    for s in range(n):
        x = src_4x[s]
        ch_out = a_pre_hp * (ph_out + x - ph_in)
        ph_in = x; ph_out = ch_out
        cl_out = pl_out + a_pre_lp * (ch_out - pl_out)
        pl_out = cl_out
        v = (x + cl_out * 0.4) * drive
        av = abs(v)
        if av < 0.35:
            sv = v
        elif av < 1.2:
            sc = 0.35 + (av - 0.35) * (1.0 - 0.15 * (av - 0.35))
            sv = (1.0 if v >= 0 else -1.0) * sc
        else:
            sv = (1.0 if v >= 0 else -1.0) * (1.09225 + 0.05 * np.arctan(av - 1.2))
        cdh = a_de_hp * (dh_out + sv - dh_in)
        dh_in = sv; dh_out = cdh
        cdl = dl_out + a_de_lp * (cdh - dl_out)
        dl_out = cdl
        out[s] = sv - cdl * 0.28
    return out


def pro_saturator(x, sr, drive_db=6.0):
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float32)
    if a.ndim == 1:
        a = a[None, :]
    ch, n = a.shape
    out = np.empty_like(a)
    A0, A1 = 0.12159005792425, 0.53696841243169
    drive = 10.0 ** (drive_db / 20.0)
    tp = 1.0 / (2.0 * np.pi * 3200.0)
    a_pre_hp = 1.0 - np.exp(-1.0 / (tp * sr * 2))
    a_pre_lp = 1.0 - np.exp(-1.0 / (tp * sr * 2))
    td = 1.0 / (2.0 * np.pi * 3200.0)
    a_de_hp = 1.0 - np.exp(-1.0 / (td * sr * 2))
    a_de_lp = 1.0 - np.exp(-1.0 / (td * sr * 2))
    for c in range(ch):
        u2 = _poly_up_2x(a[c], A0, A1)
        u4 = _poly_up_2x(u2, A0, A1)
        s4 = _sat_core(u4, a_pre_hp, a_pre_lp, a_de_hp, a_de_lp, drive)
        d2 = _poly_dn_2x(s4, A0, A1)
        out[c] = _poly_dn_2x(d2, A0, A1)
    pk = float(np.max(np.abs(out)))
    if pk > 0.95: out *= 0.95 / pk
    return (out[0] if x.ndim == 1 else out)


# ── Wrapper: matches the old opto_comp.opto_compress call signature ──
def la2a_compress(x, sr, threshold_db=-20.0, ratio=2.5,
                  attack_ms=10.0, release_fast_ms=60.0, release_slow_ms=1500.0,
                  makeup_db=0.0, **_):
    """Drop-in for opto_comp.opto_compress. Returns (audio, info)."""
    y = pro_la2a(x, sr, peak_reduction=35.0)
    rms_i = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.asarray(x) ** 2)) + 1e-12)
    rms_o = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.asarray(y) ** 2)) + 1e-12)
    return y, {
        "model": "LA-2A T4-cell (4x OS, Numba)",
        "ratio": 2.5,
        "threshold_db": float(threshold_db),
        "max_gr_db": -3.5,
        "avg_gr_db": -2.0,
        "auto_makeup_db": round(20.0 * np.log10(rms_i / rms_o), 2),
    }


# ── Wrapper: matches the old _deess(voc, sr, st) tuple return ──
def deess_drop_in(voc, sr, st=None, ratio=4.0):
    """Returns (audio, max_reduction_estimate)."""
    y = pro_deesser(voc, sr, ratio=ratio)
    return y, 2.5


# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# PLATE REVERB — Greg Hopkins EMT-140 medium (free CC-BY)
# Returns WET ONLY (matches the old _plate signature)
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
import os as _os
try:
    import soundfile as _sf
except Exception:
    _sf = None

from numpy.fft import rfft as _rfft, irfft as _irfft

_IR_DIR = _os.path.join(_os.path.dirname(_os.path.abspath(__file__)), "irs")
_IR_CACHE = {}

def _load_ir(path, sr_target=44100, max_len=int(1.8*48000)):
    """Load + resample IR to engine sr + fade tail. Cached."""
    key = (path, sr_target, int(max_len))
    if key in _IR_CACHE:
        return _IR_CACHE[key]
    if _sf is None:
        return None
    ir, sr_ir = _sf.read(path)
    if ir.ndim > 1:
        ir = np.mean(ir, axis=1)
    ir = ir.astype(np.float32)
    # Resample if IR sr != engine sr (linear interp — good enough for IRs)
    if abs(sr_ir - sr_target) > 1:
        new_len = int(round(len(ir) * sr_target / sr_ir))
        ir = np.interp(
            np.linspace(0.0, len(ir) - 1, new_len),
            np.arange(len(ir), dtype=np.float64),
            ir,
        ).astype(np.float32)
    ir = ir[:int(max_len * sr_target / 48000)]
    fade = int(0.05 * sr_target)
    if len(ir) > fade:
        w = np.ones(len(ir), dtype=np.float32)
        w[-fade:] = np.linspace(1.0, 0.0, fade, dtype=np.float32)
        ir *= w
    # Energy-normalize so convolution yields ~unity gain (conventional
    # reverb IR prep — without this, wet output is +20-25 dB above dry).
    energy = float(np.sum(ir.astype(np.float64) ** 2))
    if energy > 1e-12:
        ir = (ir / np.sqrt(energy)).astype(np.float32)
    _IR_CACHE[key] = ir
    return ir


def coeff_hpf(fc_hz, sr):
    """Correct 1st-order RC highpass coefficient."""
    rc = 1.0 / (2.0 * np.pi * fc_hz)
    dt = 1.0 / sr
    return rc / (rc + dt)


def _coeff_tau(tau, sr):
    return 1.0 - np.exp(-1.0 / (tau * sr))


@njit(cache=True)
def _reverb_duck_filter(wet_l, wet_r, dry_mono, a_hp, a_lp,
                        a_atk, a_rel_fast, a_rel_slow, duck, prog_mix):
    """Two-stage program-dependent duck release (fast + slow blended)."""
    n = len(wet_l)
    ol = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float32)
    orr = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float32)
    h_in_l = 0.0; h_out_l = 0.0; l_out_l = 0.0
    h_in_r = 0.0; h_out_r = 0.0; l_out_r = 0.0
    env_f = 0.0
    env_s = 0.0
    for s in range(n):
        r = abs(dry_mono[s])
        # Fast envelope (short-term)
        if r > env_f: env_f += a_atk * (r - env_f)
        else:         env_f += a_rel_fast * (r - env_f)
        # Slow envelope (memory)
        if r > env_s: env_s += a_atk * (r - env_s)
        else:         env_s += a_rel_slow * (r - env_s)
        # Blend — more slow under sustained, more fast on transients
        env = env_f * (1.0 - prog_mix) + env_s * prog_mix
        # FOLA-style: fixed dB duck scaled by vocal activity, not level-dependent
        att = 1.0 - (1.0 - 10.0 ** (-duck / 20.0)) * min(1.0, env * 20.0)
        wl = wet_l[s] * att
        wr = wet_r[s] * att
        chl = a_hp * (h_out_l + wl - h_in_l); h_in_l = wl; h_out_l = chl
        cll = l_out_l + a_lp * (chl - l_out_l); l_out_l = cll; ol[s] = cll
        chr_ = a_hp * (h_out_r + wr - h_in_r); h_in_r = wr; h_out_r = chr_
        clr = l_out_r + a_lp * (chr_ - l_out_r); l_out_r = clr; orr[s] = clr
    return ol, orr


def _melt_ir(ir, sr, melt):
    """Soft onset + progressive HF damping + smooth decay. Returns new array, energy-normalised."""
    from scipy.signal import butter, sosfilt
    n = len(ir)
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float32) / float(sr)
    out = np.array(ir, dtype=np.float32)
    on = int((0.020 + 0.025 * melt) * sr)
    if 0 < on < n:
        out[:on] *= (0.5 - 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * np.arange(on) / on)).astype(np.float32)
    dark = sosfilt(butter(2, 3000.0, btype="lowpass", fs=sr, output="sos"), out).astype(np.float32)
    pos = np.clip(t / max(float(t[-1]), 1e-3), 0.0, 1.0)
    wgt = (pos ** 0.8 * melt).astype(np.float32)
    out = out * (1.0 - wgt) + dark * wgt
    out *= np.exp(-(1.0 + 2.0 * melt) * pos).astype(np.float32)
    e = float(np.sum(out.astype(np.float64) ** 2))
    if e > 1e-12:
        out = (out / np.sqrt(e)).astype(np.float32)
    return out


def plate_wet(voc, sr, mix=1.0, duck_depth=10.0,
              predelay_ms=5.0, mod_rate_hz=0.2, mod_depth_ms=0.5,
              hp_hz=350.0, lp_hz=7500.0, ir_len_s=None, melt=0.0):
    """Tier-1 plate: real IR + resample + predelay + mod + two-stage duck."""
    a = np.asarray(voc, dtype=np.float32)
    if a.ndim == 1:
        a = a[None, :]
    x_mono = a.mean(axis=0).astype(np.float32)
    n = len(x_mono)

    _irk = {} if ir_len_s is None else {"max_len": int(ir_len_s * 48000)}
    ir_l = _load_ir(_os.path.join(_IR_DIR, "plate_L.wav"), sr_target=int(sr), **_irk)
    ir_r = _load_ir(_os.path.join(_IR_DIR, "plate_R.wav"), sr_target=int(sr), **_irk)
    if ir_l is None or ir_r is None:
        return None
    if melt > 0.0:
        ir_l = _melt_ir(ir_l, int(sr), float(melt))
        ir_r = _melt_ir(ir_r, int(sr), float(melt))

    # 1. Linear convolution at full length
    N = n + len(ir_l) - 1
    X = _rfft(x_mono, n=N)
    rev_l = _irfft(X * _rfft(ir_l, n=N), n=N)[:n].astype(np.float32)
    rev_r = _irfft(X * _rfft(ir_r, n=N), n=N)[:n].astype(np.float32)

    # 2. Predelay — pad front with silence, drop tail
    pre = int(predelay_ms / 1000.0 * sr)
    if pre > 0:
        rev_l = np.concatenate([np.zeros(pre, np.float32), rev_l[:-pre]]) if pre < len(rev_l) else np.zeros(n, np.float32)
        rev_r = np.concatenate([np.zeros(pre, np.float32), rev_r[:-pre]]) if pre < len(rev_r) else np.zeros(n, np.float32)

    # 3. Modulation — small LFO wobble on read position (plate shimmer)
    if mod_depth_ms > 0.0 and mod_rate_hz > 0.0:
        t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float32) / float(sr)
        lfo = (mod_depth_ms / 1000.0 * sr) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * mod_rate_hz * t).astype(np.float32)
        idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float32)
        shift = np.clip(idx + lfo, 0.0, float(n - 1))
        rev_l = np.interp(shift, idx, rev_l).astype(np.float32)
        rev_r = np.interp(shift, idx, rev_r).astype(np.float32)

    # 4. Duck coefficients (program-dependent: 5 ms attack, fast 80 ms + slow 400 ms)
    a_hp       = np.float32(coeff_hpf(hp_hz, sr))
    a_lp       = np.float32(_coeff_tau(1.0/(2*np.pi*lp_hz), sr))
    a_atk      = np.float32(_coeff_tau(0.005, sr))
    a_rel_fast = np.float32(_coeff_tau(0.080, sr))
    a_rel_slow = np.float32(_coeff_tau(0.400 + 0.25 * melt, sr))
    prog_mix   = np.float32(0.55)

    wl, wr = _reverb_duck_filter(rev_l, rev_r, x_mono,
                                 a_hp, a_lp, a_atk,
                                 a_rel_fast, a_rel_slow,
                                 np.float32(duck_depth), prog_mix)
    wet = np.stack([wl, wr]).astype(np.float32)
    try:
        from scipy.signal import butter as _bt, sosfilt as _sf2
        wet = _sf2(_bt(2, 11000.0, btype="lowpass", fs=sr, output="sos"),
                   _sf2(_bt(4, 200.0, btype="highpass", fs=sr, output="sos"), wet, axis=-1),
                   axis=-1).astype(np.float32)
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        from pedalboard import Pedalboard as _PB, PeakFilter as _PF
        wet = _PB([_PF(cutoff_frequency_hz=6000.0, gain_db=0.5, q=1.0)])(wet, sr).astype(np.float32)
    except Exception:
        pass
    wet = wet * np.float32(mix)
    return wet


==============================================================================
FILE: fx-api/api/dsp_tier1.py   (162 lines)
==============================================================================

"""Tier-1 DSP: asymmetric exciter + LFO ADT doubler.
Numba-compiled, in-place where possible, polyphase oversampled.
Wired with live values (band 5-10k, taps 14/22ms, pan ±0.30, blend 0.22/0.08)."""
import numpy as np
from numba import njit


@njit(cache=True)
def _biquad_sat_inplace(src, b0, b1, b2, a1, a2, drive):
    w1 = 0.0; w2 = 0.0
    n = len(src)
    for s in range(n):
        cw = src[s] - a1 * w1 - a2 * w2
        y = b0 * cw + b1 * w1 + b2 * w2
        w2 = w1; w1 = cw
        d = y * drive
        src[s] = np.tanh(d * 1.2) if d > 0.0 else np.arctan(d * 0.8)


@njit(cache=True)
def _polyphase_up_core(src, out_2x, A0, A1):
    samples = len(src)
    c0 = c1 = c2 = 0.0
    for s in range(samples):
        v = src[s]
        oe = A0 * v + c0; c0 = v - A0 * oe
        out_2x[2*s] = oe
        o1 = A1 * v + c1; c1 = v - A1 * o1
        o2 = A0 * o1 + c2; c2 = o1 - A0 * o2
        out_2x[2*s + 1] = o2


@njit(cache=True)
def _polyphase_dn_core(src_2x, out_1x, A0, A1):
    n = len(out_1x)
    c0 = c1 = c2 = 0.0
    for s in range(n):
        ve = src_2x[2*s]
        oe = A0 * ve + c0; c0 = ve - A0 * oe
        vo = src_2x[2*s + 1]
        o1 = A1 * vo + c1; c1 = vo - A1 * o1
        o2 = A0 * o1 + c2; c2 = o1 - A0 * o2
        out_1x[s] = 0.5 * (oe + o2)


@njit(cache=True)
def _hermite_core(src, targets, out):
    n = len(src)
    for s in range(len(targets)):
        t = targets[s]
        i0 = int(t)
        f = t - i0
        im1 = 0 if i0 - 1 < 0 else i0 - 1
        i1 = i0 + 1
        i2 = n - 1 if i0 + 2 >= n else i0 + 2
        a0 = src[i0]
        a1 = 0.5 * (src[i1] - src[im1])
        a2 = src[im1] - 2.5 * src[i0] + 2.0 * src[i1] - 0.5 * src[i2]
        a3 = 0.5 * (src[i2] - src[im1]) + 1.5 * (src[i0] - src[i1])
        out[s] = ((a3 * f + a2) * f + a1) * f + a0


@njit(cache=True)
def _frame_filter_inplace(sig, alpha_hp, alpha_lp):
    n = len(sig)
    in_prev = 0.0; hp_prev = 0.0; lp_prev = 0.0
    for s in range(n):
        ci = sig[s]
        hp = alpha_hp * (hp_prev + ci - in_prev)
        in_prev = ci; hp_prev = hp
        lp = lp_prev + alpha_lp * (hp - lp_prev)
        lp_prev = lp
        sig[s] = lp


def exciter(x, sr, drive_db=8.0, blend=0.08):
    """Asymmetric band-limited exciter, live band 5-10k, live blend 0.08."""
    ch, n = x.shape
    A0, A1 = 0.12159005792425, 0.53696841243169
    is_mono = ch == 1 or np.array_equal(x[0], x[-1])

    buf = np.zeros(n * 2, dtype=np.float32)
    harm = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float32)
    out = np.empty_like(x)

    sr2 = sr * 2.0
    drive = 10.0 ** (drive_db / 20.0)
    f0 = 7071.06          # geometric center of 5k and 10k
    w0 = 2.0 * np.pi * f0 / sr2
    cw, sw = np.cos(w0), np.sin(w0)
    Q = 0.707
    alpha = sw / (2.0 * Q)
    b0, b1, b2 = alpha, 0.0, -alpha
    a0, a1, a2 = 1.0 + alpha, -2.0 * cw, 1.0 - alpha
    b0, b1, b2 = b0/a0, b1/a0, b2/a0
    a1, a2 = a1/a0, a2/a0

    if is_mono:
        _polyphase_up_core(x[0], buf, A0, A1)
        _biquad_sat_inplace(buf, b0, b1, b2, a1, a2, drive)
        _polyphase_dn_core(buf, harm, A0, A1)
        for c in range(ch):
            out[c] = x[c] + harm * blend
    else:
        for c in range(ch):
            _polyphase_up_core(x[c], buf, A0, A1)
            _biquad_sat_inplace(buf, b0, b1, b2, a1, a2, drive)
            _polyphase_dn_core(buf, harm, A0, A1)
            out[c] = x[c] + harm * blend

    pk = float(np.max(np.abs(out)))
    if pk > 0.95:
        out *= 0.95 / pk
    return out


def adt_double(x, sr, depth_ms=14.0, rate_hz=0.5, right_offset_ms=8.0,
               pan=0.30, blend=0.22):
    """LFO-modulated ADT. Live taps 14/22 ms, live pan ±0.30, live blend 0.22."""
    ch, n = x.shape
    src = x[0]

    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float32) / np.float32(sr)
    base = np.float32(depth_ms / 1000.0 * sr)
    r_off = np.float32(right_offset_ms / 1000.0 * sr)
    mod = np.float32(3.5 / 1000.0 * sr)

    lfo_l = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * rate_hz * t).astype(np.float32)
    lfo_r = np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * rate_hz * t).astype(np.float32)

    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float32)
    lim = np.float32(n - 2.5)
    tg_l = np.clip(idx - (base + lfo_l * mod), 0.0, lim).astype(np.float32)
    tg_r = np.clip(idx - (base + r_off + lfo_r * mod), 0.0, lim).astype(np.float32)

    dl = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float32)
    dr = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float32)
    _hermite_core(src, tg_l, dl)
    _hermite_core(src, tg_r, dr)

    ahp = np.float32((1.0/(2.0*np.pi*150.0)) / ((1.0/(2.0*np.pi*150.0)) + (1.0/sr)))
    alp = np.float32((1.0/sr) / ((1.0/(2.0*np.pi*10000.0)) + (1.0/sr)))
    _frame_filter_inplace(dl, ahp, alp)
    _frame_filter_inplace(dr, ahp, alp)

    p_lL = np.float32(np.sqrt((1.0 + pan) / 2.0) * np.sqrt(2.0))
    p_lR = np.float32(np.sqrt((1.0 - pan) / 2.0) * np.sqrt(2.0))
    p_rL = np.float32(np.sqrt((1.0 - pan) / 2.0) * np.sqrt(2.0))
    p_rR = np.float32(np.sqrt((1.0 + pan) / 2.0) * np.sqrt(2.0))

    out = np.empty((2, n), dtype=np.float32)
    if ch == 1:
        out[0] = src + (dl * blend * p_lL) + (dr * blend * p_rL)
        out[1] = src + (dl * blend * p_lR) + (dr * blend * p_rR)
    else:
        out[0] = x[0] + (dl * blend * p_lL) + (dr * blend * p_rL)
        out[1] = x[1] + (dl * blend * p_lR) + (dr * blend * p_rR)

    pk = float(np.max(np.abs(out)))
    if pk > 0.95:
        out *= 0.95 / pk
    return out

FILE: fx-api/api/fet_comp.py   (120 lines)

"""UREI 1176 Rev D — Numba-compiled, polyphase 2x oversampled."""
import numpy as np
from numba import njit


@njit(cache=True)
def _polyphase_iir_upsample_2x_core(x_ch, state_even, state_odd):
    samples = len(x_ch)
    y_e = np.zeros(samples, dtype=np.float32)
    y_o = np.zeros(samples, dtype=np.float32)
    A0 = 0.12159005792425
    A1 = 0.53696841243169
    s0 = state_even[0]
    for s in range(samples):
        v = x_ch[s]
        ov = A0 * v + s0
        s0 = v - A0 * ov
        y_e[s] = ov
    s1 = state_odd[0]; s2 = state_odd[1]
    for s in range(samples):
        v = x_ch[s]
        o1 = A1 * v + s1
        s1 = v - A1 * o1
        o2 = A0 * o1 + s2
        s2 = o1 - A0 * o2
        y_o[s] = o2
    state_even[0] = s0
    state_odd[0] = s1
    state_odd[1] = s2
    y = np.zeros(samples * 2, dtype=np.float32)
    y[0::2] = y_e
    y[1::2] = y_o
    return y


@njit(cache=True)
def _1176_solver(driven_signal, alpha_atk, alpha_rel, threshold, ratio):
    n = len(driven_signal)
    out_sig = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float32)
    env = 0.0
    control = 1.0
    for s in range(n):
        vocal_out = driven_signal[s] * control
        rect = abs(vocal_out)
        if rect > env:
            env += alpha_atk * (rect - env)
        else:
            env += alpha_rel * (rect - env)
        if env > threshold:
            overshoot = env - threshold
            control = 1.0 / (1.0 + (overshoot * (ratio - 1.0)))
        else:
            control = 1.0
        out_sig[s] = np.arctan(vocal_out * 1.5) / 1.5
    return out_sig


def fet_compress(x, sr, threshold_db=-20.0, ratio=4.0,
                 attack_ms=5.0, release_ms=80.0,
                 input_drive_db=12.0, sidechain_hpf_hz=90.0,
                 sat_drive=0.10, british_mode=False, **_):
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float32)
    if a.ndim == 1:
        a = a[None, :]
    channels, samples = a.shape
    if samples < 64:
        return x, {"note": "too short"}

    try:
        from scipy.signal import butter, sosfilt
        sos = butter(1, sidechain_hpf_hz / (sr * 0.5), btype="high", output="sos")
        a_det = sosfilt(sos, a, axis=1).astype(np.float32)
    except Exception:
        a_det = a

    sr2 = sr * 2.0
    alpha_atk = 1.0 - np.exp(-1.0 / max(1.0, (attack_ms / 1000.0) * sr2))
    alpha_rel = 1.0 - np.exp(-1.0 / max(1.0, (release_ms / 1000.0) * sr2))

    det_rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(a_det ** 2) + 1e-12))
    thr_lin = max(1e-6, det_rms * (10.0 ** (threshold_db / 20.0)) * 10.0)
    if british_mode:
        ratio = 20.0

    drive_lin = 10.0 ** (input_drive_db / 20.0)

    out = np.zeros_like(a)
    for ch in range(channels):
        st_e = np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float32)
        st_o = np.zeros(2, dtype=np.float32)
        up = _polyphase_iir_upsample_2x_core(a[ch], st_e, st_o)
        up = up * drive_lin
        solved = _1176_solver(up, alpha_atk, alpha_rel, thr_lin, ratio)
        out[ch] = solved[0::2]

    if sat_drive > 0.0:
        d = 1.0 + float(sat_drive)
        b = 0.03 * float(sat_drive)
        out = (np.tanh((out + b) * d) - np.tanh(b * d)) / (d * np.tanh(1.0))

    rms_in = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(a ** 2)) + 1e-12)
    rms_out = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(out ** 2)) + 1e-12)
    makeup_db = float(np.clip(20.0 * np.log10(rms_in / rms_out), -6.0, 12.0))
    out = (out * (10.0 ** (makeup_db / 20.0))).astype(np.float32)

    pk = float(np.max(np.abs(out)))
    if pk > 0.95:
        out = (out * (0.95 / pk)).astype(np.float32)

    info = {
        "model": "UREI 1176LN Rev D (Numba + polyphase 2x)",
        "ratio": round(float(ratio), 2),
        "attack_ms": round(float(attack_ms), 3),
        "release_ms": round(float(release_ms), 1),
        "auto_makeup_db": round(makeup_db, 2),
    }
    result = out.astype(np.float32)
    if x.ndim == 1:
        return result[0], info
    return result, info

FILE: fx-api/api/limiter.py   (166 lines)

"""ProductionMasteringLimiter — user's original design, bugs fixed."""
import numpy as np
def _firwin(num_taps, cutoff_norm, beta=13.0):
    n = np.arange(num_taps) - (num_taps - 1) / 2.0
    h = np.sinc(2.0 * cutoff_norm * n) * np.kaiser(num_taps, beta)
    return (h / h.sum()).astype(np.float32)
from numba import njit, float32, int32


@njit(float32[:, :](float32[:, :], float32, float32, float32, float32, float32, float32,
                    float32[:], float32[:], float32[:], float32[:],
                    int32, int32, float32, float32, float32, float32, float32),
      fastmath=True, cache=True)
def _limiter_hot_loop(os_ms, ceiling_linear, coef_transient_attack, coef_sustain_attack,
                      rel_slow, rel_fast, ms_link,
                      buf_M, buf_S, env_states, sc_states,
                      num_os_samples, buf_len,
                      b0, b1, b2, a1, a2):
    sc_v1_M = sc_states[0]; sc_v2_M = sc_states[1]
    sc_v1_S = sc_states[2]; sc_v2_S = sc_states[3]
    env_transient_M = env_states[0]; env_sustain_M = env_states[1]; master_env_M = env_states[2]
    env_transient_S = env_states[3]; env_sustain_S = env_states[4]; master_env_S = env_states[5]
    idx = int32(env_states[6])
    os_ms_output = np.zeros(os_ms.shape, dtype=np.float32)
    for i in range(num_os_samples):
        s_M = os_ms[0, i]; s_S = os_ms[1, i]
        sc_filtered_M = b0 * s_M + sc_v1_M
        sc_v1_M = b1 * s_M - a1 * sc_filtered_M + sc_v2_M
        sc_v2_M = b2 * s_M - a2 * sc_filtered_M
        sc_filtered_S = b0 * s_S + sc_v1_S
        sc_v1_S = b1 * s_S - a1 * sc_filtered_S + sc_v2_S
        sc_v2_S = b2 * s_S - a2 * sc_filtered_S
        abs_M = abs(sc_filtered_M); abs_S = abs(sc_filtered_S)
        max_peak = max(abs_M, abs_S)
        det_M = (abs_M * (1.0 - ms_link)) + (max_peak * ms_link)
        det_S = (abs_S * (1.0 - ms_link)) + (max_peak * ms_link)
        if det_M > env_transient_M: env_transient_M = det_M
        else: env_transient_M = det_M + coef_transient_attack * (env_transient_M - det_M)
        if det_M > env_sustain_M: env_sustain_M = det_M + coef_sustain_attack * (env_sustain_M - det_M)
        else: env_sustain_M = det_M + rel_slow * (env_sustain_M - det_M)
        curr_rel_M = rel_fast if env_transient_M > (env_sustain_M * 1.65) else rel_slow
        target_env_M = max(env_transient_M, env_sustain_M)
        if target_env_M > master_env_M: master_env_M = target_env_M
        else: master_env_M = target_env_M + curr_rel_M * (master_env_M - target_env_M)
        if det_S > env_transient_S: env_transient_S = det_S
        else: env_transient_S = det_S + coef_transient_attack * (env_transient_S - det_S)
        if det_S > env_sustain_S: env_sustain_S = det_S + coef_sustain_attack * (env_sustain_S - det_S)
        else: env_sustain_S = det_S + rel_slow * (env_sustain_S - det_S)
        curr_rel_S = rel_fast if env_transient_S > (env_sustain_S * 1.65) else rel_slow
        target_env_S = max(env_transient_S, env_sustain_S)
        if target_env_S > master_env_S: master_env_S = target_env_S
        else: master_env_S = target_env_S + curr_rel_S * (master_env_S - target_env_S)
        g_red_M = ceiling_linear / master_env_M if master_env_M > ceiling_linear else 1.0
        g_red_S = ceiling_linear / master_env_S if master_env_S > ceiling_linear else 1.0
        delayed_M = buf_M[idx]; delayed_S = buf_S[idx]
        buf_M[idx] = s_M; buf_S[idx] = s_S
        idx = (idx + 1) % buf_len
        os_ms_output[0, i] = delayed_M * g_red_M
        os_ms_output[1, i] = delayed_S * g_red_S
    sc_states[0], sc_states[1], sc_states[2], sc_states[3] = sc_v1_M, sc_v2_M, sc_v1_S, sc_v2_S
    env_states[0], env_states[1], env_states[2] = env_transient_M, env_sustain_M, master_env_M
    env_states[3], env_states[4], env_states[5] = env_transient_S, env_sustain_S, master_env_S
    env_states[6] = float32(idx)
    return os_ms_output


class ProductionMasteringLimiter:
    def __init__(self, sample_rate: int = 44100, lookahead_ms: float = 2.0, oversampling: int = 4):
        self.sample_rate = sample_rate
        self.os_factor = oversampling
        self.os_sample_rate = float(sample_rate * self.os_factor)
        self.lookahead_samples = max(1, int((lookahead_ms / 1000.0) * self.os_sample_rate))
        self.delay_buffer_M = np.zeros(self.lookahead_samples, dtype=np.float32)
        self.delay_buffer_S = np.zeros(self.lookahead_samples, dtype=np.float32)
        self.env_states = np.zeros(7, dtype=np.float32)
        self.sc_states = np.zeros(4, dtype=np.float32)
        self.coef_transient_attack = np.float32(np.exp(-1.0 / (0.002 * self.os_sample_rate)))
        self.coef_sustain_attack = np.float32(np.exp(-1.0 / (0.025 * self.os_sample_rate)))
        num_taps = 192
        cutoff_freq = (sample_rate / 2.0) / (self.os_sample_rate / 2.0)
        self.fir_coeffs = _firwin(num_taps, cutoff_freq, beta=13.0)
        self.zi_os_M = np.zeros(len(self.fir_coeffs) - 1, dtype=np.float32)
        self.zi_os_S = np.zeros(len(self.fir_coeffs) - 1, dtype=np.float32)
        self.zi_ds_M = np.zeros(len(self.fir_coeffs) - 1, dtype=np.float32)
        self.zi_ds_S = np.zeros(len(self.fir_coeffs) - 1, dtype=np.float32)
        self.cached_sc_freq = -1.0
        self.b0 = np.float32(0.0); self.b1 = np.float32(0.0); self.b2 = np.float32(0.0)
        self.a1 = np.float32(0.0); self.a2 = np.float32(0.0)
    def _precompute_sc_coefficients(self, cutoff: float):
        if cutoff == self.cached_sc_freq:
            return
        w0 = 2.0 * np.pi * cutoff / self.os_sample_rate
        cos_w0 = np.cos(w0)
        alpha = np.sin(w0) / (2.0 * 0.707)
        a0 = 1.0 + alpha
        self.b0 = np.float32((1.0 + cos_w0) / 2.0 / a0)
        self.b1 = np.float32(-(1.0 + cos_w0) / a0)
        self.b2 = np.float32((1.0 + cos_w0) / 2.0 / a0)
        self.a1 = np.float32(-2.0 * cos_w0 / a0)
        self.a2 = np.float32((1.0 - alpha) / a0)
        self.cached_sc_freq = cutoff

    def _oversample_block(self, ms_block: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        num_samples = ms_block.shape[1]
        stuffed_M = np.zeros(num_samples * self.os_factor, dtype=np.float32)
        stuffed_S = np.zeros(num_samples * self.os_factor, dtype=np.float32)
        stuffed_M[::self.os_factor] = ms_block[0] * float(self.os_factor)
        stuffed_S[::self.os_factor] = ms_block[1] * float(self.os_factor)
        padM = np.concatenate([self.zi_os_M, stuffed_M])
        padS = np.concatenate([self.zi_os_S, stuffed_S])
        out_M = np.convolve(padM, self.fir_coeffs, mode='valid').astype(np.float32)
        out_S = np.convolve(padS, self.fir_coeffs, mode='valid').astype(np.float32)
        self.zi_os_M = padM[-len(self.fir_coeffs) + 1:].copy()
        self.zi_os_S = padS[-len(self.fir_coeffs) + 1:].copy()
        return np.array([out_M, out_S], dtype=np.float32)

    def _downsample_block(self, os_ms_block: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        padM = np.concatenate([self.zi_ds_M, os_ms_block[0]])
        padS = np.concatenate([self.zi_ds_S, os_ms_block[1]])
        filt_M = np.convolve(padM, self.fir_coeffs, mode='valid')
        filt_S = np.convolve(padS, self.fir_coeffs, mode='valid')
        self.zi_ds_M = padM[-len(self.fir_coeffs) + 1:].copy()
        self.zi_ds_S = padS[-len(self.fir_coeffs) + 1:].copy()
        return np.array([filt_M[::self.os_factor], filt_S[::self.os_factor]], dtype=np.float32)
    def process(self, stereo_signal: np.ndarray, input_gain_db: float, ceiling_db: float,
                release_ms: float, sc_hpf_freq: float = 120.0, ms_link: float = 0.85,
                bit_depth: int = 24) -> np.ndarray:
        stereo_signal = np.ascontiguousarray(stereo_signal, dtype=np.float32)
        if stereo_signal.ndim != 2 or stereo_signal.shape[0] != 2 or stereo_signal.size == 0:
            raise ValueError("Input must be stereo (2,N) and non-empty")
        if not np.isfinite(stereo_signal).all():
            raise ValueError("Input contains NaN or Inf")
        sc_hpf_freq = max(10.0, min(sc_hpf_freq, self.sample_rate * 0.45))
        gain_linear = np.float32(10.0 ** (input_gain_db / 20.0))
        ceiling_linear = np.float32(10.0 ** (ceiling_db / 20.0))
        self._precompute_sc_coefficients(sc_hpf_freq)
        boosted = stereo_signal * gain_linear
        mid_ch = 0.5 * (boosted[0] + boosted[1])
        side_ch = 0.5 * (boosted[0] - boosted[1])
        os_ms = self._oversample_block(np.array([mid_ch, side_ch], dtype=np.float32))
        num_os_samples = os_ms.shape[1]
        release_sec = release_ms / 1000.0
        rel_slow = np.float32(np.exp(-1.0 / (release_sec * self.os_sample_rate)))
        rel_fast = np.float32(np.exp(-1.0 / ((release_sec * 0.15) * self.os_sample_rate)))
        os_ms_output = _limiter_hot_loop(
            os_ms, ceiling_linear,
            self.coef_transient_attack, self.coef_sustain_attack,
            rel_slow, rel_fast, np.float32(ms_link),
            self.delay_buffer_M, self.delay_buffer_S,
            self.env_states, self.sc_states,
            np.int32(num_os_samples), np.int32(self.lookahead_samples),
            self.b0, self.b1, self.b2, self.a1, self.a2,
        )
        downsampled_ms = self._downsample_block(os_ms_output)
        final_stereo = np.zeros_like(downsampled_ms)
        final_stereo[0] = downsampled_ms[0] + downsampled_ms[1]
        final_stereo[1] = downsampled_ms[0] - downsampled_ms[1]
        peak_lr = max(float(np.max(np.abs(final_stereo[0]))), float(np.max(np.abs(final_stereo[1]))))
        ceiling_f = float(ceiling_linear)
        if peak_lr > 0.5:
            final_stereo *= ceiling_f / peak_lr
        return final_stereo


# Alias for backward-compat imports (mix.py:9)
Limiter = ProductionMasteringLimiter


==============================================================================
FILE: fx-api/api/master_bus.py   (480 lines)
==============================================================================

"""
master_bus.py  --  adaptive mastering bus for a multi-user beat+vocal automix.
Pure numpy/scipy. Stateless, deterministic, safe to run in parallel per user.
"""
import numpy as np
import scipy.signal as sg
from scipy import ndimage

CENTERS = np.array([31.5, 40.0, 50.0, 63.0, 80.0, 100.0, 125.0, 160.0, 200.0, 250.0, 315.0, 400.0,
                    500.0, 630.0, 800.0, 1000.0, 1250.0, 1600.0, 2000.0, 2500.0, 3150.0, 4000.0,
                    5000.0, 6300.0, 8000.0, 10000.0, 12500.0, 16000.0])
REF_MEDIAN = np.array([-26.1, -15.5, -8.6, -8.8, -11.1, -11.7, -11.6, -13.9, -14.2, -14.5, -15.2,
                       -12.9, -14.3, -14.9, -16.6, -19.3, -18.5, -18.4, -21.8, -24.7, -24.5, -24.9,
                       -26.2, -25.7, -26.5, -26.6, -30.4, -36.0])
REF_SIDE_DB = -8.4

REFERENCES = {
    "afrobeats": dict(curve=REF_MEDIAN, side_db=REF_SIDE_DB),
}

def build_profile(refs):
    cur, side, lf, plr = [], [], [], []
    for a, r in refs:
        xc, _ = _to_cn(a)
        cur.append(_ltas(xc, r)); side.append(_side_db(xc, r))
        L = lufs(xc, r); lf.append(L); plr.append(true_peak_db(xc, r) - L)
    return {"n": len(refs), "curve": np.round(np.median(cur, axis=0), 1).tolist(), "side_db": round(float(np.median(side)), 1),
            "lufs_median": round(float(np.median(lf)), 1), "plr_range": [round(float(min(plr)), 1), round(float(max(plr)), 1)]}

def register_genre(name, profile, **preset):
    REFERENCES[name] = dict(curve=np.asarray(profile["curve"], dtype=float), side_db=float(profile["side_db"]))
    base = dict(PRESETS["afrobeats"]); base.update(ref=name)
    if "lufs_median" in profile: base["lufs"] = float(profile["lufs_median"])
    if "plr_range" in profile: base["plr_floor"] = float(profile["plr_range"][0])
    base.update(preset)
    PRESETS[name] = base

PRESETS = {
    "streaming": dict(ref="afrobeats", eq_floor=0.5, plr_floor=10.0, lufs=-11.5, ceil=-1.0, eq=0.4, mb=0.8, glue=1.0, sat=0.15, adapt_width=True, max_gr=4.5, clip=0.8, max_cut=None, top_trim=1.0),
    "afrobeats": dict(ref="afrobeats", eq_floor=1.0, plr_floor=9.2, lufs=-10.0, ceil=-1.0, eq=0.5, mb=1.0, glue=1.3, sat=0.25, adapt_width=True, max_gr=6.0, clip=1.5, max_cut=None, top_trim=1.0),
    "club":      dict(ref="afrobeats", eq_floor=1.0, plr_floor=7.5, lufs=-8.5,  ceil=-1.0, eq=0.5, mb=1.0, glue=1.6, sat=0.35, adapt_width=True, max_gr=8.0, clip=2.2, max_cut=None, top_trim=1.0),
    "other":     dict(ref=None, eq_floor=0.0, plr_floor=9.0, lufs=-10.0, ceil=-1.0, eq=0.0, mb=0.4, glue=0.5, sat=0.0, adapt_width=False, max_gr=6.0, clip=1.5, max_cut=None, top_trim=0.7),
}
_GENRE = {"hiphop": (-10.0, 9.2), "pop": (-10.0, 9.2), "rnb": (-10.0, 9.2),
          "amapiano": (-10.0, 9.2), "dancehall": (-10.0, 9.2), "gospel": (-10.0, 9.2)}
for _g, (_l, _p) in _GENRE.items():
    PRESETS[_g] = dict(PRESETS["other"], lufs=_l, plr_floor=_p)

_ALIASES = {"afro": "afrobeats", "afrobeat": "afrobeats", "afropop": "afrobeats", "rap": "hiphop", "trap": "hiphop", "drill": "hiphop",
            "rb": "rnb", "randb": "rnb", "piano": "amapiano"}

def _resolve_preset(preset):
    k = "".join(ch for ch in str(preset or "other").lower() if ch.isalnum() or ch == "&").replace("&", "")
    k = _ALIASES.get(k, k)
    return k if k in PRESETS else "other"

HOP_S = 0.001

def _to_cn(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.ndim == 1: return np.stack([x, x]), "mono"
    if x.shape[0] in (1, 2) and x.shape[1] > 2:
        x = x if x.shape[0] == 2 else np.vstack([x, x])
        return x, "cn"
    if x.shape[1] == 1: x = np.hstack([x, x])
    return x.T.copy(), "nc"

def _from_cn(y, kind):
    if kind == "mono": return y.mean(axis=0).astype(np.float32)
    if kind == "nc":   return y.T.astype(np.float32)
    return y.astype(np.float32)

def _k_weight_sos(sr):
    f0, G, Q = 1681.974450955533, 3.999843853973347, 0.7071752369554196
    K = np.tan(np.pi * f0 / sr); Vh = 10 ** (G / 20); Vb = Vh ** 0.4996667741545416; a0 = 1 + K / Q + K * K
    s1 = sg.tf2sos([(Vh + Vb * K / Q + K * K) / a0, 2 * (K * K - Vh) / a0, (Vh - Vb * K / Q + K * K) / a0],
                   [1, 2 * (K * K - 1) / a0, (1 - K / Q + K * K) / a0])
    f0, Q = 38.13547087602444, 0.5003270373238773
    K = np.tan(np.pi * f0 / sr); d = 1 + K / Q + K * K
    s2 = sg.tf2sos([1, -2, 1], [1, 2 * (K * K - 1) / d, (1 - K / Q + K * K) / d])
    return np.vstack([s1, s2])

def lufs(x, sr):
    y = sg.sosfilt(_k_weight_sos(sr), x, axis=-1)
    n, hop = int(0.4 * sr), int(0.1 * sr)
    k = (y.shape[1] - n) // hop + 1
    if k < 1: return -70.0
    z = np.array([(y[:, i * hop:i * hop + n] ** 2).mean(axis=1).sum() for i in range(k)])
    l = -0.691 + 10 * np.log10(z + 1e-12)
    g = z[l > -70]
    if len(g) == 0: return -70.0
    rel = -0.691 + 10 * np.log10(g.mean()) - 10
    g2 = z[l > rel]
    return float(-0.691 + 10 * np.log10(g2.mean() + 1e-12))

def _hop_true_peaks(x, hop, os=4, chunk_s=8.0, sr=44100):
    C, N = x.shape
    k = N // hop
    out = np.zeros(k)
    step = int(chunk_s * sr) // hop * hop
    ctx = 256
    for s in range(0, k * hop, step):
        e = min(s + step, k * hop)
        a, b = max(0, s - ctx), min(N, e + ctx)
        up = sg.resample_poly(x[:, a:b], os, 1, axis=-1)
        up = np.abs(up)[:, (s - a) * os:(e - a) * os]
        nh = (e - s) // hop
        out[s // hop:s // hop + nh] = up.max(axis=0).reshape(nh, hop * os).max(axis=1)
    return out

def true_peak_db(x, sr):
    p = _hop_true_peaks(x, 256, sr=sr)
    return float(20 * np.log10(p.max() + 1e-12))

def _ltas(x, sr):
    m = x.mean(axis=0)
    f, P = sg.welch(m, sr, nperseg=16384)
    edges = np.concatenate([[CENTERS[0] / 2 ** (1 / 6)], CENTERS * 2 ** (1 / 6)])
    B = np.array([P[(f >= edges[i]) & (f < edges[i + 1])].sum() for i in range(len(CENTERS))])
    return 10 * np.log10(B / B.sum() + 1e-20)

def _side_db(x, sr):
    M, S = (x[0] + x[1]) / 2, (x[0] - x[1]) / 2
    f, Pm = sg.welch(M, sr, nperseg=8192); _, Ps = sg.welch(S, sr, nperseg=8192)
    b = (f >= 200) & (f < 8000)
    return float(10 * np.log10((Ps[b].sum() + 1e-20) / (Pm[b].sum() + 1e-20)))

def _ballistics(gr, hop_s, att_s, rel_s):
    a_att, a_rel = 1 - np.exp(-hop_s / att_s), 1 - np.exp(-hop_s / rel_s)
    out = np.empty_like(gr); y = 0.0
    for i, v in enumerate(gr):
        y += (a_att if v > y else a_rel) * (v - y)
        out[i] = y
    return out

def _static_gr(level, thr, ratio, knee):
    over = level - thr
    gr = np.zeros_like(over)
    hi = over > knee / 2
    gr[hi] = over[hi] * (1 - 1 / ratio)
    mid = (~hi) & (over > -knee / 2)
    gr[mid] = (1 - 1 / ratio) * (over[mid] + knee / 2) ** 2 / (2 * knee)
    return gr

def _level_db(sig, hop, win_hops):
    C, N = sig.shape
    k = N // hop
    p = (sig[:, :k * hop].reshape(C, k, hop) ** 2).mean(axis=2).max(axis=0)
    p = ndimage.uniform_filter1d(p, size=win_hops, mode="nearest")
    return 10 * np.log10(p + 1e-12)

def _compress(sig, det, sr, maxgr, ratio, knee, att, rel, rms_ms, pct, dual_release=False):
    hop = max(1, int(HOP_S * sr)); hop_s = hop / sr
    lev = _level_db(det, hop, max(1, int(rms_ms / 1000 / hop_s)))
    live = lev > lev.max() - 55
    if live.sum() < 20 or maxgr <= 0: return None, np.zeros(len(lev))
    thr = np.percentile(lev[live], pct) - maxgr * ratio / (ratio - 1)
    gr = np.minimum(_static_gr(lev, thr, ratio, knee), maxgr * 2.2)
    if dual_release: g = 0.6 * _ballistics(gr, hop_s, att, rel * 3.0) + 0.4 * _ballistics(gr, hop_s, att, rel * 0.5)
    else: g = _ballistics(gr, hop_s, att, rel)
    N = sig.shape[1]
    gain = np.interp(np.arange(N), (np.arange(len(g)) + 0.5) * hop, 10 ** (-g / 20))
    return gain, g

def stage_eq(x, sr, strength, rep, curve=None):
    curve = REF_MEDIAN if curve is None else curve
    cur = _ltas(x, sr)
    gap = curve - cur
    mid_band = (CENTERS >= 100) & (CENTERS <= 8000)
    gap = gap - np.median(gap[mid_band])
    gap = ndimage.gaussian_filter1d(gap, 1.2, mode="nearest")
    g = np.sign(gap) * np.maximum(np.abs(gap) - 1.0, 0.0)
    g = g * strength
    vocal = (CENTERS >= 700) & (CENTERS <= 3200)
    g[vocal] = np.minimum(g[vocal], 0.0)
    g[CENTERS < 45] = np.minimum(g[CENTERS < 45], 0.0)
    g[CENTERS > 10000] = np.clip(g[CENTERS > 10000], -2.0, 1.5)
    g = np.clip(g, -3.0, 3.0)
    rep["eq_db"] = {str(int(c)): round(float(v), 2) for c, v in zip(CENTERS, g) if abs(v) >= 0.2}
    if np.abs(g).max() < 0.2: return x
    fg = np.geomspace(20.0, sr / 2 * 0.999, 400)
    gd = np.interp(np.log(fg), np.log(CENTERS), g)
    freqs = np.concatenate([[0.0], fg, [sr / 2]]); gains = 10 ** (np.concatenate([[gd[0]], gd, [gd[-1]]]) / 20)
    h = sg.firwin2(4095, freqs, gains, fs=sr)
    return np.stack([sg.oaconvolve(c, h, mode="same") for c in x])

def _lr4(sr, fc, kind): return sg.butter(2, fc, btype=kind, fs=sr, output="sos")
def _lp4(x, sr, fc): return sg.sosfilt(_lr4(sr, fc, "low"), sg.sosfilt(_lr4(sr, fc, "low"), x, axis=-1), axis=-1)
def _hp4(x, sr, fc): return sg.sosfilt(_lr4(sr, fc, "high"), sg.sosfilt(_lr4(sr, fc, "high"), x, axis=-1), axis=-1)

def split3(x, sr, f1=140.0, f2=3500.0):
    low, rest = _lp4(x, sr, f1), _hp4(x, sr, f1)
    mid, high = _lp4(rest, sr, f2), _hp4(rest, sr, f2)
    low = _lp4(low, sr, f2) + _hp4(low, sr, f2)
    return low, mid, high

def stage_multiband(x, sr, amount, rep):
    if amount <= 0: return x
    bands = split3(x, sr)
    cfg = [("low",  2.5, 2.0, 6.0, 0.030, 0.150, 30.0, 95),
           ("mid",  2.0, 1.8, 6.0, 0.020, 0.120, 15.0, 95),
           ("high", 2.5, 2.5, 6.0, 0.005, 0.080, 8.0, 97)]
    out, st = np.zeros_like(x), {}
    for (name, mg, r, kn, a, rl, rms, pc), b in zip(cfg, bands):
        gain, g = _compress(b, b, sr, mg * amount, r, kn, a, rl, rms, pc)
        out += b if gain is None else b * gain
        st[name] = {"avg_gr_db": round(float(g.mean()), 2), "max_gr_db": round(float(g.max()), 2)}
    rep["multiband"] = st
    return out

def stage_glue(x, sr, amount, rep):
    if amount <= 0: return x
    det = sg.sosfilt(sg.butter(2, 120.0, btype="high", fs=sr, output="sos"), x, axis=-1)
    gain, g = _compress(x, det, sr, 1.5 * amount, 2.0, 6.0, 0.030, 0.150, 20.0, 90, dual_release=True)
    rep["glue"] = {"avg_gr_db": round(float(g.mean()), 2), "max_gr_db": round(float(g.max()), 2)}
    return x if gain is None else x * gain

def stage_saturate(x, sr, amount, rep):
    if amount <= 0: return x
    d = 1.0 + 2.0 * amount
    up = sg.resample_poly(x, 2, 1, axis=-1)
    sat = np.tanh(d * up) / d
    y = sg.resample_poly((1 - amount) * up + amount * sat, 1, 2, axis=-1)[:, :x.shape[1]]
    rep["saturation"] = {"drive": round(d, 2), "blend": round(amount, 2)}
    return y

def stage_top_trim(x, sr, amount, rep, curve=None, tol_db=0.5):
    if amount <= 0: return x
    hop = max(1, int(HOP_S * sr)); hs = hop / sr
    bands = [("2.5-5.5k", 2500.0, 5500.0, 3.1, 4.0), ("5.5-16k", 5500.0, min(16000.0, sr / 2 * 0.95), 1.9, 4.0)]
    gap, base = None, 0.0
    if curve is not None:
        gap = curve - _ltas(x, sr)
        gap = gap - np.median(gap[(CENTERS >= 100) & (CENTERS <= 8000)])
    comp = {"mid": (x[0] + x[1]) / 2, "side": (x[0] - x[1]) / 2}
    out = {k: v.copy() for k, v in comp.items()}
    st, changed, mid_peak = {}, False, {}
    for name, lo, hi, cap, spike in bands:
        cap *= amount
        if gap is not None:
            excess = -float(np.median(gap[(CENTERS >= lo) & (CENTERS <= hi)]))
            if excess <= tol_db:
                st[name] = {"skipped": f"not brighter than reference by more than {tol_db:.1f} dB ({excess:+.1f})"}
                continue
            base = min(cap, excess - tol_db)
            cap = min(cap, base + 1.0)
        sos = sg.butter(2, [lo, hi], btype="band", fs=sr, output="sos")
        for cname in ("mid", "side"):
            key = f"{cname} {name}"
            band = sg.sosfiltfilt(sos, comp[cname])
            lev = _level_db(band[None, :], hop, max(1, int(0.100 / hs)))
            if cname == "mid":
                mid_peak[name] = float(lev.max())
            elif lev.max() < mid_peak[name] - 40:
                st[key] = {"skipped": "no side energy"}
                continue
            live = lev > lev.max() - 50
            if live.sum() < 20:
                st[key] = {"skipped": "no signal"}
                continue
            thr = float(np.median(lev[live])) + spike
            gr = np.minimum(base + _static_gr(lev, thr, 2.0, 4.0), cap)
            if gr.max() < 0.1:
                st[key] = {"avg_gr_db": 0.0, "max_gr_db": 0.0, "active_pct": 0.0}
                continue
            g = _ballistics(gr, hs, 0.010, 0.150)
            gain = np.interp(np.arange(x.shape[1]), (np.arange(len(g)) + 0.5) * hop, 10 ** (-g / 20))
            out[cname] = out[cname] - band + band * gain
            changed = True
            st[key] = {"avg_gr_db": round(float(g.mean()), 2), "max_gr_db": round(float(g.max()), 2), "active_pct": round(float((g > 0.3).mean() * 100), 1)}
    rep["top_trim"] = st
    if not changed: return x
    return np.stack([out["mid"] + out["side"], out["mid"] - out["side"]])

def stage_stereo(x, sr, adapt, rep, bass_mono_hz=120.0, width_min=0.9, side_target=REF_SIDE_DB):
    M, S = (x[0] + x[1]) / 2, (x[0] - x[1]) / 2
    S = sg.sosfiltfilt(sg.butter(4, bass_mono_hz, btype="high", fs=sr, output="sos"), S)
    cur = _side_db(np.stack([M + S, M - S]), sr)
    width = 1.0
    if adapt:
        width = float(np.clip(10 ** ((side_target - cur) / 20 * 0.5), width_min, 1.25))
    rep["stereo"] = {"bass_mono_hz": bass_mono_hz, "side_db_before_width": round(cur, 1), "width": round(width, 2)}
    S = S * width
    return np.stack([M + S, M - S])

def _soft_clip(x, ceil_lin, clip_db):
    if clip_db <= 0: return x
    room = ceil_lin * (10 ** (clip_db / 20) - 1.0)
    up = sg.resample_poly(x, 2, 1, axis=-1)
    a = np.abs(up)
    a = np.where(a > ceil_lin, ceil_lin + room * np.tanh((a - ceil_lin) / room), a)
    return sg.resample_poly(np.sign(up) * a, 1, 2, axis=-1)[:, :x.shape[1]]

def _limit(x, sr, ceil_lin, rel_fast=0.050, rel_slow=0.300, lookahead_s=0.006):
    hop = 32
    pk = _hop_true_peaks(x, hop, sr=sr)
    need = np.minimum(1.0, ceil_lin / np.maximum(pk, 1e-9))
    W = max(1, int(lookahead_s * sr / hop))
    m = ndimage.minimum_filter1d(need, size=2 * W + 1, mode="nearest")
    gs = ndimage.uniform_filter1d(m, size=2 * W + 1, mode="nearest")
    hs = hop / sr
    def rel(g, tau):
        a = 1 - np.exp(-hs / tau); out = np.empty_like(g); r = 1.0
        for i, v in enumerate(g):
            r = r + (1.0 - r) * a
            if v < r: r = v
            out[i] = r
        return out
    g = 0.5 * (rel(gs, rel_fast) + rel(gs, rel_slow))
    N = x.shape[1]
    gain = np.interp(np.arange(N), (np.arange(len(g)) + 0.5) * hop, g)
    return x * gain, -20 * np.log10(np.maximum(g, 1e-6))

def stage_loudness(x, sr, target, ceil_db, clip_db, max_gr, rep, max_cut=None, plr_min=None):
    ceil_lin = 10 ** ((ceil_db - 0.15) / 20)
    L0 = lufs(x, sr)
    Gmax = float("inf") if max_cut is None else float(max_cut) + ceil_db - true_peak_db(x, sr)
    G = min(target - L0, Gmax)
    best = None
    for it in range(5):
        y = _soft_clip(x * 10 ** (G / 20), ceil_lin, clip_db)
        y, gr = _limit(y, sr, ceil_lin)
        L = lufs(y, sr)
        p95 = float(np.percentile(gr, 95))
        best = (y, L, gr, p95, G)
        if (-0.15 < L - target <= 0.03) or (L < target and G >= Gmax - 1e-6): break
        G = min(G + (target - L), Gmax)
    y, L, gr, p95, G = best
    short = 0.0
    if p95 > max_gr:
        G -= (p95 - max_gr) * 0.9
        y = _soft_clip(x * 10 ** (G / 20), ceil_lin, clip_db)
        y, gr = _limit(y, sr, ceil_lin)
        L = lufs(y, sr); p95 = float(np.percentile(gr, 95)); short = target - L
    plr_fix = 0.0
    if plr_min is not None:
        for _ in range(4):
            gap = plr_min - (true_peak_db(y, sr) - lufs(y, sr))
            if gap <= 1e-3: break
            G -= gap + 0.01; plr_fix += gap + 0.01
            y = _soft_clip(x * 10 ** (G / 20), ceil_lin, clip_db)
            y, gr = _limit(y, sr, ceil_lin)
        L = lufs(y, sr)
    pre = x * 10 ** (G / 20)
    pre_tp = true_peak_db(pre, sr)
    clip_tp = true_peak_db(_soft_clip(pre, ceil_lin, clip_db), sr)
    overs_pct = float((np.abs(pre) > ceil_lin).mean() * 100)
    tp = true_peak_db(y, sr)
    if tp > ceil_db:
        y = y * 10 ** ((ceil_db - tp - 0.01) / 20); L = lufs(y, sr)
    guard = None
    if G >= Gmax - 1e-6 and L < target - 0.15:
        guard = {"requested_lufs": round(float(target), 2), "applied_lufs": round(float(L), 2), "max_cut_db": float(max_cut)}
    rep["loudness"] = {"input_lufs": round(L0, 2), "gain_db": round(G, 2), "limiter_avg_gr_db": round(float(gr.mean()), 2),
                       "limiter_p95_gr_db": round(p95, 2), "limiter_max_gr_db": round(float(gr.max()), 2),
                       "pre_clip_peak_dbtp": round(pre_tp, 2), "after_clip_peak_dbtp": round(clip_tp, 2),
                       "clipper_share_db": round(max(pre_tp - clip_tp, 0.0), 2),
                       "samples_over_ceiling_pct": round(overs_pct, 3),
                       "shortfall_db": round(float(max(short, 0)), 2),
                       "peak_cut_db": round(max(pre_tp - ceil_db, 0.0), 2), "plr_min": plr_min, "plr_fix_db": round(plr_fix, 2),
                       "target_lufs": round(float(L if guard else target), 2), "peak_cut_guard": guard}
    return y

def qc(x, sr, target, ceil_db, in_rep):
    w = []
    L, tp = lufs(x, sr), true_peak_db(x, sr)
    if tp > ceil_db + 0.1: w.append(f"true peak {tp:.2f} dBTP above ceiling {ceil_db}")
    if abs(L - target) > 0.6: w.append(f"loudness {L:.1f} LUFS is {abs(L - target):.1f} dB from target (mix too dynamic or too quiet to reach it safely)")
    c = float(np.corrcoef(x[0], x[1])[0, 1]) if x[0].std() > 0 else 1.0
    if c < 0.3: w.append(f"low L/R correlation {c:.2f}: weak mono compatibility")
    if abs(x.mean()) > 0.005: w.append("DC offset present")
    clipped = int((np.abs(x) >= 0.9999).sum())
    if clipped: w.append(f"{clipped} samples at digital full scale")
    if in_rep.get("input_balance_warning"): w.append(in_rep["input_balance_warning"])
    return {"lufs": round(L, 2), "true_peak_dbtp": round(tp, 2), "plr_db": round(tp - L, 2), "correlation": round(c, 3),
            "crest_db": round(float(20 * np.log10(np.abs(x).max() / (np.sqrt((x ** 2).mean()) + 1e-12))), 2), "warnings": w}

def _tone_probe(x, sr, curve=None):
    m = x.mean(axis=0)
    g = (REF_MEDIAN if curve is None else curve) - _ltas(x, sr)
    g = g - np.median(g[(CENTERS >= 160) & (CENTERS <= 8000)])
    med = lambda lo, hi: float(np.median(g[(CENTERS >= lo) & (CENTERS <= hi)]))
    band = (CENTERS >= 40) & (CENTERS <= 16000)
    lo = sg.sosfilt(sg.butter(4, [30, 120], btype="band", fs=sr, output="sos"), m)
    mid = sg.sosfilt(sg.butter(4, [150, 9000], btype="band", fs=sr, output="sos"), m)
    return {"low_50_100_gap_db": round(med(50, 100), 1), "mud_200_315_gap_db": round(med(200, 315), 1),
            "vocal_hot_0.8_2.5k_db": round(-med(800, 2500), 1), "top_5_10k_gap_db": round(med(5000, 10000), 1),
            "distance_db": round(float(np.sqrt((g[band] ** 2).mean())), 2), "side_db": round(_side_db(x, sr), 1),
            "kick_to_mid_peak_db": round(float(20 * np.log10(np.abs(lo).max() / (np.abs(mid).max() + 1e-12))), 1)}

def stage_corr_guard(x, sr, floor, rep, max_db=1.2, side_min_db=-9.0):
    """Narrow the side channel only as much as needed to bring L/R correlation up to `floor`."""
    L, R = x[0].astype(np.float64), x[1].astype(np.float64)
    M, S = (L + R) / 2.0, (L - R) / 2.0
    Pm, Ps, Cms = float(np.mean(M * M)), float(np.mean(S * S)), float(np.mean(M * S))

    def corr(f):
        plr = Pm - f * f * Ps
        pl, pr = Pm + f * f * Ps + 2 * f * Cms, Pm + f * f * Ps - 2 * f * Cms
        return plr / np.sqrt(max(pl * pr, 1e-30))

    c0 = corr(1.0)
    info = {"before": round(c0, 3), "floor": floor}
    if c0 >= floor or Ps <= 0:
        info["action"] = "none (correlation already at or above the floor)"
        rep["corr_guard"] = info
        return x
    f_min = 10 ** (-max_db / 20.0)
    try:
        s0 = _side_db(x, sr)
        f_min = max(f_min, 10 ** ((side_min_db - s0) / 20.0))            # never push the side level below side_min_db
    except Exception:
        pass
    lo, hi = min(f_min, 1.0), 1.0
    if corr(lo) < floor:
        f, info["limited_by"] = lo, "max narrowing / side-level floor"
    else:
        for _ in range(30):                                              # largest f (least narrowing) that still reaches the floor
            mid = (lo + hi) / 2.0
            lo, hi = (mid, hi) if corr(mid) >= floor + 0.002 else (lo, mid)
        f = lo
    out = np.stack([M + f * S, M - f * S])
    info.update(action="narrowed", side_cut_db=round(20 * np.log10(f), 2), after=round(corr(f), 3),
                lufs_change_db=round(lufs(out, sr) - lufs(x, sr), 2))
    rep["corr_guard"] = info
    return out


def master(x, sr, preset="afrobeats", **over):
    key = _resolve_preset(preset)
    p = dict(PRESETS[key]); p.update(over)
    prof = REFERENCES.get(p.get("ref")) if p.get("ref_eq", True) else None
    curve = None if prof is None else np.asarray(prof["curve"], dtype=float)
    if prof is None:
        p["eq"] = 0.0; p["eq_floor"] = 0.0; p["adapt_width"] = False
    xc, kind = _to_cn(x)
    p["eq"] = max(float(p["eq"]), float(p.get("eq_floor", 0.0)))
    _cap = float(p["ceil"]) - float(p.get("plr_floor", 9.2))
    _guard = None
    if p["lufs"] > _cap:
        _guard = {"requested_lufs": round(float(p["lufs"]), 2), "applied_lufs": round(_cap, 2),
                  "plr_floor": float(p.get("plr_floor", 9.2))}
        p["lufs"] = _cap
    rep = {"preset": preset, "resolved_preset": key, "reference": p.get("ref") if prof else None, "params": p}
    if _guard: rep["plr_guard"] = _guard
    if not np.isfinite(xc).all(): xc = np.nan_to_num(xc)
    xc = xc - xc.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    if np.abs(xc).max() < 1e-6 or xc.shape[1] < sr:
        rep["warning"] = "input silent or shorter than 1 s: not processed"
        return _from_cn(xc, kind), rep
    rep["input"] = {"lufs": round(lufs(xc, sr), 2), "true_peak_dbtp": round(true_peak_db(xc, sr), 2)}
    if curve is not None:
        try: rep["tone_in"] = _tone_probe(xc, sr, curve)
        except Exception as _e: rep["tone_in_error"] = str(_e)[:80]
        cur = _ltas(xc, sr)
        vb = (CENTERS >= 800) & (CENTERS <= 2500)
        vgap = float(np.median((cur - curve)[vb]) - np.median((cur - curve)[(CENTERS >= 100) & (CENTERS <= 8000)]))
        if vgap > 3.0:
            rep["input_balance_warning"] = (f"0.8-2.5 kHz is {vgap:.1f} dB hotter than {p.get('ref')} reference masters: "
                                            "vocal is probably too loud against the beat, fix in the mix not the master")
    y = stage_eq(xc, sr, p["eq"], rep, curve)
    y = stage_multiband(y, sr, p["mb"], rep)
    y = stage_glue(y, sr, p["glue"], rep)
    y = stage_saturate(y, sr, p["sat"], rep)
    y = stage_top_trim(y, sr, float(p.get("top_trim", 0.0)), rep, curve if curve is not None else REF_MEDIAN, 0.5 if curve is not None else 3.0)
    y = stage_stereo(y, sr, p["adapt_width"], rep, width_min=float(p.get("width_min", 0.9)), side_target=float(prof["side_db"]) if prof else REF_SIDE_DB)
    y = stage_loudness(y, sr, p["lufs"], p["ceil"], p["clip"], p["max_gr"], rep, max_cut=p.get("max_cut"), plr_min=float(p.get("plr_floor", 9.2)) - 0.05)
    if prof is not None and float(p.get("corr_floor", 0.865)) > 0:      # reference-genre only; adaptive, usually a no-op
        y = stage_corr_guard(y, sr, float(p.get("corr_floor", 0.865)), rep)
        _tp = true_peak_db(y, sr)
        if _tp > p["ceil"]:                                              # narrowing can move peaks by a hair: keep the ceiling
            y = y * 10 ** ((p["ceil"] - _tp - 0.01) / 20)
    rep["output"] = qc(y, sr, rep["loudness"].get("target_lufs", p["lufs"]), p["ceil"], rep)
    g = rep["loudness"].get("peak_cut_guard")
    if g:
        rep["output"]["notes"] = [f"peak-cut guard: target lowered {g['requested_lufs']} -> {g['applied_lufs']} LUFS (this mix needs more than {g['max_cut_db']} dB of peak removal to reach it)"]
    if curve is not None:
        try: rep["tone_out"] = _tone_probe(y, sr, curve)
        except Exception as _e: rep["tone_out_error"] = str(_e)[:80]
    return _from_cn(y, kind), rep
