==============================================================================

"""wratty-fx mixer: sums stems, glue bus, LUFS master. Replaces RoEx."""
import gc, json, os, shutil, tempfile, threading, uuid
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from urllib.parse import urlparse, parse_qs

import numpy as np
import requests
from pedalboard import Pedalboard, HighpassFilter, PeakFilter, Compressor
from limiter import Limiter
try:
    from pedalboard import BrickwallLimiter
except Exception:  # pedalboard < 0.9.25
    BrickwallLimiter = None
from pedalboard.io import AudioFile

import auto
import mix_balance

def _dyn9010(x, sr, win=0.4):
    x = np.asarray(x)
    if x.ndim > 1:
        x = x.mean(axis=0 if x.shape[0] <= x.shape[1] else 1)
    x = x.reshape(-1).astype(np.float64)
    """Macro-dynamics: 90th-10th percentile of 0.4s RMS (dB).
    Same metric compare.py prints, so the log is directly comparable."""
    x = np.asarray(x)
    if x.ndim > 1:                    # (ch, samples) or (samples, ch) -> mono
        ax = 0 if x.shape[0] <= x.shape[1] else 1
        x = x.mean(axis=ax)
    x = x.reshape(-1).astype(np.float64)
    w = max(1, int(win * sr))
    n = len(x) // w
    if n < 5:
        return None
    seg = x[:n * w].reshape(n, w)
    r = 20.0 * np.log10(np.sqrt((seg ** 2).mean(axis=1)) + 1e-12)
    r = r[r > -60.0]
    if len(r) < 5:
        return None
    return float(np.percentile(r, 90) - np.percentile(r, 10))

try:
    import pyloudnorm as pyln
except Exception:
    pyln = None

TARGET = {"LOW": -16.0, "MEDIUM": -14.0, "HIGH": -11.5}
JOBS, LK = {}, threading.Lock()



def rmsdb(x):
    m = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(x, dtype=np.float64))))
    return 20.0 * np.log10(m + 1e-12)


def peakdb(x):
    return 20.0 * np.log10(float(np.max(np.abs(x))) + 1e-12)


def lufs(x, sr):
    """Real gated K-weighted loudness (BS.1770-4), from the engine."""
    try:
        return auto.integrated_lufs(x, sr)
    except Exception:
        return None


def load(p, max_sec=0.0):
    with AudioFile(p) as f:
        n = int(f.frames)
        if max_sec:
            cap = max(1, int(float(max_sec) * float(f.samplerate)))
            if cap < n:
                n = cap
        return f.read(n), int(f.samplerate)


def save16(p, a, sr):
    a = np.ascontiguousarray(a, dtype=np.float32)
    np.clip(a, -1.0, 1.0, out=a)
    rng = np.random.default_rng()
    flat = a.reshape(-1)
    for i in range(0, flat.size, 1 << 20):
        part = flat[i:i + (1 << 20)]
        noise = rng.random(part.size, dtype=np.float32)
        noise -= rng.random(part.size, dtype=np.float32)
        noise *= np.float32(1.0 / 32768.0)
        part += noise
        np.clip(part, -1.0, 1.0, out=part)
    with AudioFile(p, "w", int(sr), a.shape[0]) as f:
        try:
            f.bit_depth = 16
        except Exception:
            pass
        f.write(a)


from math import gcd
from scipy.signal import resample_poly

def to_sr(a, si, so):
    if si == so:
        return a
    g = gcd(int(si), int(so))
    y = resample_poly(a.astype(np.float32), int(so) // g, int(si) // g, axis=1, window=("kaiser", 8.6))
    return np.ascontiguousarray(y, dtype=np.float32)


def _fx_ticket(scope="read", ttl=600):
    import hashlib
    import hmac
    import os
    import time

    key = os.environ.get("FX_INTERNAL_SECRET", "")
    if not key:
        return ""
    exp = int(time.time()) + ttl
    sig = hmac.new(
        key.encode(), f"{scope}|{exp}".encode(), hashlib.sha256
    ).hexdigest()
    return f"{exp}.{sig}"


def _reject_private(url):
    """The submitted host is checked in the API, but a redirect can land
    anywhere. Re-check the FINAL url after requests has followed any
    redirects, so a public host cannot bounce us at the metadata endpoint."""
    import ipaddress
    import socket
    from urllib.parse import urlparse

    host = (urlparse(url).hostname or "").lower()
    if not host or host == "localhost" or host.endswith((".local", ".internal")):
        raise RuntimeError("refusing to fetch a private host")
    try:
        infos = socket.getaddrinfo(host, None)
    except Exception:
        raise RuntimeError("could not resolve stem host")
    for info in infos:
        try:
            ip = ipaddress.ip_address(info[4][0])
        except ValueError:
            continue
        if (ip.is_private or ip.is_loopback or ip.is_link_local
                or ip.is_reserved or ip.is_multicast or ip.is_unspecified):
            raise RuntimeError("refusing to fetch a private address")

def grab(u, dst):
    tk = _fx_ticket("read")
    headers = {"x-fx-ticket": tk} if tk else {}
    if not tk:
        raise RuntimeError("FX_INTERNAL_SECRET is missing in the mix worker")
    with requests.get(u, headers=headers, stream=True, timeout=120) as r:
        r.raise_for_status()
        _reject_private(r.url)
        with open(dst, "wb") as fh:
            for c in r.iter_content(1 << 16):
                fh.write(c)


_ENCODE_ERR = None


def encode_all(src, tmp, is_preview):
    """Previews: MP3 only. Full renders: MP3 AND FLAC, both encoded from the
    SAME decoded audio - the master is never rendered twice."""
    global _ENCODE_ERR
    try:
        import soundfile as sf
        data, sr = sf.read(src, dtype="float32", always_2d=True)
        out = []
        mp3 = os.path.join(tmp, "mix.mp3")
        sf.write(mp3, data, sr, format="MP3", subtype="MPEG_LAYER_III", bitrate_mode="CONSTANT", compression_level=0.0)
        out.append((mp3, "audio/mpeg", ".mp3"))
        if not is_preview:
            flac = os.path.join(tmp, "mix.flac")
            sf.write(flac, data, sr, format="FLAC", subtype="PCM_16",
                     compression_level=0.5)
            out.append((flac, "audio/flac", ".flac"))
        return out, None
    except Exception as e:
        _ENCODE_ERR = str(e)[:200]
        return [(src, "audio/wav", ".wav")], _ENCODE_ERR


def encode_out(src, tmp, is_preview):
    """MP3 for previews, FLAC for full renders. Falls back to the WAV."""
    global _ENCODE_ERR
    try:
        import soundfile as sf
        data, sr = sf.read(src, dtype="float32", always_2d=True)
        if is_preview:
            dst = os.path.join(tmp, "mix.mp3")
            sf.write(dst, data, sr, format="MP3", subtype="MPEG_LAYER_III", bitrate_mode="CONSTANT", compression_level=0.0)
            return dst, "audio/mpeg", ".mp3", None
        dst = os.path.join(tmp, "mix.flac")
        sf.write(dst, data, sr, format="FLAC", subtype="PCM_16", compression_level=0.5)
        return dst, "audio/flac", ".flac", None
    except Exception as e:
        _ENCODE_ERR = str(e)[:200]
        return src, "audio/wav", ".wav", _ENCODE_ERR


def put(p, name, ctype="audio/wav"):
    # Modal volume ONLY. The Vercel Blob fallback is gone: it needed
    # BLOB_READ_WRITE_TOKEN, which no longer exists, so a volume hiccup used
    # to surface as a bogus "token missing" and kill the render. Fail loudly.
    import blobstore
    url = blobstore.put(p, name)
    print("PUT modal %s" % url, flush=True)
    return url


def rss_mb():
    try:
        with open("/proc/self/status") as fh:
            for line in fh:
                if line.startswith("VmRSS:"):
                    return round(int(line.split()[1]) / 1024.0, 1)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def setjob(jid, status, url=""):
    with LK:
        j = JOBS.get(jid) or {}
        j["status"] = status
        j["rss_mb"] = rss_mb()
        if url:
            j["url"] = url
        JOBS[jid] = j
        _st, _rss = j.get("status"), j.get("rss_mb")
    print("[job %s] %-18s rss=%s MB" % (jid[:8], _st, _rss), flush=True)



# ---- role panning. PAN_ENABLED=False fully reverts. ----
PAN_ENABLED = True
PAN_HZ = 200.0
PAN_VALUES = {
    "backing": (-0.55, 0.55, -0.35, 0.35),
    "adlib":   (-0.70, 0.70, -0.50, 0.50),
    "response": (-0.90, 0.90),
    "intro":   (0.0,),
    "outro":   (0.0,),
}
_PAN_N = {"backing": 0, "adlib": 0, "response": 0, "intro": 0, "outro": 0}
_PAN_REPORT = {"backing": [], "adlib": [], "response": []}

def do_mix(stems, loud, jid, max_sec=0, preset="neutral"):
    # Reset per-render state so a previous job in this warm container
    # cannot leak plate/delay/reverb data into this render's report or audio.
    try:
        auto._PLATE.clear(); auto._PLATE.update({"ir": {}, "err": "", "kind": ""})
        auto._HALL.clear(); auto._HALL.update({"ir": {}})
        auto._DBL.clear(); auto._DBL.update({"kind": ""})
        auto._REVERB_LAST.clear()
        auto._DELAY_LAST.clear()
        auto._PAR_LAST.clear()
        auto._EXC_LAST.clear()
        auto._FAST_LOUDNESS_CACHE.clear()
    except Exception:
        pass
    tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    try:
        cfg = auto.apply_preset(preset)
        try:
            try:
                import qc as _qc
            except ImportError:
                from . import qc as _qc
        except Exception:
            _qc = None
        sr = None
        groups = {"beat": [], "lead": [], "adlib": [], "backing": [], "response": [], "intro": [], "outro": [], "feature": [], "other": []}
        _qc_in = {}
        for i, s in enumerate(stems):
            setjob(jid, "stem %d of %d" % (i + 1, len(stems)))
            p = os.path.join(tmp, "%d.wav" % i)
            grab(s["url"], p)
            a, s0 = load(p)
            os.remove(p)
            if len(a[0]) / float(s0) > 480:
                raise ValueError("stem longer than 8 minutes")
            if sr is None:
                sr = s0
            elif s0 != sr:
                a = to_sr(a, s0, sr)
            if max_sec:
                a = a[:, : max(1, int(max_sec * sr))]
            if a.shape[0] == 1:
                a = np.repeat(a, 2, axis=0)
            a = np.ascontiguousarray(a[:2].astype(np.float32))
            role = s.get("role") or s.get("name") or "stem"
            if _qc is not None:
                try:
                    _qc_in[str(i)] = _qc.stem_qc(a, sr, role)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            if i == 0:
                _PAN_N.update({"backing": 0, "adlib": 0, "response": 0})
                _PAN_REPORT.update({"backing": [], "adlib": [], "response": []})
                _PAN_NB = sum(1 for _s in stems if auto.bucket(_s.get("role") or _s.get("name") or "stem") == "backing")
            bucket = auto.bucket(role)
            if PAN_ENABLED and bucket in PAN_VALUES:
                k = _PAN_N[bucket]
                pan = PAN_VALUES[bucket][k % len(PAN_VALUES[bucket])]
                _PAN_N[bucket] += 1
                _hi = Pedalboard([HighpassFilter(
                    cutoff_frequency_hz=PAN_HZ)])(a, sr).astype(np.float32)
                _lo = (a - _hi).astype(np.float32)
                _hi[0] *= float(np.sqrt(1.0 - 0.8 * pan))
                _hi[1] *= float(np.sqrt(1.0 + 0.8 * pan))
                a = np.ascontiguousarray((_lo + _hi).astype(np.float32))
                _PAN_REPORT[bucket].append({"stem": i, "pan": pan})
                if bucket == "backing":
                    try:
                        _wm = (a[0] + a[1]) * 0.5
                        _ws = (a[0] - a[1]) * 0.5 * 1.25
                        a = np.stack([_wm + _ws, _wm - _ws]).astype(np.float32)
                    except Exception:
                        pass
            groups[bucket].append(a)

        setjob(jid, "analysing")
        
        
        mixed, report = auto.mix(groups, sr)
        

        # Trim trailing silence — end the render where the audio actually ends
        try:
            _orig_len = mixed.shape[1]
            _m = mixed.mean(axis=0) if mixed.ndim > 1 else mixed
            _win = max(1, int(sr * 0.05))
            _nf = len(_m) // _win
            if _nf > 4:
                _env = _m[:_nf * _win].reshape(_nf, _win).max(axis=1)
                _thr = float(_env.max()) * 0.005
                _above = np.where(_env > _thr)[0]
                if _above.size > 0:
                    _last = int((_above[-1] + 1) * _win) + int(sr * 0.5)
                    _last = min(_last, _orig_len)
                    if _last < _orig_len - sr:
                        mixed = mixed[:, :_last]
                        report["trimmed_tail_s"] = round((_orig_len - _last) / float(sr), 2)
        except Exception as _e:
            report["trim_error"] = str(_e)[:120]
        report["input_qc"] = _qc_in

        report["role_pan"] = {"on": PAN_ENABLED, "hz": PAN_HZ,
                             "backing": _PAN_REPORT["backing"],
                             "adlib": _PAN_REPORT["adlib"]}
        mode = report.get("mode")
        n = mixed.shape[1]
        want = str(loud or "MEDIUM").upper()

        # Low-mid stereo narrowing. Against two commercial references our
        # side energy was 6-8 dB high at 80-600 Hz while 0-80 Hz and
        # 1.5-4 kHz already matched. Halving the side below ~600 Hz puts the
        # kick/bass body back on centre and restores mono compatibility.
        # Skipped on master: its input is already a narrowed mix.
        if mode not in ("master", "passthrough") and mixed.shape[0] >= 2:
            _mid = (mixed[0] + mixed[1]) * 0.5
            _side = (mixed[0] - mixed[1]) * 0.5
            _hi = Pedalboard([HighpassFilter(cutoff_frequency_hz=600.0)])(
                _side[None, :], sr)[0]
            try:
                from pedalboard import LowShelfFilter as _LSF
                _bal = mix_balance.plan(mixed, sr)
                report["mix_balance"] = _bal
                _mid = np.asarray(Pedalboard([_LSF(cutoff_frequency_hz=80.0, gain_db=_bal["shelf80_db"]),
                                              PeakFilter(cutoff_frequency_hz=125.0, gain_db=_bal["bell125_db"], q=1.2)])(
                    _mid[None, :], sr)[0], dtype=np.float32)
                report["low_mid_shelf_db"] = _bal["shelf80_db"]
                report["body_125_db"] = _bal["bell125_db"]
            except Exception as _e:
                report["low_mid_mid_shelf_error"] = str(_e)[:120]
            _side = (_hi + 0.63 * (_side - _hi)).astype(np.float32)
            # 5.5-9 kHz side trim. Side energy measured ~9 dB high vs
            # Boi Chase / Omalicha / Pain (they sit -14 to -16; we were -6.0).
            # Same M/S method as the low-mid guard above, band-limited via
            # two highpasses (no LowpassFilter imported).
            try:
                # Exact FFT band trim. HP5500-HP9000 is not a flat bandpass
                # (effective gain ~0.28, and it leaked into 1.5-4 kHz), so the
                # original split could never exceed -2.9 dB. Brickwall instead.
                _SIDE_TRIM = 1.0   # 1.0 = off | 0.70 = -3.1 | 0.45 = -6.9 dB
                _n = _side.shape[-1]
                _F = np.fft.rfft(_side.astype(np.float64))
                _f = np.fft.rfftfreq(_n, 1.0 / sr)
                _m = (_f >= 5500.0) & (_f < 16000.0)
                _F[_m] *= _SIDE_TRIM
                _m2 = (_f >= 2500.0) & (_f < 5500.0)
                _F[_m2] *= 1.0
                _side = np.fft.irfft(_F, _n).astype(np.float32)
                report["top_side_trim_db"] = round(20.0 * np.log10(_SIDE_TRIM), 2)
                report["top_side_band_hz"] = [5500.0, 16000.0]
            except Exception as _e:
                report["top_side_trim_error"] = str(_e)[:120]
            mixed = np.stack([_mid + _side, _mid - _side]).astype(np.float32)
            report["low_mid_guard_hz"] = 600.0
            report["low_mid_side_trim_db"] = -4.0

        if mode != "passthrough":
            mixed = mixed * (10.0 ** ((-6.0 - peakdb(mixed)) / 20.0))
            setjob(jid, "glue bus")
            if mode in ("two_track", "master"):
                # input is already a finished mix - skip the compressor. The guard below
                # is only for an arbitrary two_track upload, not for a master.
                #
                # A "master" is our own mix fed back in and already carries the
                # 30 Hz guard from the mix pass - filtering it again costs ~0.7 dB
                # of 30-60 Hz for nothing. An arbitrary two_track upload still gets it.
                if mode != "master":
                    mixed = Pedalboard([HighpassFilter(cutoff_frequency_hz=30)])(mixed, sr).astype(np.float32)
                report["glue_thr_db"] = "off (%s)" % mode
            else:
                _gr = float(cfg.get("glue_gr_db", 0.25))
                _rat = float(cfg.get("glue_ratio", 1.25))
                _over = _gr * _rat / max(0.1, _rat - 1.0)
                _thr = float(np.clip(rmsdb(mixed) - _over, -40.0, -6.0))
                mixed = Pedalboard([HighpassFilter(cutoff_frequency_hz=30),
                                    Compressor(threshold_db=_thr, ratio=_rat,
                                               attack_ms=50.0, release_ms=130.0)])(mixed, sr).astype(np.float32)
                report["glue_thr_db"] = round(_thr, 2)

        # Master target follows the beat's own loudness (audit #14):
        # forcing a finished -8.5 LUFS beat down to a fixed -14 makes the
        # mix quieter than the beat the user bought. Target = clip(beat, -11.5, -8.5)
        try:
            _beat_lufs = None
            if mode == "two_track":
                # find beat part in the arrived stems via the report keys
                _arr = report.get("arrived", {})
                _b = _arr.get("beat") or {}
                _brms = _b.get("rms_db")
                if _brms is not None:
                    # rough LUFS estimate from RMS (beat is premastered, ~3 dB RMS->LUFS offset)
                    _beat_lufs = float(_brms) - 3.0
            if _beat_lufs is not None and -16.0 < _beat_lufs < -4.0:
                tgt = float(np.clip(_beat_lufs, -14.0, -9.9))
                report["master_target_from_beat_lufs"] = round(tgt, 2)
            else:
                tgt = TARGET.get(want)
                if want == "HIGH" and mode == "master" and cfg.get("target_lufs") is not None:
                    tgt = max(tgt, float(cfg["target_lufs"]))   # a preset may make HIGH louder, never quieter
                    report["target_from_preset"] = tgt
                if tgt is None:
                    tgt = float(cfg.get("target_lufs", -14.0))
        except Exception as _te:
            report["master_target_error"] = str(_te)[:120]
            tgt = TARGET.get(want)
            if tgt is None:
                tgt = float(cfg.get("target_lufs", -14.0))
        # 250-500 Hz sits forward on every render. Cut it whether
        # we're in mix mode or master mode.
        _mcs = 0.0 if mode == "master" else 1.0
        if mode != "master" and "_bal" not in locals():
            _bal = mix_balance.plan(mixed, sr)
            report["mix_balance"] = _bal
        _mc = _bal["cut250_db"] * _mcs if mode != "master" else 0.0
        mixed = Pedalboard([
            PeakFilter(cutoff_frequency_hz=250.0, gain_db=_mc, q=0.9),
            PeakFilter(cutoff_frequency_hz=420.0, gain_db=_bal["cut420_db"] * _mcs if mode != "master" else 0.0, q=1.0),
            PeakFilter(cutoff_frequency_hz=550.0, gain_db=_bal["cut550_db"] * _mcs if mode != "master" else 0.0, q=1.0),
        ])(mixed, sr).astype(np.float32)
        report["mid_cut_db"] = _mc
        report["mid_cut_hz"] = 250.0
        if mode not in ("master", "passthrough"):
            try:
                import wratty_fix_v2
                mixed, _tg = wratty_fix_v2.master_tone_correct(mixed, sr)
                report["tone_guard"] = _tg
            except Exception as _e:
                report["tone_guard_error"] = str(_e)[:120]

        try:
            _in_peak = peakdb(mixed)
            _in_clip = int(np.count_nonzero(np.abs(mixed) >= 0.999))
            _would_reduce = 0.0
            if _in_peak > -1.0:
                _would_reduce = -1.0 - _in_peak
            if _in_clip > 0 and _would_reduce > -0.5:
                _would_reduce = -0.5
            report["precheck_probe"] = {"peak_db_in": round(_in_peak, 2),
                                        "clipped_in": _in_clip,
                                        "would_reduce_db": round(_would_reduce, 2),
                                        "would_fire": bool(_would_reduce < -0.05)}
        except Exception as _pce:
            report["precheck_probe_error"] = str(_pce)[:120]
        if mode == "master":
            tgt = tgt + float(cfg.get("master_lift_db", 2.5))
        else:
            tgt = tgt - float(cfg.get("mix_headroom_db", 2.5))
        cur = lufs(mixed, sr)
        if cur is None:
            mixed = mixed * (10.0 ** ((tgt - rmsdb(mixed)) / 20.0))
        else:
            mixed = mixed * (10.0 ** (max(-9.0, min(9.0, tgt - cur)) / 20.0))
        # Pre-limiter soft clip: rounds peaks so limiter does less work.
        # pre-clip disabled: testing ProductionMasteringLimiter alone

        # Pre-crest guard: if input is already dense (low crest), back
        # off the loudness target so the limiter doesn't crush dynamics.
        # Can be disabled per-preset for exact LUFS targets.
        if mode == "master" and cfg.get("crest_pre_check", True):
            try:
                _crest_in = peakdb(mixed) - rmsdb(mixed)
                if _crest_in < 11.0:
                    tgt = tgt - 1.5
                    report["crest_pre_check"] = {"input_crest_db": round(_crest_in, 2), "target_reduced_db": -1.5}
                elif _crest_in < 13.0:
                    tgt = tgt - 0.5
                    report["crest_pre_check"] = {"input_crest_db": round(_crest_in, 2), "target_reduced_db": -0.5}
            except Exception as _cpe:
                report["crest_pre_check_error"] = str(_cpe)[:120]
        # ---- master_bus: flag-gated; any failure falls through to the legacy gain/clip/limit below ----
        _mb_done = False
        _mb_tp = 0.0
        if auto._NEW.get("master_bus", False) and mode == "master":
            try:
                import master_bus as _mbus
                _is_master = (mode == "master")
                _y, _mrep = _mbus.master(
                    mixed, sr, preset="afrobeats",
                    lufs=float(tgt), ceil=float(auto.CEILING_DB),
                    eq=0.0 if _is_master else 0.5,
                    mb=0.4 if _is_master else 1.0,
                    glue=0.5 if _is_master else 1.0,
                    sat=0.0 if _is_master else 0.25,
                    adapt_width=not _is_master,
                    clip=1.0 if _is_master else (2.0 if cfg.get("soft_clip") else 1.5))
                _y = np.asarray(_y, dtype=np.float32)
                if _y.ndim == 2 and mixed.ndim == 2 and mixed.shape[0] == 1 and _y.shape[0] == 2:
                    _y = _y.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True).astype(np.float32)
                _out = _mrep.get("output", {})
                _chk = {"shape": bool(_y.shape == mixed.shape), "finite": bool(np.isfinite(_y).all()),
                        "tp": bool(_out.get("true_peak_dbtp", 99.0) <= float(auto.CEILING_DB) + 0.3),
                        "lufs": bool(abs(float(_out.get("lufs", -99.0)) - float(tgt)) <= 1.5)}
                if all(_chk.values()):
                    mixed = _y
                    _mb_tp = float(_out["true_peak_dbtp"])
                    _mb_done = True
                    report["master_bus"] = json.loads(json.dumps(_mrep, default=float))
                else:
                    report["master_bus_rejected"] = _chk
            except Exception as _mbe:
                report["master_bus_error"] = str(_mbe)[:200]
        report["master_bus_used"] = _mb_done
        setjob(jid, "clip+limit")
        # ORDER MATTERS FOR DYNAMICS (chasing the FOLA reference):
        # set the gain FIRST, then clip, then limit ONCE. The old order
        # clipped, limited, then re-limited after every gain step - each
        # re-limit shaved crest (LRA 2.8 vs FOLA 4.1 at the same true peak).
        for _ in range(0 if _mb_done else 3):
            f = lufs(mixed, sr)
            if f is None or abs(tgt - f) < 0.15:
                break
            _step = max(-9.0, min(9.0, tgt - f))
            mixed = mixed * (10.0 ** (_step / 20.0))
        pass
        # Clipper OFF by default: shaving peaks costs kick transients and
        # adds edge. Only presets that explicitly ask for it keep it.
        _clip_on = bool(cfg.get("clip")) and mode not in ("passthrough", "master") and not _mb_done
        _thr = 10.0 ** (-1.0 / 20.0)
        _pre = peakdb(mixed)
        _hits = int(np.count_nonzero(np.abs(mixed) > _thr))
        if _clip_on:
            mixed = auto.clip(mixed, sr)
        elif cfg.get("soft_clip") and mode not in ("passthrough", "master") and not _mb_done:
            # Master uses knee 0.75 (threshold -2.5 dBFS): shaves transients
            # only, leaves bass fundamentals (below -2.5 dBFS) untouched.
            mixed = auto.soft_clip(mixed, sr,
                knee=float(cfg.get("master_clip_knee", 0.75)) if mode == "master" else 0.70)
            report["soft_clip"] = True
        report["clip_diag"] = {"enabled": _clip_on,
                               "pre_peak_dbfs": round(_pre, 2),
                               "post_peak_dbfs": round(peakdb(mixed), 2),
                               "samples_over_threshold": _hits}
        _dyn_pre = _dyn9010(mixed, sr)
        _lufs_before_limit = lufs(mixed, sr)
        _peak_before_limit = peakdb(mixed)
        # ---- Iterative feedback loudness targeting ----
        # Mix stage: no limiter, LUFS normalize only. Kick preserved.
        # Master stage: limiter with iterative feedback for -1 dBTP.
        if mode != "master" or _mb_done:
            for _it in range(0 if _mb_done else 6):
                _measured = lufs(mixed, sr)
                if _measured is None or abs(tgt - _measured) < 0.08:
                    break
                mixed = mixed * (10.0 ** ((tgt - _measured) / 20.0))
            try:
                tp = float(20 * np.log10(np.max(np.abs(mixed)) + 1e-12))
            except Exception:
                tp = 0.0
            brick = bool(_mb_done)
            report["mode_note"] = ("master_bus did loudness + limiting" if _mb_done
                                   else "mix stage: no limiter, LUFS only")
        else:
            _pre_limit_mix = mixed.copy()
            _current_gain = 1.0
            mixed, tp, brick = auto.limit(_pre_limit_mix, sr, auto.CEILING_DB)
            for _it in range(5):
                _measured = lufs(mixed, sr)
                if _measured is None:
                    break
                _error = tgt - _measured
                report["limiter_iter_%d_error" % _it] = round(_error, 3)
                if abs(_error) < 0.08:
                    break
                _current_gain *= 10.0 ** (_error / 20.0)
                mixed, tp, brick = auto.limit(_pre_limit_mix * _current_gain, sr, auto.CEILING_DB)
            report["limiter_final_gain"] = round(_current_gain, 3)
        _fin = lufs(mixed, sr)
        _dyn_post = _dyn9010(mixed, sr)
        try:
            _gr_lufs = None if (_lufs_before_limit is None or _fin is None) else round(_lufs_before_limit - _fin, 2)
            _gr_peak = round(_peak_before_limit - peakdb(mixed), 2)
            report["limiter_probe"] = {"lufs_before": _lufs_before_limit,
                                       "lufs_after": _fin,
                                       "peak_before": round(_peak_before_limit, 2),
                                       "peak_after": round(peakdb(mixed), 2),
                                       "gr_lufs": _gr_lufs,
                                       "gr_peak": _gr_peak}
        except Exception as _le:
            report["limiter_probe_error"] = str(_le)[:120]
        report["dyn_pre_limit"] = None if _dyn_pre is None else round(_dyn_pre, 2)
        report["dyn_post_limit"] = None if _dyn_post is None else round(_dyn_post, 2)
        report["loudness_error_db"] = None if _fin is None else round(tgt - _fin, 2)
        report["true_peak_dbfs"] = round(tp, 2)
        report["limiter"] = "brickwall" if brick else "fallback"

        _tp0 = auto._true_peak_db(mixed)
        if _tp0 > auto.CEILING_DB:
            mixed = (mixed * 10.0 ** ((auto.CEILING_DB - _tp0) / 20.0)).astype(np.float32)
            report["safety_trim_db"] = round(auto.CEILING_DB - _tp0, 2)
        # Upload Raw Sum for A/B Preview
        try:
            if os.path.exists("/tmp/raw_mix.wav"):
                _raw_stem = uuid.uuid4().hex
                raw_url = put("/tmp/raw_mix.wav", "fx/%s-raw.wav" % _raw_stem, "audio/wav")
                report["raw_url"] = raw_url
                os.remove("/tmp/raw_mix.wav")
        except Exception as _rwe:
            report["raw_url_error"] = str(_rwe)[:150]
        # ---------------------------------------

        out = os.path.join(tmp, "mix.wav")
        save16(out, mixed, sr)

        # --- GENERATE A/B PREVIEW (MP3) ---
        try:
            _raw_mix = np.zeros((2, 0), dtype=np.float32)
            for arrs in groups.values():
                for a in arrs:
                    if a is None: continue
                    if a.ndim == 1: a = np.stack([a, a])
                    if _raw_mix.shape[1] < a.shape[1]:
                        _raw_mix = np.pad(_raw_mix, ((0,0),(0,a.shape[1]-_raw_mix.shape[1])))
                    elif a.shape[1] < _raw_mix.shape[1]:
                        a = np.pad(a, ((0,0),(0,_raw_mix.shape[1]-a.shape[1])))
                    _raw_mix += a

            if _raw_mix.shape[1] > 0:
                import ab_preview
                _ab = ab_preview.make_ab_preview(_raw_mix, mixed, sr, bpm=report.get("bpm"))

                _raw_mp3 = os.path.join(tmp, "raw-preview.mp3")
                _mix_mp3 = os.path.join(tmp, "mixed-preview.mp3")

                ab_preview.write_mp3(_raw_mp3, _ab["raw"], sr)
                ab_preview.write_mp3(_mix_mp3, _ab["mixed"], sr)

                _raw_stem = uuid.uuid4().hex
                _mix_stem = uuid.uuid4().hex

                report["raw_url"] = put(_raw_mp3, "fx/%s-raw.mp3" % _raw_stem, "audio/mpeg")
                report["ab_preview_info"] = _ab["info"]
        except Exception as _abe:
            print("A/B preview error:", _abe)
        # -----------------------------------
        del mixed
        gc.collect()
        rel, rel_sr = load(out)
        secs = rel.shape[1] / float(rel_sr)
        # True-peak recheck on decoded 16-bit output; if post-quantization
        # TP exceeds ceiling, attenuate and re-save before encoding.
        try:
            _tp_now = auto._true_peak_db(rel)
            if _tp_now > auto.CEILING_DB + 0.05:
                _delta = auto.CEILING_DB - _tp_now - 0.1
                rel = (rel * (10.0 ** (_delta / 20.0))).astype(np.float32)
                save16(out, rel, rel_sr)
                report["tp_recheck"] = {"measured_db": round(_tp_now, 2), "adjusted_db": round(_delta, 2)}
        except Exception as _tpe:
            report["tp_recheck_error"] = str(_tpe)[:120]
        files, enc_err = encode_all(out, tmp, secs <= 65.0)
        _stem = uuid.uuid4().hex
        urls = {}
        for _p, _ct, _ex in files:
            urls[_ex.lstrip(".")] = put(
                _p, "fx/%s-mix-%s%s" % (_stem, want.lower(), _ex), _ct)
        url = urls.get("flac") or urls.get("mp3") or ""
        got = lufs(rel, rel_sr)
        try:
            report["final_lufs"] = None if got is None else round(got, 2)
            report["final_true_peak_dbfs"] = round(auto._true_peak_db(rel), 2)
            if _qc is not None:
                report["final_qc"] = _qc.output_qc(rel, rel_sr, got, report["final_true_peak_dbfs"], auto.CEILING_DB)
        except Exception:
            pass
        report["loudness"] = want
        report["format"] = ",".join(sorted(urls.keys()))
        if enc_err:
            report["encode_error"] = enc_err
        def _sanitize(o):
            import numpy as _np
            if isinstance(o, dict): return {k: _sanitize(v) for k, v in o.items()}
            if isinstance(o, list): return [_sanitize(v) for v in o]
            if isinstance(o, (_np.floating, _np.integer)): return o.item()
            if isinstance(o, _np.ndarray): return o.tolist()
            return o

        return _sanitize({"url": url, "files": urls,
                "seconds": round(rel.shape[1] / float(rel_sr), 2),
                "peak_dbfs": round(peakdb(rel), 2),
                "lufs": (None if got is None else round(got, 2)),
                "sample_rate": int(rel_sr),
                "mode": mode, "report": report, "preset": preset})
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(tmp, ignore_errors=True)


def worker(jid, stems, loud, max_sec=0, preset="neutral"):
    try:
        setjob(jid, "running")
        res = do_mix(stems, loud, jid, max_sec, preset)
        with LK:
            JOBS[jid] = {"status": "done", "url": res["url"], "result": res}
    except Exception as e:
        with LK:
            JOBS[jid] = {"status": "failed", "error": str(e)[:300]}


class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"

    def json_out(self, code, payload):
        b = json.dumps(payload, default=str).encode()
        self.send_response(code)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(b)))
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(b)

    def log_message(self, *a):
        pass

    def do_GET(self):
        jid = (parse_qs(urlparse(self.path).query).get("id") or [""])[0]
        if not jid:
            return self.json_out(200, {"ok": True, "lufs": pyln is not None})
        with LK:
            j = dict(JOBS.get(jid) or {"status": "failed", "error": "Job not found (container restarted)"})
        return self.json_out(200, j)

    def do_POST(self):
        sec = os.environ.get("FX_INTERNAL_SECRET", "")
        if not sec or self.headers.get("authorization") != "Bearer " + sec:
            return self.json_out(401, {"error": "unauthorized"})
        try:
            n = int(self.headers.get("content-length") or 0)
            data = json.loads(self.rfile.read(n) or b"{}")
        except Exception:
            return self.json_out(400, {"error": "bad json"})
        stems = data.get("stems")
        if not isinstance(stems, list) or not stems:
            return self.json_out(400, {"error": "stems[] required"})
        if len(stems) > 12:
            return self.json_out(400, {"error": "max 12 stems"})
        for s in stems:
            if not str(s.get("url") or "").startswith("https://"):
                return self.json_out(400, {"error": "each stem needs an https url"})
        jid = uuid.uuid4().hex
        with LK:
            busy = any(v.get("status") in ("queued", "running") for v in JOBS.values())
            if not busy:
                JOBS[jid] = {"status": "queued"}
        if busy:
            return self.json_out(429, {"error": "engine busy - try again in a minute"})
        t = threading.Thread(target=worker, kwargs=dict(jid=jid, stems=stems, loud=str(data.get("loudness") or "MEDIUM"), max_sec=float(data.get("maxSeconds") or 0), preset=str(data.get("preset") or "neutral")))
        t.daemon = True
        t.start()
        return self.json_out(202, {"job": jid})


if __name__ == "__main__":
    port = int(os.environ.get("PORT", "8080"))
    print("wratty-fx mixer on 0.0.0.0:%d" % port, flush=True)
    ThreadingHTTPServer(("0.0.0.0", port), Handler).serve_forever()

FILE: fx-api/api/mix_balance.py   (55 lines)

"""
mix_balance.py -- per-song tonal balance planner for the automix (Afrobeats).
"""
import numpy as np
import scipy.signal as sg
from scipy import ndimage

CENTERS = np.array([31.5, 40.0, 50.0, 63.0, 80.0, 100.0, 125.0, 160.0, 200.0, 250.0, 315.0, 400.0,
                    500.0, 630.0, 800.0, 1000.0, 1250.0, 1600.0, 2000.0, 2500.0, 3150.0, 4000.0,
                    5000.0, 6300.0, 8000.0, 10000.0, 12500.0, 16000.0])
REF_MEDIAN = np.array([-26.1, -15.5, -8.6, -8.8, -11.1, -11.7, -11.6, -13.9, -14.2, -14.5, -15.2,
                       -12.9, -14.3, -14.9, -16.6, -19.3, -18.5, -18.4, -21.8, -24.7, -24.5, -24.9,
                       -26.2, -25.7, -26.5, -26.6, -30.4, -36.0])

MAX_SHELF_DB, MIN_SHELF_DB = 4.0, -3.0
MAX_BELL_DB, MIN_BELL_DB = 2.0, -2.0
MAX_CUT_DB, MAX_BOOST_DB = -3.0, 1.0

def _mono(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    return x.mean(axis=0) if x.ndim == 2 else x

def _ltas(m, sr):
    f, P = sg.welch(m, sr, nperseg=16384)
    edges = np.concatenate([[CENTERS[0] / 2 ** (1 / 6)], CENTERS * 2 ** (1 / 6)])
    B = np.array([P[(f >= edges[i]) & (f < edges[i + 1])].sum() for i in range(len(CENTERS))])
    return 10 * np.log10(B / B.sum() + 1e-20)

def _gap(m, sr):
    g = REF_MEDIAN - _ltas(m, sr)
    anchor = np.median(g[(CENTERS >= 160) & (CENTERS <= 8000)])
    return ndimage.gaussian_filter1d(g - anchor, 1.0, mode="nearest")

def _med(g, lo, hi):
    return float(np.median(g[(CENTERS >= lo) & (CENTERS <= hi)]))

def plan(mixed, sr):
    m = _mono(mixed)
    if m.shape[0] < sr * 2 or np.abs(m).max() < 1e-6:
        return {"shelf80_db": MAX_SHELF_DB, "bell125_db": MAX_BELL_DB, "cut250_db": -2.5, "cut420_db": -2.0,
                "cut550_db": -1.0, "vocal_hot_db": 0.0, "note": "input too short/silent: legacy fixed values"}
    g = _gap(m, sr)
    shelf = float(np.clip(0.9 * _med(g, 40, 80), MIN_SHELF_DB, MAX_SHELF_DB))
    bell = float(np.clip(0.5 * _med(g, 100, 160), MIN_BELL_DB, MAX_BELL_DB))
    cuts = []
    for lo, hi in ((200, 315), (400, 400), (500, 630)):
        cuts.append(float(np.clip(0.8 * _med(g, lo, hi), MAX_CUT_DB, MAX_BOOST_DB)))
    return {"shelf80_db": round(shelf, 2), "bell125_db": round(bell, 2),
            "cut250_db": round(cuts[0], 2), "cut420_db": round(cuts[1], 2), "cut550_db": round(cuts[2], 2),
            "vocal_hot_db": round(float(-_med(g, 800, 2500)), 2),
            "gap_db": {str(int(c)): round(float(v), 1) for c, v in zip(CENTERS, g) if 40 <= c <= 2500}}

def vocal_trim_db(p):
    hot = float(p.get("vocal_hot_db", 0.0))
    return float(np.clip((hot - 1.0) * 0.7, 0.0, 3.0)) if hot > 2.0 else 0.0


==============================================================================
FILE: fx-api/api/noise_clean.py   (299 lines)
==============================================================================

"""
noise_clean.py (v2, merged)  -  adaptive noise / rumble / hum cleaner for vocal STEMS (lead, backing, adlib). Never the beat.

Keeps everything from v1 (learn -> decide -> clean -> check itself) and adds:
  * RUMBLE HIGH-PASS  (zero-phase, 70 Hz): mic-stand thump, AC/fan/street rumble. Only applied when cleaning happens,
    so stems that are skipped stay bit-identical.
  * CONTINUOUS NOISE TRACKER (minimum-statistics style) used automatically when a stem has no clear gaps to learn from
    (singer barely pauses) - v1 had to give up there. When gaps exist, the gap profile + level tracker is used as before.
  * REAL HUM DETECTOR: narrow peaks (>=10 dB over their immediate neighbours, >=2 harmonics of 50/60 Hz) measured at
    3 Hz resolution on the quiet audio, removed with narrow zero-phase notches. A broad rumble hump is NOT hum.
  * GAIN RULE option: "wiener" (v1 behaviour, default - best under the self-check) or "lsa" (log-MMSE, optional).
  * sample-rate fix: the 50 ms release no longer assumes 44.1 kHz.

USAGE (first on every vocal stem, before EQ / compression / saturation)
    y, rep = noise_clean.clean(stem, sr, role="lead")     # (N,) or (2,N). role "beat" is returned untouched.
    rep    = noise_clean.analyse(stem, sr)                # dry run for UI / stem QC
"""
import time
import numpy as np
import scipy.signal as sg
from scipy.ndimage import uniform_filter1d, percentile_filter
from scipy.special import exp1
from scipy.stats import gamma as _gamma

__all__ = ["clean", "analyse"]

NFFT, HOP = 2048, 512
ROLE = {"lead": 1.0, "adlib": 0.9, "backing": 0.8}
MAX_DEPTH, ACTIVE_DEPTH, MIN_DEPTH = 14.0, 8.0, 2.0
DEV_LIMIT_DB, FLUTTER_LIMIT_DB = 0.6, 3.0
HP_HZ = 70.0


def _frames(mono, sr, nt):
    rms = np.sqrt(np.maximum(uniform_filter1d(mono ** 2, size=NFFT, mode="constant"), 0.0) + 1e-24)
    idx = np.clip(np.arange(nt) * HOP, 0, len(mono) - 1)
    return 20.0 * np.log10(rms[idx] + 1e-12)


def _runs(mask, min_len):
    m = uniform_filter1d(mask.astype(float), size=min_len, mode="nearest") > 0.999
    return mask & (m | (uniform_filter1d(m.astype(float), size=min_len, mode="nearest") > 0.0))


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- noise tracking (no-gap fallback)
def _tracker(P, valid, window_s, sr, pct=12.0):
    """continuous noise PSD: percentile of the time-smoothed spectrogram over a sliding window + bias compensation"""
    nb, nt = P.shape
    msm = 5
    Ps = uniform_filter1d(P, size=msm, axis=1)
    E = Ps.sum(0)
    q = valid & (E <= np.percentile(E[valid], 10))
    glob = np.median(Ps[:, q], axis=1)
    Pf = Ps.copy()
    Pf[:, ~valid] = glob[:, None]
    sub = 4
    win = max(3, int(window_s * sr / HOP / sub) | 1)
    Nd = percentile_filter(Pf[:, ::sub], pct, size=(1, win), mode="nearest")
    m_eff = max(1.0, msm / 2.5)
    bias = m_eff / _gamma.ppf(pct / 100.0, m_eff)
    xs, tt = np.arange(Nd.shape[1]), np.arange(nt) / sub
    N = np.empty((nb, nt), np.float32)
    for k in range(nb):
        N[k] = np.interp(tt, xs, Nd[k])
    N = uniform_filter1d(N * bias, size=7, axis=0)
    N = uniform_filter1d(N, size=25, axis=1)
    return np.maximum(N, 1e-20).astype(np.float32)


def _learn(mono, sr, P):
    nb, nt = P.shape
    L = _frames(mono, sr, nt)
    valid = L > -110.0
    if valid.sum() < 50:
        return {"skip": "digital silence / too short"}
    p10, p90 = np.percentile(L[valid], 10), np.percentile(L[valid], 90)
    if p90 < -95.0:
        return {"skip": "stem is inaudible"}
    act = L > p90 - 20.0
    gap = _runs((L < p10 + 6.0) & valid, 8)
    if p90 - p10 >= 15.0 and gap.sum() >= 20:
        return {"L": L, "valid": valid, "gap": gap, "act": act, "mode": "gap-profile",
                "noise_dbfs": float(np.median(L[gap])),
                "rel": float(np.median(L[act]) - np.median(L[gap]))}
    # no clear gaps: track the noise continuously instead of giving up
    N = _tracker(P, valid, 3.0, sr)
    snr = 10 * np.log10((P.sum(0) + 1e-20) / (N.sum(0) + 1e-20))
    rel = float(np.median(snr[act])) if act.any() else 0.0
    q = valid & (L <= np.percentile(L[valid], 12))
    return {"L": L, "valid": valid, "gap": q, "act": act, "mode": "continuous-tracker", "N": N,
            "noise_dbfs": float(np.median(L[act]) - rel), "rel": rel}


def _plan(info, strength, role):
    if "skip" in info:
        return 0.0, info["skip"]
    if info["noise_dbfs"] < -80.0:
        return 0.0, "noise below -80 dBFS (inaudible)"
    depth = float(np.clip((72.0 - info["rel"]) / 3.0, 0.0, MAX_DEPTH)) * ROLE.get(role, 1.0) * float(np.clip(strength, 0.0, 1.0))
    return (depth, "ok") if depth >= MIN_DEPTH else (0.0, "already clean enough (SNR %.0f dB)" % info["rel"])


def _profile(P, gap, L):
    prof = np.median(P[:, gap], axis=1)
    prof = uniform_filter1d(prof, size=5, mode="nearest") + 1e-20
    gsum = P[:, gap].sum(axis=0) / prof.sum()
    gi = np.flatnonzero(gap)
    g = np.interp(np.arange(P.shape[1]), gi, np.clip(gsum, 0.25, 4.0))
    return prof, uniform_filter1d(g, size=15, mode="nearest")


def _noise_matrix(P, info):
    if info["mode"] == "continuous-tracker":
        return info["N"]
    prof, gt = _profile(P, info["gap"], info["L"])
    return (prof[:, None] * gt[None, :]).astype(np.float32)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- pre-filters: rumble HP + hum notches
def _find_hum(chan, sr, info):
    """Hum is STATIONARY: it is still there in the 25th-percentile spectrum over the whole stem, while voice and
    phrase noise move around.  Narrow peaks (3 Hz resolution) on 50/60 Hz harmonics, >=2 of them, >=10 dB over neighbours."""
    nper = 16384
    if len(chan) < nper * 4:
        return []
    f, _, Z = sg.stft(chan, sr, window="hann", nperseg=nper, noverlap=nper // 2, boundary=None)
    Pw = np.abs(Z) ** 2
    keep = Pw.sum(0) > 1e-20
    if keep.sum() < 6:
        return []
    pd = 10 * np.log10(np.percentile(Pw[:, keep], 25, axis=1) + 1e-30)
    for base in (50.0, 60.0):
        hits = []
        for h in range(1, 7):
            fc = base * h
            if fc > 800:
                break
            band = (f > fc - 1.5) & (f < fc + 1.5)
            around = ((f > fc - 12) & (f < fc - 5)) | ((f > fc + 5) & (f < fc + 12))
            if band.any() and around.any() and pd[band].max() - np.median(pd[around]) > 10.0:
                hits.append(fc)
        if len(hits) >= 2:
            top = int(round(max(hits) / base))                 # notch every harmonic up to one above the highest found
            return [base * k for k in range(1, min(top + 1, 8) + 1) if base * k <= 800]
    return []


def _prefilter(chan, sr, hum):
    y = chan - np.mean(chan)
    y = sg.sosfiltfilt(sg.butter(4, HP_HZ, "hp", fs=sr, output="sos"), y)
    for fc in hum:
        b, a = sg.iirnotch(fc, Q=40, fs=sr)
        y = sg.filtfilt(b, a, y)
    return y


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- gain
def _gain(P, N, act_frames, depth, alpha, L, sr, rule):
    nb, nt = P.shape
    gmin_gap = np.full(nb, 10 ** (-depth / 20.0))
    gmin_act = np.full(nb, 10 ** (-min(depth, ACTIVE_DEPTH) / 20.0))
    G = np.empty((nb, nt), np.float32)
    Gp, Pp = np.ones(nb), np.zeros(nb)
    for t in range(nt):
        Nt = N[:, t]
        gamma = P[:, t] / Nt
        xi = alpha * (Gp * Gp * Pp / Nt) + (1.0 - alpha) * np.maximum(gamma - 1.0, 0.0)
        if rule == "lsa":
            xi = np.maximum(xi, 10 ** (-25 / 10))
            v = xi / (1.0 + xi) * gamma
            g = np.minimum(xi / (1.0 + xi) * np.exp(0.5 * exp1(np.maximum(v, 1e-8))), 1.0)
        else:
            g = xi / (1.0 + xi)
        g = np.maximum(g, gmin_act if act_frames[t] else gmin_gap)
        G[:, t] = g
        Gp, Pp = g, P[:, t]
    G = uniform_filter1d(G, size=3, axis=0, mode="nearest")
    decay = np.exp(-(HOP / float(sr)) / 0.050)
    for t in range(1, nt):
        G[:, t] = np.maximum(G[:, t], G[:, t - 1] * decay)
    rise = np.flatnonzero(L[3:] - L[:-3] > 6.0) + 3
    for t in rise:
        G[:, max(0, t - 1):t + 2] = np.maximum(G[:, max(0, t - 1):t + 2], 0.7)
    return G


def _bands(sr):
    c = np.array([150, 200, 250, 315, 400, 500, 630, 800, 1000, 1250, 1600, 2000, 2500, 3150, 4000, 5000, 6300, 8000.0])
    return [(int(f / 2 ** (1 / 6) / (sr / NFFT)), int(f * 2 ** (1 / 6) / (sr / NFFT))) for f in c]


def _guard(P, G, act, gap, sr, N):
    devs = []
    ai = np.flatnonzero(act)
    for a, b in _bands(sr):
        Pa = P[a:b][:, ai]
        m = Pa > 10.0 * N[a:b][:, ai]
        if m.sum() < 50:
            continue
        before = Pa[m].sum()
        after = ((G[a:b][:, ai] ** 2) * Pa)[m].sum()
        devs.append(abs(10 * np.log10((after + 1e-30) / (before + 1e-30))))
    devs = devs or [0.0]
    b0, b1 = _bands(sr)[0][0], _bands(sr)[-1][1]
    gdb = 20 * np.log10(G[b0:b1][:, gap] + 1e-9)
    return float(np.mean(devs)), float(np.max(devs)), float(np.mean(np.std(gdb, axis=1)))


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- public API
def analyse(x, sr, role="lead", strength=1.0):
    x = np.asarray(x, np.float64)
    mono = x if x.ndim == 1 else x.mean(axis=0)
    if len(mono) < 2 * sr:
        return {"plan": "skip", "reason": "too short"}
    _, _, Z = sg.stft(mono, sr, window="hann", nperseg=NFFT, noverlap=NFFT - HOP)
    info = _learn(mono, sr, (np.abs(Z) ** 2).astype(np.float32))
    depth, why = _plan(info, strength, role)
    return {"plan": "clean" if depth else "skip", "reason": why, "depth_db": round(depth, 1),
            "noise_source": info.get("mode", "n/a"),
            "noise_dbfs": round(info.get("noise_dbfs", float("nan")), 1), "snr_db": round(info.get("rel", float("nan")), 1)}


def clean(x, sr, role="lead", strength=1.0, rule="wiener", return_gain=False):
    t0 = time.time()
    x = np.asarray(x, np.float64)
    rep = {"role": role}
    if role == "beat":
        rep["action"] = "skipped (beat is never denoised)"
        return x, rep
    mono0 = x if x.ndim == 1 else x.mean(axis=0)
    n = mono0.shape[-1]
    if n < 2 * sr:
        rep["action"] = "skipped (too short)"
        return x, rep

    # 1) DECIDE on the untouched stem (rumble counts as noise here, so a rumble-only stem is still cleaned)
    _, _, Z0 = sg.stft(mono0, sr, window="hann", nperseg=NFFT, noverlap=NFFT - HOP)
    info0 = _learn(mono0, sr, (np.abs(Z0) ** 2).astype(np.float32))
    depth, why = _plan(info0, strength, role)
    rep.update(noise_dbfs=round(info0.get("noise_dbfs", float("nan")), 1), snr_db=round(info0.get("rel", float("nan")), 1))
    if depth == 0.0:
        rep["action"] = "skipped (%s)" % why
        return (x, rep) if not return_gain else (x, rep, None)

    # 2) PRE-FILTER: rumble high-pass + real hum notches
    hum = _find_hum(mono0, sr, info0)
    chans0 = [x] if x.ndim == 1 else [x[0], x[1]]
    chans = [_prefilter(c, sr, hum) for c in chans0]
    mono = chans[0] if x.ndim == 1 else 0.5 * (chans[0] + chans[1])

    # 3) LEARN again on the pre-filtered audio (depth stays as decided above)
    Zs = [sg.stft(c, sr, window="hann", nperseg=NFFT, noverlap=NFFT - HOP)[2].astype(np.complex64) for c in chans]
    P = np.mean([np.abs(Z) ** 2 for Z in Zs], axis=0).astype(np.float32)
    info = _learn(mono, sr, P)
    if "skip" in info:
        info = info0
        P = (np.abs(Z0) ** 2).astype(np.float32)
    L, gap, act = info["L"], info["gap"], info["act"]
    N = _noise_matrix(P, info)

    # 4) CLEAN with self-check (retries gentler if the voice moves or the gaps flutter)
    attempts, best = [], None

    def judge(G, rec):
        dev_mean, dev_max, flutter = _guard(P, G, act, gap, sr, N)
        rec.update(voice_change_db=round(dev_mean, 2), flutter_db=round(flutter, 2))
        attempts.append(rec)
        return max(dev_mean / DEV_LIMIT_DB, flutter / FLUTTER_LIMIT_DB), G

    G = None
    for alpha, mult in ((0.98, 1.0), (0.985, 1.0), (0.985, 0.7), (0.99, 0.5)):
        G = _gain(P, N, act, depth * mult, alpha, L, sr, rule)
        score, G = judge(G, {"depth_db": round(depth * mult, 1)})
        if best is None or score < best[0]:
            best = (score, G, dict(attempts[-1]))
        if score <= 1.0:
            break
    if best[0] > 1.0:
        base = G
        for w in (0.6, 0.4, 0.25):
            Gb = ((1.0 - w) + w * base).astype(np.float32)
            score, Gb = judge(Gb, {"depth_db": round(depth * 0.5, 1), "wet_blend": w})
            if score < best[0]:
                best = (score, Gb, dict(attempts[-1]))
            if score <= 1.0:
                break
    G = best[1]
    final = best[2]
    outs = [sg.istft(Z * G, sr, window="hann", nperseg=NFFT, noverlap=NFFT - HOP)[1][:n] for Z in Zs]
    y = outs[0] if x.ndim == 1 else np.stack(outs)
    mo = y if y.ndim == 1 else y.mean(axis=0)
    Lo = _frames(mo, sr, P.shape[1])
    rep.update(action="cleaned", noise_source=info["mode"], gain_rule=rule, highpass_hz=HP_HZ, hum_hz=hum or None,
               depth_requested_db=round(depth, 1),
               gap_noise_reduction_db=round(float(np.median(L[gap]) - np.median(Lo[gap])), 1),
               voice_change_db=final["voice_change_db"], flutter_db=final["flutter_db"], applied=final, attempts=attempts,
               quality_guard="passed" if best[0] <= 1.0 else "limits not met - the least damaging setting was used",
               seconds=round(time.time() - t0, 1))
    return (y, rep) if not return_gain else (y, rep, G)


==============================================================================
FILE: fx-api/api/opto_comp.py   (155 lines)
==============================================================================

"""LA-2A T4-cell optical compressor emulation.

Models the actual signal path of a Teletronix LA-2A, not just its
broad-stroke behavior:

  1. Input transformer rolloff on the SIDECHAIN only (audio path is flat).
  2. Sidechain drives an EL panel model: fast attack (~10 ms), fast decay (~60 ms).
  3. EL panel light drives a CdS T4 cell: leaky integrator with slow thermal
     recovery (~1.5 s tail). This is the "memory" that gives the LA-2A its
     program-dependent release.
  4. Cell conductance → gain reduction via soft-knee curve with LEVEL-DEPENDENT
     ratio (≈2.5:1 at low GR, rising toward 6:1 under sustained drive).
  5. Stereo channels share a linked sidechain (mono-summed peak), so imaging
     stays intact — matches how two hardware LA-2As would be strapped.

The result: transients poke through cleanly, sustained notes sit in a pocket,
and the recovery tail breathes instead of pumping. This is why the LA-2A is
the industry standard for vocals and bass.
"""
import numpy as np


def _db_to_lin(db):
    return 10.0 ** (db / 20.0)


def _lin_to_db(lin):
    return 20.0 * np.log10(np.maximum(lin, 1e-12))


def opto_compress(x, sr,
                  threshold_db=-20.0,
                  ratio=2.5,              # nominal LOW-GR ratio (LA-2A: ~2.5–3:1)
                  attack_ms=10.0,         # fixed on real LA-2A (T4 cell), not user-tweakable
                  release_fast_ms=60.0,   # EL panel phosphor decay
                  release_slow_ms=1500.0, # CdS cell thermal memory
                  fast_weight=0.55,       # kept for API compatibility (unused; blending is implicit)
                  knee_db=6.0,
                  makeup_db=0.0):
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim == 1:
        a = a[None, :]
    if a.shape[1] < 64:
        return x, {"note": "too short"}

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Sidechain input transformer (audio path is UNTOUCHED).
    #    LA-2A's input transformer has limited low-end on the sidechain,
    #    which is why bass plosives don't trigger compression. Model as
    #    1st-order HPF at ~80 Hz on the detector path only.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    try:
        from scipy.signal import butter, sosfilt
        _sc_sos = butter(1, 80.0 / (sr * 0.5), btype="high", output="sos")
        a_det = sosfilt(_sc_sos, a, axis=1)
    except Exception:
        a_det = a

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Sidechain detector. Mono-linked peak (max over L/R) matches
    #    strapped hardware. LA-2A is inherently peak-responsive.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    peak = np.max(np.abs(a_det), axis=0)

    atk_coef      = float(np.exp(-1.0 / max(1.0, sr * attack_ms / 1000.0)))
    rel_fast_coef = float(np.exp(-1.0 / max(1.0, sr * release_fast_ms / 1000.0)))
    rel_slow_coef = float(np.exp(-1.0 / max(1.0, sr * release_slow_ms / 1000.0)))

    n = peak.shape[0]
    light_panel = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)   # EL panel intensity (fast)
    cell_state  = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)   # T4 cell memory (slow)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Two-stage envelope:
    #    (a) light_panel tracks the sidechain fast (~10 ms up, ~60 ms down).
    #    (b) cell_state follows light_panel with slow thermal release,
    #        which is the actual LA-2A "memory" behavior.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    lp = 0.0
    cs = 0.0
    for i in range(n):
        s = peak[i]
        # EL panel: fast attack, fast release
        if s > lp:
            lp = s + atk_coef * (lp - s)
        else:
            lp = s + rel_fast_coef * (lp - s)
        light_panel[i] = lp

        # T4 cell: responds to panel light with slow thermal recovery
        if lp > cs:
            cs = lp + atk_coef * (cs - lp)
        else:
            cs = lp + rel_slow_coef * (cs - lp)
        cell_state[i] = cs

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Gain-reduction curve. Soft knee, LEVEL-DEPENDENT ratio.
    #    Ratio rises with drive to match the T4 cell's inherent
    #    compression behavior under sustained signal.
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    light_db = _lin_to_db(cell_state + 1e-12)
    over = light_db - threshold_db

    gr_db = np.zeros_like(over)

    # Soft-knee transition (below threshold)
    knee_mask = (over > -knee_db) & (over <= 0.0)
    if knee_mask.any():
        # quadratic soft-knee (standard formulation)
        gr_db[knee_mask] = -(over[knee_mask] + knee_db) ** 2 / (2.0 * knee_db) * (1.0 - 1.0 / ratio)

    # Above-threshold: ratio rises from `ratio` toward ~2.4x under drive
    high_mask = over > 0.0
    if high_mask.any():
        excess = over[high_mask]
        ratio_low = float(ratio)
        ratio_high = ratio_low * 2.4
        # Smooth rise: 0 → 1 as excess goes 0 → ∞ (tanh at 8 dB scale)
        local_ratio = ratio_low + (ratio_high - ratio_low) * np.tanh(excess / 8.0)
        # Effective GR is integral of (1 - 1/ratio). Pointwise approx is
        # within <0.5 dB of the exact integral for this curve.
        gr_db[high_mask] = -excess * (1.0 - 1.0 / local_ratio)

    # Hard cap so an unusually hot take can't slam the cell into -30 dB
    gr_db = np.maximum(gr_db, -18.0)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Apply gain and match output RMS (LA-2A has a manual Gain knob;
    #    we auto-match so heavy GR doesn't leave the chain under-leveled).
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    gain = _db_to_lin(gr_db + makeup_db)
    y = (a * gain[None, :]).astype(np.float32)

    if makeup_db == 0.0:
        rms_in  = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(a ** 2))) + 1e-12
        rms_out = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(y ** 2))) + 1e-12
        auto_makeup_db = float(np.clip(20.0 * np.log10(rms_in / rms_out), -6.0, 12.0))
        y = (y * (10.0 ** (auto_makeup_db / 20.0))).astype(np.float32)
    else:
        auto_makeup_db = 0.0

    info = {
        "model": "LA-2A T4-cell",
        "ratio_low": round(float(ratio), 2),
        "ratio_high": round(float(ratio) * 2.4, 2),
        "threshold_db": round(float(threshold_db), 2),
        "attack_ms": round(float(attack_ms), 1),
        "release_fast_ms": round(float(release_fast_ms), 1),
        "release_slow_ms": round(float(release_slow_ms), 1),
        "max_gr_db": round(float(np.min(gr_db)), 2),
        "avg_gr_db": round(float(np.mean(gr_db)), 2),
        "auto_makeup_db": round(auto_makeup_db, 2),
    }
    return y, info


==============================================================================
FILE: fx-api/api/parallel_comp.py   (23 lines)
==============================================================================

"""NY-style parallel compression. Additive blend, no dry attenuation."""
import numpy as np
from pedalboard import Pedalboard, Compressor, Gain, HighpassFilter


def industry_parallel_compressor(x, sr, blend_amount=0.30):
    dry = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float32).copy()
    if dry.ndim != 2 or dry.shape[1] == 0:
        return x

    smash = Pedalboard([
        HighpassFilter(cutoff_frequency_hz=120.0),
        Compressor(threshold_db=-32.0, ratio=12.0,
                   attack_ms=0.5, release_ms=60.0),
        Gain(gain_db=14.0),
    ])
    wet = smash(dry, int(sr)).astype(np.float32)

    out = (dry + wet * float(blend_amount)).astype(np.float32)
    pk = float(np.max(np.abs(out)))
    if pk > 0.95:
        out = (out * (0.95 / pk)).astype(np.float32)
    return out

FILE: fx-api/api/polish.py   (128 lines)

"""polish.py - dynamic control AFTER the effects. Pure numpy, no scipy.

Used by auto.py's _master() (the full-mix master bus).
One primitive:
  dynamic_eq   - tracking dynamic EQ (harshness + sibilance), stereo-linked
"""
import numpy as np

NFFT = 2048
HOP = 512


def _stft(x, n=NFFT, hop=HOP, chunk=256):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float32).ravel()
    if len(x) < n:
        p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float32); p[:len(x)] = x; x = p
    nf = 1 + (len(x) - n) // hop
    win = np.hanning(n).astype(np.float32)
    S = np.empty((nf, n // 2 + 1), dtype=np.complex64)
    for i in range(0, nf, chunk):
        j = min(i + chunk, nf)
        idx = np.arange(n)[None, :] + hop * np.arange(i, j)[:, None]
        S[i:j] = np.fft.rfft(x[idx] * win, axis=1)
    return S, win


def _istft(S, win, hop=HOP, length=None):
    n = win.shape[0]
    out = np.zeros(S.shape[0] * hop + n, dtype=np.float32)
    acc = np.zeros_like(out)
    ww = (win * win).astype(np.float32)
    for i in range(0, S.shape[0], 256):
        j = min(i + 256, S.shape[0])
        fr = np.fft.irfft(S[i:j], n, axis=1).astype(np.float32) * win
        for k in range(j - i):
            p = (i + k) * hop
            out[p:p + n] += fr[k]
            acc[p:p + n] += ww
    acc[acc < 1e-8] = 1.0
    y = out / acc
    return y if length is None else y[:length]


def _smooth(x, fast_frames=1.0, slow_frames=8.0):
    """One-pole smoothing. fast when the value drops (more reduction), slow when it rises."""
    f = 1.0 - np.exp(-1.0 / max(1.0, float(fast_frames)))
    s = 1.0 - np.exp(-1.0 / max(1.0, float(slow_frames)))
    y = np.empty(len(x), dtype=np.float32)
    v = 0.0
    for i in range(len(x)):
        c = float(x[i])
        v += (f if c < v else s) * (c - v)
        y[i] = v
    return y


def _dyn_gain(key, sr, specs):
    """Per-bin, per-frame real gain. Thresholds are RELATIVE to each band's median."""
    S, win = _stft(key)
    mag = np.abs(S) * (2.0 / max(1e-9, float(win.sum())))
    freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(NFFT, 1.0 / float(sr))
    hop_ms = 1000.0 * HOP / float(sr)
    g = np.ones_like(mag, dtype=np.float32)
    stats = []
    for b in specs:
        lo, hi, off, max_db, ratio, atk_ms, rel_ms = b
        m = (freq >= lo) & (freq <= hi)
        if not m.any():
            stats.append((lo, hi, None, None, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0)); continue
        band = np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(mag[:, m]), axis=1))
        edb = 20.0 * np.log10(band + 1e-12)
        med = float(np.median(edb))
        thr = med + float(off)
        over = np.maximum(edb - thr, 0.0)
        red = -np.minimum(over * (1.0 - 1.0 / float(ratio)), float(max_db))
        red = _smooth(red, max(1.0, atk_ms / hop_ms), max(1.0, rel_ms / hop_ms))
        g[:, m] *= (10.0 ** (red[:, None] / 20.0)).astype(np.float32)
        act = 100.0 * float(np.count_nonzero(red < -0.1)) / max(1, len(red))
        stats.append((lo, hi, med, thr, float(np.mean(red)), float(np.min(red)), act))
    return g, win, stats


def calibrate(x, sr, bands=((2500.0, 4500.0), (5500.0, 9000.0)),
              offsets=(3.0, 6.0, 9.0, 12.0)):
    """How much time each band would spend over threshold, per offset (dB above median)."""
    key = x.mean(axis=0) if x.ndim > 1 else x
    S, win = _stft(key)
    mag = np.abs(S) * (2.0 / max(1e-9, float(win.sum())))
    freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(NFFT, 1.0 / float(sr))
    rows = []
    for lo, hi in bands:
        m = (freq >= lo) & (freq <= hi)
        if not m.any():
            continue
        band = np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(mag[:, m]), axis=1))
        edb = 20.0 * np.log10(band + 1e-12)
        med = float(np.median(edb))
        pct = []
        for off in offsets:
            over = np.maximum(edb - (med + off), 0.0)
            pct.append(100.0 * float(np.count_nonzero(over > 0.2)) / max(1, len(over)))
        rows.append((lo, hi, med, list(zip(offsets, pct))))
    return rows


def _specs(harsh_off=12.0, sib_off=10.0):
    """(lo_hz, hi_hz, offset_db_above_median, max_cut_db, ratio, attack_ms, release_ms)"""
    return [
        (2500.0, 4500.0, float(harsh_off), 3.0, 3.0, 10.0, 80.0),
        (5500.0, 9000.0, float(sib_off), 2.5, 3.0, 2.0, 40.0),
    ]


def dynamic_eq(x, sr, specs=None, get_stats=False):
    if specs is None:
        specs = _specs()
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float32)
    single = x.ndim == 1
    if single:
        x = x[None, :]
    key = x.mean(axis=0)
    g, win, stats = _dyn_gain(key, sr, specs)
    out = np.empty_like(x)
    for c in range(x.shape[0]):
        Sc, _ = _stft(x[c])
        out[c] = _istft(Sc * g, win, length=x.shape[1])
    res = out[0] if single else out
    return (res, stats) if get_stats else res

"""
qc.py - advisory audio QC for WrattyGstudio.  numpy only (no scipy).
"""
import json
import re
import subprocess
import sys

import numpy as np

QC_LIMITS = {
    "clip_level": 0.999,
    "clip_runs_flag": 20,
    "hot_peak_db": -0.1,
    "quiet_active_rms_db": -35.0,
    "noise_floor_flag_db": -50.0,
    "sparse_active_ratio": 0.35,
    "out_plr_min_db": 7.0,
    "out_mono_loss_max_db": 3.0,
    "out_corr_min": 0.20,
    "out_sub_gap_max_db": 8.0,
    "out_tp_margin_db": 0.3,
}

BANDS = [(20, 60), (60, 120), (120, 250), (250, 600),
         (600, 2000), (2000, 5500), (5500, 10000), (10000, 16000)]

_VOCAL_WORDS = ("lead", "adlib", "ad-lib", "backing", "vocal", "vox", "harmony", "bv")


def _arr(a):
    a = np.asarray(a)
    if a.ndim == 1:
        a = a[None, :]
    if a.shape[0] > a.shape[1]:
        a = a.T
    return a.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def _db(v):
    return float(20.0 * np.log10(max(float(v), 1e-12)))


def _rms(x):
    return float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(x)))) if x.size else 0.0


def clip_stats(a, level=None):
    level = QC_LIMITS["clip_level"] if level is None else level
    hit = (np.abs(_arr(a)) >= level).any(axis=0)
    d = np.diff(np.concatenate([[0], hit.astype(np.int8), [0]]))
    starts = np.where(d == 1)[0]
    ends = np.where(d == -1)[0]
    runs = ends - starts
    return {
        "clipped_samples": int(hit.sum()),
        "clip_runs": int(runs.size),
        "clip_runs_ge3": int((runs >= 3).sum()),
        "clip_max_run": int(runs.max()) if runs.size else 0,
    }


def activity(a, sr, frame_s=0.05, gate_db=-45.0):
    m = _arr(a).mean(axis=0)
    w = max(1, int(frame_s * sr))
    k = len(m) // w
    if k < 1:
        return {"active_ratio": 0.0, "whole_rms_db": _db(_rms(m)),
                "active_rms_db": _db(_rms(m)), "noise_floor_db": None}
    seg = m[: k * w].reshape(k, w)
    fr = 20.0 * np.log10(np.sqrt((seg ** 2).mean(axis=1)) + 1e-12)
    act = fr > gate_db
    out = {
        "active_ratio": round(float(act.mean()), 3),
        "whole_rms_db": round(_db(_rms(m)), 2),
        "active_rms_db": round(_db(_rms(seg[act])) if act.any() else _db(_rms(m)), 2),
    }
    out["noise_floor_db"] = round(float(np.percentile(fr[~act], 10)), 1) if (~act).any() else None
    return out


def f0_percentiles(a, sr, max_frames=4000):
    x = _arr(a).mean(axis=0)
    N, H = 2048, 512
    lo, hi = int(sr / 600), int(sr / 70)
    win = np.hanning(N)
    starts = np.arange(0, len(x) - N, H)
    if starts.size == 0:
        return None
    if starts.size > max_frames * 3:
        starts = starts[:: int(starts.size // (max_frames * 3))]
    f0 = []
    for i in starts:
        s = x[i:i + N]
        if _db(_rms(s)) < -40.0:
            continue
        F = np.fft.rfft(s * win, 2 * N)
        ac = np.fft.irfft(np.abs(F) ** 2)[:N]
        ac = ac / (ac[0] + 1e-12)
        seg = ac[lo:hi]
        j = int(np.argmax(seg))
        if seg[j] > 0.5:
            f0.append(sr / (lo + j))
        if len(f0) >= max_frames:
            break
    if len(f0) < 20:
        return None
    p = np.percentile(f0, [2, 5, 50, 95])
    return {"f0_p2": round(float(p[0])), "f0_p5": round(float(p[1])),
            "f0_p50": round(float(p[2])), "f0_p95": round(float(p[3])),
            "voiced_frames": len(f0)}


def band_levels_db(a, sr, nfft=16384):
    x = _arr(a)
    w = np.hanning(nfft)
    norm = nfft * float(np.sum(w ** 2))
    freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(nfft, 1.0 / sr)
    acc = np.zeros(freqs.size)
    cnt = 0
    for ch in range(x.shape[0]):
        for i in range(0, x.shape[1] - nfft, nfft // 2):
            X = np.fft.rfft(x[ch, i:i + nfft] * w)
            p = (np.abs(X) ** 2) * 2.0 / norm
            acc += p
            cnt += 1
    if cnt == 0:
        return {}
    acc /= cnt
    out = {}
    for lo, hi in BANDS:
        m = (freqs >= lo) & (freqs < hi)
        out["%d-%d" % (lo, hi)] = round(10.0 * np.log10(float(acc[m].sum()) + 1e-20), 1)
    return out


def stereo_stats(a):
    x = _arr(a)
    if x.shape[0] < 2:
        return {"corr": 1.0, "mono_loss_db": 0.0, "side_minus_mid_db": None, "mono_file": True}
    L, R = x[0], x[1]
    d = float(np.std(L) * np.std(R))
    corr = float(np.mean((L - L.mean()) * (R - R.mean())) / d) if d > 1e-12 else 1.0
    mid, side = (L + R) / 2, (L - R) / 2
    avg = np.sqrt((_rms(L) ** 2 + _rms(R) ** 2) / 2)
    return {
        "corr": round(corr, 3),
        "mono_loss_db": round(_db(_rms(mid)) - _db(avg), 2),
        "side_minus_mid_db": round(_db(_rms(side)) - _db(_rms(mid)), 1),
        "mono_file": bool(corr > 0.999),
    }


def stem_qc(a, sr, role="stem"):
    x = _arr(a)
    L = QC_LIMITS
    out = {"role": str(role), "duration_s": round(x.shape[1] / float(sr), 2),
           "peak_db": round(_db(np.abs(x).max()), 2)}
    out.update(clip_stats(x))
    out.update(activity(x, sr))
    out.update(stereo_stats(x))
    flags = []
    if out["clip_runs_ge3"] >= L["clip_runs_flag"]:
        flags.append("clipped_input")
    if out["peak_db"] >= L["hot_peak_db"]:
        flags.append("hot_peak")
    if out["active_rms_db"] < L["quiet_active_rms_db"]:
        flags.append("very_quiet")
    if out["noise_floor_db"] is not None and out["noise_floor_db"] > L["noise_floor_flag_db"]:
        flags.append("noisy")
    if out["active_ratio"] < L["sparse_active_ratio"]:
        flags.append("sparse")
    if any(w in str(role).lower() for w in _VOCAL_WORDS):
        f0 = f0_percentiles(x, sr)
        if f0:
            out.update(f0)
            out["suggested_hpf_hz"] = int(min(110, max(70, 0.7 * f0["f0_p2"])))
    out["flags"] = flags
    return out


def output_qc(a, sr, lufs=None, true_peak_db=None, ceiling_db=-1.0):
    x = _arr(a)
    L = QC_LIMITS
    out = {"peak_db": round(_db(np.abs(x).max()), 2)}
    if lufs is not None:
        out["lufs"] = round(float(lufs), 2)
    if true_peak_db is not None:
        out["true_peak_db"] = round(float(true_peak_db), 2)
    if lufs is not None and true_peak_db is not None:
        out["plr_db"] = round(float(true_peak_db) - float(lufs), 2)
    out.update(stereo_stats(x))
    bl = band_levels_db(x, sr)
    out["bands_db"] = bl
    if bl:
        out["sub_minus_mid_db"] = round(bl["20-60"] - bl["600-2000"], 1)
        out["dip_250_600_db"] = round(bl["250-600"] - 0.5 * (bl["120-250"] + bl["600-2000"]), 1)
        out["top_minus_presence_db"] = round(bl["5500-10000"] - bl["2000-5500"], 1)
    out["full_scale_samples"] = int((np.abs(x) >= 0.9999).sum())
    flags = []
    if true_peak_db is not None and true_peak_db > ceiling_db + L["out_tp_margin_db"]:
        flags.append("true_peak_over_ceiling")
    if out["full_scale_samples"] > 0:
        flags.append("full_scale_samples")
    if "plr_db" in out and out["plr_db"] < L["out_plr_min_db"]:
        flags.append("low_plr")
    if out["mono_loss_db"] < -L["out_mono_loss_max_db"]:
        flags.append("mono_loss")
    if out["corr"] < L["out_corr_min"]:
        flags.append("low_correlation")
    if out.get("sub_minus_mid_db", 0) > L["out_sub_gap_max_db"]:
        flags.append("sub_heavy")
    out["flags"] = flags
    return out


def _decode(path):
    cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-v", "error", "-i", path, "-f", "f32le", "-acodec", "pcm_f32le",
           "-ac", "2", "-ar", "44100", "-"]
    raw = subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, check=True).stdout
    return np.frombuffer(raw, dtype=np.float32).reshape(-1, 2).T.astype(np.float64), 44100


def _ffmpeg_loudness(path):
    p = subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-nostats", "-i", path, "-af", "ebur128=peak=true",
                        "-f", "null", "-"], stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True)
    s = p.stderr.split("Summary:")[-1]
    i = re.search(r"I:\s+(-?[\d.]+) LUFS", s)
    t = re.search(r"Peak:\s+(-?[\d.]+) dBFS", s)
    return (float(i.group(1)) if i else None, float(t.group(1)) if t else None)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
        print(__doc__)
        sys.exit(1)
    path = sys.argv[1]
    role = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "stem"
    a, sr = _decode(path)
    if role in ("master", "mix", "output"):
        lu, tp = _ffmpeg_loudness(path)
        print(json.dumps(output_qc(a, sr, lu, tp), indent=2))
    else:
        print(json.dumps(stem_qc(a, sr, role), indent=2))

"""
reverb_fx.py - professional vocal reverb for WrattyGstudio.  numpy only (no scipy, no pedalboard).
"""
import numpy as np

REVERB_PRESETS = {
    "afrobeats": dict(rt60=1.67, hp=450.0, lp=9000.0, duck_db=13.0, width=0.90, level_db=-20.0, er_db=-9.0, hf_damp=0.72, lf_mult=0.90, pd_scale=1.0),
    "amapiano":  dict(rt60=1.80, hp=500.0, lp=10000.0, duck_db=5.0, width=1.00, level_db=-21.0, er_db=-11.0, hf_damp=0.60, lf_mult=0.85, pd_scale=1.1),
    "highlife":  dict(rt60=1.10, hp=400.0, lp=6500.0,  duck_db=3.0, width=0.80, level_db=-19.0, er_db=-7.0,  hf_damp=0.50, lf_mult=0.95, pd_scale=0.8),
    "pop":       dict(rt60=1.50, hp=450.0, lp=9500.0,  duck_db=4.0, width=0.90, level_db=-20.0, er_db=-9.0,  hf_damp=0.58, lf_mult=0.90, pd_scale=1.0),
    "rnb":       dict(rt60=2.10, hp=450.0, lp=7500.0,  duck_db=4.0, width=1.00, level_db=-18.0, er_db=-10.0, hf_damp=0.50, lf_mult=0.85, pd_scale=1.1),
    "rap":       dict(rt60=0.85, hp=500.0, lp=8500.0,  duck_db=6.0, width=0.70, level_db=-23.0, er_db=-6.0,  hf_damp=0.50, lf_mult=0.95, pd_scale=0.8),
    "neutral":   dict(rt60=1.20, hp=450.0, lp=9000.0,  duck_db=4.0, width=0.80, level_db=-21.0, er_db=-9.0,  hf_damp=0.55, lf_mult=0.90, pd_scale=1.0),
}

RT60_MIN, RT60_MAX = 0.5, 3.0
ACTIVE_GATE_DB = -45.0
_CENTERS = np.array([150.0, 500.0, 1500.0, 4500.0, 10000.0])
_IR_CACHE = {}


def fold_bpm(bpm, lo=70.0, hi=140.0):
    try:
        b = float(bpm)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return 100.0
    if not np.isfinite(b) or b <= 0:
        return 100.0
    while b < lo:
        b *= 2.0
    while b > hi:
        b /= 2.0
    return b


def _mono(voc):
    a = np.asarray(voc)
    if a.ndim == 1:
        return a.astype(np.float64)
    return a.mean(axis=0, dtype=np.float64) if a.shape[0] <= a.shape[1] else a.mean(axis=1, dtype=np.float64)


def _rms(a, chunk=1 << 20):
    flat = np.asarray(a).reshape(-1)
    acc = 0.0
    for i in range(0, flat.size, chunk):
        c = flat[i:i + chunk].astype(np.float64)
        acc += float(np.dot(c, c))
    return float(np.sqrt(acc / max(flat.size, 1)))


def _shape(x, sr, hp, lp, order=2, block=1 << 17, pad=4096):
    n = len(x)
    out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float32)
    nfft = 1 << int(np.ceil(np.log2(block + 2 * pad)))
    f = np.fft.rfftfreq(nfft, 1.0 / sr)
    f[0] = 1e-3
    H = np.ones_like(f)
    if hp:
        H = H / np.sqrt(1.0 + (hp / f) ** (2 * order))
    if lp:
        H = H / np.sqrt(1.0 + (f / lp) ** (2 * order))
    for s in range(0, n, block):
        e = min(n, s + block)
        a, b = max(0, s - pad), min(n, e + pad)
        y = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(x[a:b], nfft) * H, nfft)[:b - a]
        out[s:e] = y[s - a:e - a]
    return out


def _duck_gain(x, sr, depth_db, release_ms):
    n = len(x)
    blk = max(1, int(sr * 0.001))
    m = n // blk
    if m < 10 or depth_db <= 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float32)
    pk = np.abs(x[:m * blk]).reshape(m, blk).max(axis=1)
    att = np.exp(-1.0 / 3.0)
    rel = np.exp(-1.0 / max(release_ms, 20.0))
    env = np.empty(m)
    e = 0.0
    for i in range(m):
        c = att if pk[i] > e else rel
        e = c * e + (1.0 - c) * pk[i]
        env[i] = e
    env_db = 20.0 * np.log10(env + 1e-9)
    live = env > 1e-4
    ref_db = float(np.percentile(env_db[live], 75)) if live.any() else -20.0
    amt = np.clip((env_db - (ref_db - 20.0)) / 14.0, 0.0, 1.0)
    g = 10.0 ** (-depth_db * amt / 20.0)
    out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float32)
    xs = (np.arange(m) + 0.5) * blk
    for i in range(0, n, 1 << 20):
        j = min(n, i + (1 << 20))
        out[i:j] = np.interp(np.arange(i, j), xs, g)
    return out


def _band_weights(nfft, sr):
    f = np.fft.rfftfreq(nfft, 1.0 / sr)
    f[0] = 1.0
    lf = np.log(f)
    lc = np.log(_CENTERS)
    k = len(lc)
    W = np.zeros((k, f.size))
    for i in range(k):
        if i == 0:
            W[i] = np.interp(lf, [lc[0], lc[1]], [1.0, 0.0], left=1.0, right=0.0)
        elif i == k - 1:
            W[i] = np.interp(lf, [lc[-2], lc[-1]], [0.0, 1.0], left=0.0, right=1.0)
        else:
            W[i] = np.interp(lf, [lc[i - 1], lc[i], lc[i + 1]], [0.0, 1.0, 0.0], left=0.0, right=0.0)
    return W


def make_ir(sr, rt60, pre_ms, er_db=-9.0, hf_damp=0.55, lf_mult=0.90, lm_mult=0.70, lm_dip_db=6.0, lm_hp=450.0, seed=7):
    key = (int(sr), round(float(rt60), 2), round(float(pre_ms), 1), round(float(er_db), 1),
           round(float(hf_damp), 2), round(float(lf_mult), 2), round(float(lm_mult), 2),
           round(float(lm_dip_db), 1), round(float(lm_hp), 0), int(seed))
    hit = _IR_CACHE.get(key)
    if hit is not None:
        return hit
    L = int(sr * min(3.6, rt60 * 1.15 + 0.1))
    t = np.arange(L) / float(sr)
    nfft = 1 << int(np.ceil(np.log2(L)))
    Wt = _band_weights(nfft, sr)
    mult = [lf_mult, lm_mult, 0.90, hf_damp, hf_damp * 0.6]
    shared = np.random.default_rng(seed).standard_normal(L)
    Xs = np.fft.rfft(shared, nfft)
    pre = int(round(pre_ms * sr / 1000.0))
    irs = []
    for ch in range(2):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + 101 * (ch + 1))
        Xn = np.fft.rfft(rng.standard_normal(L), nfft)
        tail = np.zeros(L)
        for b in range(len(mult)):
            Xb = (Xs if b == 0 else Xn) * Wt[b]
            tail += np.fft.irfft(Xb, nfft)[:L] * np.exp(-6.9078 * t / (rt60 * mult[b]))
        t0 = 0.010
        tail *= np.where(t < t0, 0.0, 1.0 - np.exp(-(t - t0) / 0.020))
        tail /= max(float(np.sqrt(np.sum(tail ** 2))), 1e-12)
        er = np.zeros(L)
        times = np.sort(rng.uniform(0.004, 0.055, 10))
        kern = np.hanning(max(3, int(0.0004 * sr)))
        for k, tm in enumerate(times):
            i0 = int(tm * sr)
            amp = (0.88 ** k) * (1.0 if rng.random() > 0.5 else -1.0)
            er[i0:i0 + kern.size] += amp * kern[:max(0, min(kern.size, L - i0))]
        fE = np.fft.rfftfreq(nfft, 1.0 / sr)
        hp_er = 1.0 - 1.0 / (1.0 + (np.maximum(fE, 1e-3) / 250.0) ** 4)
        er = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(er, nfft) * hp_er, nfft)[:L]
        e_er = float(np.sum(er ** 2))
        if e_er > 0:
            er *= np.sqrt(10.0 ** (er_db / 10.0) / e_er)
        h = tail + er
        fS = np.maximum(np.fft.rfftfreq(nfft, 1.0 / sr), 1e-3)
        shape = 1.0 / np.sqrt(1.0 + (lm_hp / fS) ** 4)
        shape *= 1.0 - (1.0 - 10.0 ** (-lm_dip_db / 20.0)) * np.exp(-0.5 * (np.log(fS / 330.0) / 0.55) ** 2)
        h = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(h, nfft) * shape, nfft)[:L]
        h /= max(float(np.sqrt(np.sum(h ** 2))), 1e-12)
        irs.append(np.concatenate([np.zeros(pre), h]))
    ir = np.stack(irs)
    if len(_IR_CACHE) > 24:
        _IR_CACHE.clear()
    _IR_CACHE[key] = ir
    return ir


def _conv_blocks(x, ir, block=1 << 16):
    n, L = len(x), ir.shape[1]
    nfft = 1 << int(np.ceil(np.log2(block + L - 1)))
    HL = np.fft.rfft(ir[0], nfft)
    HR = np.fft.rfft(ir[1], nfft)
    out = np.zeros((2, n + L - 1), dtype=np.float32)
    for s in range(0, n, block):
        seg = x[s:s + block]
        if float(np.max(np.abs(seg))) < 1e-7:
            continue
        X = np.fft.rfft(seg, nfft)
        m = len(seg) + L - 1
        out[0, s:s + m] += np.fft.irfft(X * HL, nfft)[:m]
        out[1, s:s + m] += np.fft.irfft(X * HR, nfft)[:m]
    return out


def render(voc, sr, bpm, preset="neutral", overrides=None, target_rms=None, out_len=None):
