    p = dict(REVERB_PRESETS.get(str(preset).lower(), REVERB_PRESETS["neutral"]))
    if overrides:
        p.update(overrides)
    x = _mono(voc)
    n = len(x)
    n_out = int(out_len) if out_len else n
    info = {"preset": str(preset), "rt60_s": None, "predelay_ms": None, "bpm_used": None,
            "density": None, "duck_db": None, "ir_len_s": None}
    wet = np.zeros((2, n_out), dtype=np.float32)
    if n < sr // 2 or float(np.max(np.abs(x))) < 1e-6:
        return wet, info
    w = int(0.05 * sr)
    k = n // w
    fr = 20.0 * np.log10(np.sqrt((x[:k * w].reshape(k, w) ** 2).mean(axis=1)) + 1e-12)
    act = fr > ACTIVE_GATE_DB
    dens = float(act.mean()) if k else 0.0
    b = fold_bpm(bpm)
    rt = float(p["rt60"]) * float(np.clip(100.0 / b, 0.8, 1.25))
    lvl = float(p["level_db"])
    duck = float(p["duck_db"])
    if dens > 0.75:
        rt *= 0.85
        lvl -= 1.5
    elif dens < 0.45:
        rt *= 1.15
        lvl += 1.5
    rt = float(np.clip(rt, RT60_MIN, RT60_MAX))
    pre_ms = float(np.clip(60000.0 / b / 16.0 * float(p["pd_scale"]), 18.0, 45.0))
    ir = make_ir(sr, rt, pre_ms, p["er_db"], p["hf_damp"], p["lf_mult"],
                 p.get("lm_mult", 0.70), p.get("lm_dip_db", 6.0), p.get("lm_hp", 450.0))
    send = _shape(x, sr, p["hp"], p["lp"])
    full = _conv_blocks(send, ir)
    m = min(full.shape[1], n_out)
    wet[:, :m] = full[:, :m]
    del full
    wd = float(np.clip(p["width"], 0.0, 1.0))
    if wd < 1.0:
        mid = (wet[0] + wet[1]) * 0.5
        side = (wet[0] - wet[1]) * 0.5 * wd
        wet[0] = mid + side
        wet[1] = mid - side
    mask = np.repeat(act, w)
    mask = np.concatenate([mask, np.zeros(max(0, n - mask.size), dtype=bool)])[:n]
    ref_send = _rms(send[mask]) if mask.any() else 1e-6
    wm = wet[:, :n][:, mask[:min(n, n_out)]] if mask.any() else wet
    cur_act = _rms(wm) if wm.size else 1e-6
    if cur_act > 1e-12:
        wet *= np.float32(ref_send * 10.0 ** (lvl / 20.0) / cur_act)
    release = float(np.clip(0.20 * rt * 1000.0, 120.0, 350.0))
    gain = _duck_gain(x, sr, duck, release)
    mm = min(n, n_out)
    wet[:, :mm] *= gain[None, :mm]
    if target_rms is not None and target_rms > 0:
        cur = _rms(wet)
        if cur > 1e-12:
            wet *= np.float32(target_rms / cur)
    peak = float(np.abs(wet).max())
    if peak > 0.5:
        wet *= np.float32(0.5 / peak)
    info.update({"rt60_s": round(rt, 2), "predelay_ms": round(pre_ms, 1), "bpm_used": round(b, 1),
                 "density": round(dens, 3), "duck_db": round(duck, 1), "ir_len_s": round(ir.shape[1] / sr, 2),
                 "release_ms": round(release)})
    return wet, info


==============================================================================
FILE: fx-api/api/song_sections.py   (269 lines)
==============================================================================

"""
song_sections.py -- adaptive section awareness (intro / verse / chorus / bridge / outro).
"""
import numpy as np
import scipy.signal as sg
from scipy import ndimage

MIN_BARS = 24
MIN_CONFIDENCE = 0.5
CTRL_RATE = 100.0
MAX_VOCAL_DB = 1.5
MAX_SEND_DB = 2.0
BAND_EDGES = np.geomspace(40, 12000, 17)
CHROMA_BINS = 12

def _mono(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    return x.mean(axis=0) if x.ndim == 2 else x

def _stft_pow(x, sr, n=2048, hop=512):
    f, t, S = sg.stft(x, sr, nperseg=n, noverlap=n - hop, window="hann", boundary=None, padded=False)
    return f, t, np.abs(S) ** 2

def _band_db(f, P):
    out = np.zeros((len(BAND_EDGES) - 1, P.shape[1]))
    for i in range(len(BAND_EDGES) - 1):
        out[i] = P[(f >= BAND_EDGES[i]) & (f < BAND_EDGES[i + 1])].sum(0)
    return 10 * np.log10(out + 1e-12)

def _onset_env(P_db_bands):
    d = np.maximum(np.diff(P_db_bands, axis=1, prepend=P_db_bands[:, :1]), 0).sum(0)
    return d

def estimate_tempo(env, frame_s):
    e = env - ndimage.uniform_filter1d(env, int(2.0 / frame_s))
    ac = sg.correlate(e, e, mode="full", method="fft")[len(e) - 1:]
    lags = np.arange(len(ac))
    sel = (lags >= int(0.3 / frame_s)) & (lags <= int(1.0 / frame_s))
    k = lags[sel][np.argmax(ac[sel])]
    return 60.0 / (k * frame_s)

def downbeat_phase(env, frame_s, bar_s):
    nb = int(len(env) * frame_s / bar_s) - 1
    cands = np.arange(0.0, bar_s, 0.01); scores = np.zeros(len(cands))
    for j, cand in enumerate(cands):
        idx = ((cand + np.arange(nb) * bar_s) / frame_s).astype(int)
        scores[j] = env[idx[idx < len(env)]].sum()
    top = scores.max()
    near = cands[scores >= 0.93 * top]
    thr = np.percentile(env[env > 0], 90) if (env > 0).any() else 0.0
    first = np.argmax(env > thr) * frame_s
    ph0 = first % bar_s
    d = np.minimum(np.abs(near - ph0), bar_s - np.abs(near - ph0))
    return float(near[np.argmin(d)])

def _bar_features(beat, voc, sr, bpm):
    hop = 512; frame_s = hop / sr
    fb, tb, Pb = _stft_pow(_mono(beat), sr)
    fv, tv, Pv = _stft_pow(_mono(voc), sr)
    n = min(Pb.shape[1], Pv.shape[1]); Pb, Pv, tb = Pb[:, :n], Pv[:, :n], tb[:n]
    Bb = _band_db(fb, Pb)
    env = _onset_env(Bb)
    if bpm is None or not (50 <= bpm <= 210):
        bpm = estimate_tempo(env, frame_s)
        while bpm < 70: bpm *= 2
        while bpm > 150: bpm /= 2
    bar_s = 4 * 60.0 / bpm
    phase = downbeat_phase(env, frame_s, bar_s)
    nbar = int((n * frame_s - phase) / bar_s)
    Vv = _band_db(fv, Pv)
    vrms = 10 * np.log10(Pv.sum(0) + 1e-12)
    vact = vrms > (np.percentile(vrms, 95) - 35.0)
    sel = (fv >= 90) & (fv <= 1000)
    pc = np.round(12 * np.log2(np.maximum(fv, 1.0) / 440.0) + 69).astype(int) % 12
    cent = (fv[:, None] * Pv).sum(0) / (Pv.sum(0) + 1e-12)
    feats = {"beat_timbre": [], "beat_rms": [], "beat_density": [], "voc_rms": [], "voc_act": [],
             "voc_cent": [], "voc_chroma": [], "voc_rate": []}
    venv = _onset_env(Vv)
    for b in range(nbar):
        a = int((phase + b * bar_s) / frame_s); z = int((phase + (b + 1) * bar_s) / frame_s)
        z = min(max(z, a + 2), n)
        feats["beat_timbre"].append(Bb[:, a:z].mean(1))
        feats["beat_rms"].append(10 * np.log10(Pb[:, a:z].sum(0).mean() + 1e-12))
        feats["beat_density"].append(float((env[a:z] > np.percentile(env, 75)).mean()))
        act = vact[a:z]
        feats["voc_act"].append(float(act.mean()))
        if act.sum() >= 3:
            feats["voc_rms"].append(float(vrms[a:z][act].mean()))
            feats["voc_cent"].append(float(np.log2(cent[a:z][act].mean() + 1.0)))
            ch = np.zeros(12)
            for k in range(12):
                ch[k] = Pv[sel & (pc == k)][:, a:z][:, act].sum()
            feats["voc_chroma"].append(ch / (ch.sum() + 1e-12))
            feats["voc_rate"].append(float((venv[a:z] > np.percentile(venv, 85)).mean()))
        else:
            feats["voc_rms"].append(np.nan); feats["voc_cent"].append(np.nan)
            feats["voc_chroma"].append(np.zeros(12)); feats["voc_rate"].append(0.0)
    out = {k: np.array(v) for k, v in feats.items()}
    out.update(bpm=float(bpm), bar_s=float(bar_s), phase=float(phase), nbar=int(nbar))
    return out

def _z(x):
    x = np.array(x, dtype=float)
    ok = np.isfinite(x)
    if ok.sum() < 3:
        return np.zeros_like(x)
    m, s = x[ok].mean(), x[ok].std() + 1e-9
    z = (x - m) / s
    z[~ok] = 0.0
    return z

def _feature_matrix(F):
    tim = F["beat_timbre"] - F["beat_timbre"].mean(0)
    tim = tim / (tim.std(0, keepdims=True) + 1e-6)
    tim = tim / np.sqrt(tim.shape[1])
    cols = [0.9 * tim, 0.6 * _z(F["beat_rms"])[:, None], 0.4 * _z(F["beat_density"])[:, None],
            0.7 * _z(F["voc_rms"])[:, None], 0.8 * _z(F["voc_act"])[:, None], 0.5 * _z(F["voc_cent"])[:, None],
            0.6 * _z(F["voc_rate"])[:, None]]
    ch = F["voc_chroma"]
    cols.append(0.9 * (ch - ch.mean(0)) / (ch.std(0) + 1e-6) / np.sqrt(12))
    return np.hstack(cols)

def _ssm(X):
    Xn = X / (np.linalg.norm(X, axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-9)
    S = Xn @ Xn.T
    return S

def _novelty(S, K=4):
    n = S.shape[0]
    g = np.exp(-0.5 * (np.arange(-K, K) + 0.5) ** 2 / (K / 2.0) ** 2)
    sign = np.sign(np.arange(-K, K) + 0.5)
    Kern = np.outer(sign * g, sign * g)
    pad = np.pad(S, K, mode="edge")
    nov = np.zeros(n)
    for i in range(n):
        nov[i] = (pad[i: i + 2 * K, i: i + 2 * K] * Kern).sum()
    return nov

def _boundaries(S, min_len=4):
    nov = _novelty(S, 4)
    nov = ndimage.gaussian_filter1d(nov, 0.6)
    thr = nov.mean() + 0.35 * nov.std()
    p, _ = sg.find_peaks(nov, height=thr, distance=min_len)
    b = [0] + [int(x) for x in p if min_len <= x <= S.shape[0] - min_len] + [S.shape[0]]
    return sorted(set(b)), nov

def _merge_short(b, X, min_len=4):
    changed = True
    while changed and len(b) > 2:
        changed = False
        for i in range(len(b) - 1):
            if b[i + 1] - b[i] < min_len:
                if i == 0: del b[1]
                elif i == len(b) - 2: del b[i]
                else:
                    sl = X[b[i - 1]:b[i]].mean(0); sr_ = X[b[i + 1]:b[i + 2]].mean(0); me = X[b[i]:b[i + 1]].mean(0)
                    cs = lambda a, c: a @ c / (np.linalg.norm(a) * np.linalg.norm(c) + 1e-9)
                    j = i if cs(me, sl) >= cs(me, sr_) else i + 1
                    del b[j]
                changed = True
                break
    return b

def _group(segvecs, thr=0.55):
    gid = []; protos = []
    cs = lambda a, c: float(a @ c / (np.linalg.norm(a) * np.linalg.norm(c) + 1e-9))
    for v in segvecs:
        best, bi = -1.0, -1
        for i, p in enumerate(protos):
            s = cs(v, p)
            if s > best: best, bi = s, i
        if best >= thr: gid.append(bi)
        else: protos.append(v); gid.append(len(protos) - 1)
    return np.array(gid)

def analyze(beat, voc, sr, bpm=None):
    res = {"sections": [], "confidence": 0.0, "strength": 0.0, "reason": "", "bars": 0}
    b = np.asarray(beat); v = np.asarray(voc)
    if b.size == 0 or v.size == 0 or b.shape[-1] < sr * 30:
        res["reason"] = "too short"; return res
    try:
        F = _bar_features(beat, voc, sr, bpm)
    except Exception as e:
        res["reason"] = "feature extraction failed: %s" % str(e)[:60]; return res
    nb = F["nbar"]; res.update(bpm=round(F["bpm"], 1), bars=nb)
    if nb < MIN_BARS:
        res["reason"] = "only %d bars (need %d)" % (nb, MIN_BARS); return res
    if (F["voc_act"] > 0.25).mean() < 0.25:
        res["reason"] = "vocal is active in too few bars"; return res
    X = _feature_matrix(F)
    S = _ssm(X)
    bnd, nov = _boundaries(S)
    bnd = _merge_short(bnd, X)
    segs = [(bnd[i], bnd[i + 1]) for i in range(len(bnd) - 1)]
    inten = 0.35 * _z(F["beat_rms"]) + 0.15 * _z(F["beat_density"]) + 0.30 * _z(F["voc_rms"]) + 0.20 * _z(F["voc_cent"])
    inten = ndimage.uniform_filter1d(inten, 2)
    segvec = np.array([X[a:z].mean(0) for a, z in segs])
    gid = _group(segvec)
    sact = np.array([F["voc_act"][a:z].mean() for a, z in segs])
    sint = np.array([inten[a:z].mean() for a, z in segs])
    counts = np.bincount(gid, minlength=gid.max() + 1)
    labels = ["verse"] * len(segs)
    for i, (a, z) in enumerate(segs):
        if sact[i] < 0.2: labels[i] = "break"
    if sact[0] < 0.25 or segs[0][1] <= 12 and sact[0] < 0.4: labels[0] = "intro"
    if len(segs) > 1 and (sact[-1] < 0.25): labels[-1] = "outro"
    cand = [g for g in range(len(counts)) if counts[g] >= 2 and np.mean([sact[i] for i in range(len(segs)) if gid[i] == g]) > 0.4]
    if cand:
        chorus_g = max(cand, key=lambda g: np.mean([sint[i] for i in range(len(segs)) if gid[i] == g]))
        for i in range(len(segs)):
            if gid[i] == chorus_g and labels[i] == "verse": labels[i] = "chorus"
    for i in range(len(segs)):
        if labels[i] == "verse" and counts[gid[i]] == 1 and i > len(segs) // 2 and 0 < i < len(segs) - 1 and sint[i] < np.median(sint):
            labels[i] = "bridge"
    inside = np.mean([S[a:z, a:z].mean() for a, z in segs]) if segs else 0.0
    mask = np.ones_like(S, bool)
    for a, z in segs: mask[a:z, a:z] = False
    between = S[mask].mean() if mask.any() else 0.0
    contrast = float(inside - between)
    c_struct = float(np.clip((contrast - 0.08) / 0.30, 0, 1))
    c_len = float(np.clip((len(segs) - 2) / 3.0, 0, 1)) * float(np.clip((nb - MIN_BARS + 8) / 24.0, 0, 1))
    c_rep = 1.0 if cand else 0.0
    c_voc = float(np.clip(((F["voc_act"] > 0.25).mean() - 0.2) / 0.3, 0, 1))
    conf = float((c_struct * c_len * max(c_rep, 0.05) * c_voc) ** 0.25)
    res.update(confidence=round(conf, 3), parts={"structure": round(c_struct, 2), "segments": round(c_len, 2), "repeats": c_rep, "vocal": round(c_voc, 2)}, contrast=round(contrast, 3))
    bs = F["bar_s"]; ph = F["phase"]
    res["sections"] = [{"label": labels[i], "start_s": round(ph + a * bs, 2), "end_s": round(ph + z * bs, 2), "bars": int(z - a), "group": int(gid[i]), "vocal_activity": round(float(sact[i]), 2), "intensity": round(float(sint[i]), 2)} for i, (a, z) in enumerate(segs)]
    res["_internal"] = {"bar_s": bs, "phase": ph, "labels": labels, "segs": segs, "intensity": inten, "nbar": nb}
    if conf < MIN_CONFIDENCE:
        res["reason"] = "structure not clear enough (confidence %.2f): neutral curves" % conf
        res["strength"] = 0.0
    else:
        res["strength"] = float(np.clip((conf - MIN_CONFIDENCE) / 0.3, 0, 1))
        res["reason"] = "sections detected"
    return res

LIFT = {"chorus": (+1.0, +1.5, +1.5, +0.06), "verse": (0.0, -1.0, -1.0, -0.03), "bridge": (-0.3, +0.5, 0.0, 0.0), "intro": (0.0, +1.0, 0.0, 0.0), "outro": (0.0, +1.0, 0.0, 0.0), "break": (0.0, +0.5, 0.0, 0.0)}

def control_curves(res, n_samples, sr):
    n = int(np.ceil(n_samples / sr * CTRL_RATE)) + 1
    zero = lambda: np.zeros(n)
    out = {"rate": CTRL_RATE, "vocal_db": zero(), "send_db": zero(), "double_db": zero(), "width": zero(), "strength": res.get("strength", 0.0)}
    if res.get("strength", 0.0) <= 0 or "_internal" not in res:
        return out
    I = res["_internal"]; bs, ph = I["bar_s"], I["phase"]
    t = np.arange(n) / CTRL_RATE
    cont = np.interp(t, ph + (np.arange(I["nbar"]) + 0.5) * bs, I["intensity"])
    vals = {k: np.zeros(n) for k in ("vocal_db", "send_db", "double_db", "width")}
    for lab, (a, z) in zip(I["labels"], I["segs"]):
        m = ((t >= ph + a * bs) & (t < ph + z * bs)).astype(float)
        for key, val in zip(("vocal_db", "send_db", "double_db", "width"), LIFT[lab]):
            vals[key] += m * val
    xf = int(round(bs * CTRL_RATE))
    for k in vals:
        v = ndimage.gaussian_filter1d(vals[k], xf / 2.5)
        if k == "vocal_db":
            v = v + 0.35 * np.clip(ndimage.gaussian_filter1d(cont, xf / 4.0), -1.5, 1.5)
        out[k] = v * res["strength"]
    out["vocal_db"] = np.clip(out["vocal_db"], -MAX_VOCAL_DB, MAX_VOCAL_DB)
    out["send_db"] = np.clip(out["send_db"], -MAX_SEND_DB, MAX_SEND_DB)
    out["double_db"] = np.clip(out["double_db"], -MAX_SEND_DB, MAX_SEND_DB)
    out["width"] = np.clip(out["width"], -0.08, 0.08)
    return out

def apply_gain_db(x, sr, curve_db, rate=CTRL_RATE):
    a = np.asarray(x)
    n = a.shape[-1]
    g = 10 ** (np.interp(np.arange(n) / sr, np.arange(len(curve_db)) / rate, curve_db) / 20.0)
    return (a * g).astype(a.dtype if np.issubdtype(a.dtype, np.floating) else np.float32)


==============================================================================
FILE: fx-api/api/vocal_align.py   (167 lines)
==============================================================================

"""
vocal_align.py -- MODULE 1: adaptive phase alignment (vocal to beat).
"""
import numpy as np
import scipy.signal as sg
from scipy import ndimage

HOP_S = 0.001
DEADBAND_MS = 1.0
MIN_CONFIDENCE = 0.55
MIN_ONSETS = 30

def _mono(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    return x.mean(axis=0) if x.ndim == 2 else x

def _onset_env(x, sr, bands):
    hop = max(1, int(round(HOP_S * sr)))
    nper = 1 << int(np.ceil(np.log2(max(64, 0.006 * sr))))
    f, t, S = sg.stft(x, sr, nperseg=nper, noverlap=nper - hop, window="hann", boundary=None, padded=False)
    P = np.abs(S) ** 2
    out = np.zeros(P.shape[1])
    for lo, hi, w in bands:
        e = P[(f >= lo) & (f < min(hi, sr / 2 * 0.98))].sum(axis=0) + 1e-12
        le = 10 * np.log10(e)
        le = ndimage.uniform_filter1d(le, 3)
        rise = np.maximum(le[3:] - le[:-3], 0.0)
        rise = np.concatenate([np.zeros(3), rise])
        scale = np.median(rise[rise > 0]) if (rise > 0).any() else 1.0
        out += w * rise / (scale + 1e-9)
    out = out - ndimage.uniform_filter1d(out, 201)
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0), t[0] if len(t) else 0.0

VOCAL_BANDS = [(300, 1500, 1.0), (1500, 8000, 1.0)]
BEAT_BANDS = [(40, 150, 1.0), (150, 600, 0.8), (2000, 9000, 0.7)]

def _syllables(env):
    e = ndimage.gaussian_filter1d(env, 8.0)
    pos = e[e > 0]
    if pos.size < 10: return np.array([], dtype=int)
    p, _ = sg.find_peaks(e, height=np.percentile(pos, 85), distance=int(0.09 / HOP_S))
    return p

def vocal_density(voc, sr):
    m = _mono(voc)
    env, _ = _onset_env(m, sr, VOCAL_BANDS)
    pk = _syllables(env)
    n = int(0.05 * sr); k = len(m) // n
    e = 20 * np.log10(np.sqrt((m[: k * n].reshape(k, n) ** 2).mean(1)) + 1e-9)
    active_s = max(float((e > np.percentile(e, 95) - 35).sum()) * 0.05, 1.0)
    return len(pk) / active_s, pk, env

def max_shift_for(rate):
    return float(np.interp(rate, [2.5, 5.5], [22.0, 8.0]))

def _lag_sums(v, b, W, starts):
    n = len(v); out = np.zeros((len(starts), 2 * W + 1))
    for j, L in enumerate(range(-W, W + 1)):
        if L >= 0:
            prod = np.zeros(n); prod[: n - L] = v[L:] * b[: n - L]
        else:
            prod = np.zeros(n); prod[-L:] = v[: n + L] * b[-L:]
        out[:, j] = np.add.reduceat(prod, starts)
    return out

def _peak_lag(c, W, sigma_ms):
    c = ndimage.gaussian_filter1d(c, sigma_ms / (HOP_S * 1000))
    k = int(np.argmax(c))
    if 0 < k < len(c) - 1:
        a, b, d = c[k - 1], c[k], c[k + 1]; den = a - 2 * b + d
        kk = k + 0.5 * (a - d) / den if abs(den) > 1e-15 else float(k)
    else:
        kk = float(k)
    return (kk - W) * HOP_S * 1000.0, c, k

def estimate(voc, beat, sr, max_shift_ms=None):
    m_v, m_b = _mono(voc), _mono(beat)
    rate, pk, env_v = vocal_density(m_v, sr)
    lim = max_shift_ms if max_shift_ms is not None else max_shift_for(rate)
    env_b, _ = _onset_env(m_b, sr, BEAT_BANDS)
    n = min(len(env_v), len(env_b))
    v = ndimage.gaussian_filter1d(env_v[:n], 2.0); b = ndimage.gaussian_filter1d(env_b[:n], 2.0)
    v = (v - v.mean()) / (v.std() + 1e-9); b = (b - b.mean()) / (b.std() + 1e-9)
    W = int(round((lim + 6.0) / (HOP_S * 1000)))
    blk = int(2.5 / HOP_S); starts = np.arange(0, n - blk // 2, blk)
    S = _lag_sums(v, b, W, starts)
    c_all = S.sum(0) / n
    lag, cs, k = _peak_lag(c_all, W, 3.0)
    peak = cs[k]
    rng = np.random.default_rng(1234); nulls = []
    for _ in range(30):
        sh = int(rng.uniform(3.0, max(3.5, n * HOP_S - 3.0)) / HOP_S)
        Sn = _lag_sums(np.roll(v, sh), b, W, starts)
        nulls.append(ndimage.gaussian_filter1d(Sn.sum(0) / n, 3.0 / (HOP_S * 1000)).max())
    null95 = float(np.percentile(nulls, 95))
    ratio = float(peak / (null95 + 1e-9))
    lags = []
    for _ in range(80):
        idx = rng.integers(0, len(starts), len(starts))
        lags.append(_peak_lag(S[idx].sum(0) / n, W, 3.0)[0])
    boot = float(np.std(lags))
    edge = float(max(cs[0], cs[-1]) / (peak + 1e-12))
    at_edge = abs(lag) > lim + 4.5
    c_sig = float(np.clip((ratio - 1.3) / 1.2, 0, 1))
    c_boot = float(np.clip((4.0 - boot) / 3.0, 0, 1))
    c_edge = float(np.clip((0.85 - edge) / 0.35, 0, 1))
    c_n = float(np.clip((len(pk) - MIN_ONSETS + 25) / 50.0, 0, 1))
    conf = float((c_sig * c_boot * c_edge * c_n) ** 0.25)
    return {"offset_ms": float(lag), "confidence": round(conf, 3),
            "parts": {"significant": round(c_sig, 2), "stable": round(c_boot, 2), "inside_window": round(c_edge, 2), "evidence": round(c_n, 2)},
            "peak_vs_chance": round(ratio, 2), "bootstrap_std_ms": round(boot, 2), "onsets": int(len(pk)),
            "attacks_per_s": round(float(rate), 2), "max_shift_ms": round(float(lim), 1), "at_search_edge": bool(at_edge)}

def shift_ms(x, sr, ms):
    s = ms * sr / 1000.0
    ia = int(np.floor(s)); fr = s - ia
    a = np.asarray(x)
    one = a.ndim == 1
    A = a[None, :] if one else a
    out = np.zeros(A.shape, dtype=np.float64)
    if fr > 1e-6:
        taps = 32
        k = np.arange(-taps + 1, taps + 1) - fr
        h = np.sinc(k) * np.kaiser(2 * taps, 9.0)
        h /= h.sum()
        for ch in range(A.shape[0]):
            y = sg.fftconvolve(A[ch].astype(np.float64), h[::-1], mode="full")
            y = y[taps - 1: taps - 1 + A.shape[1]]
            out[ch] = y
    else:
        out = A.astype(np.float64).copy()
    if ia > 0:
        out = np.concatenate([out[:, ia:], np.zeros((out.shape[0], ia))], axis=1)
    elif ia < 0:
        out = np.concatenate([np.zeros((out.shape[0], -ia)), out[:, :out.shape[1] + ia]], axis=1)
    out = out.astype(a.dtype if np.issubdtype(a.dtype, np.floating) else np.float32)
    return out[0] if one else out

def align(voc, beat, sr, groove_ms=0.0, max_shift_ms=None, min_confidence=MIN_CONFIDENCE):
    v = np.asarray(voc)
    if v.size == 0 or np.asarray(beat).size == 0 or v.shape[-1] < sr * 8:
        return voc, {"action": "abort", "reason": "too short to measure"}
    try:
        est = estimate(voc, beat, sr, max_shift_ms)
    except Exception as e:
        return voc, {"action": "abort", "reason": "measurement failed: %s" % str(e)[:80]}
    rep = dict(est)
    off = est["offset_ms"] - groove_ms
    rep["measured_offset_ms"] = round(est["offset_ms"], 2)
    if est["onsets"] < MIN_ONSETS:
        rep.update(action="abort", reason="too few syllable attacks (%d) to trust the lock" % est["onsets"]); return voc, rep
    if est["confidence"] < min_confidence:
        rep.update(action="abort", reason="low confidence %.2f" % est["confidence"]); return voc, rep
    if est["at_search_edge"] or abs(off) > est["max_shift_ms"]:
        rep.update(action="abort", reason="needed shift exceeds limit"); return voc, rep
    if abs(off) < DEADBAND_MS:
        rep.update(action="bypass", reason="already within %.1f ms" % DEADBAND_MS, applied_ms=0.0); return voc, rep
    out = shift_ms(voc, sr, off)
    rep.update(action="aligned", applied_ms=round(-off, 2), reason="vocal was %.1f ms %s the beat" % (abs(off), "behind" if off > 0 else "ahead of"))
    return out, rep

def align_group(stems, beat, sr, lead_index=0, **kw):
    out0, rep = align(stems[lead_index], beat, sr, **kw)
    if rep.get("action") != "aligned":
        return list(stems), rep
    ms = -rep["applied_ms"]
    return [shift_ms(s, sr, ms) for s in stems], rep


==============================================================================
FILE: fx-api/api/vocal_fix.py   (57 lines)
==============================================================================

"""vocal_fix.py -- vocal 'forwardness' guard for the Afrobeats auto-mix.

Problem it solves: the engine pushes vocal presence (raise + beat ducking) until the vocal sits far
above the beat in 500 Hz-4 kHz. Commercial Afrobeats mixes (5 references, measured as acapella
power / (master - acapella), 250 Hz-16 kHz) keep the vocal much closer to the beat there.

Call it on the PROCESSED vocal bus (`core`) and the PROCESSED beat (`ducked`) right before they
are summed.  It only cuts a band when the vocal is over its limit, cuts at most `cap_db` per band,
never changes overall gain, and leaves the vocal alone if the vocal is quiet there already.
Pure numpy/scipy."""
import numpy as np
import scipy.signal as sg
from adaptive_tone import _peak

BANDS = {
    "500-2k": (500, 2000, 10.3),
}

def _band_pow(x, sr, N=8192):
    m = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    m = m.mean(axis=0) if m.ndim == 2 else m
    k = len(m) // N
    X = np.abs(np.fft.rfft(m[:k * N].reshape(k, N) * np.hanning(N), axis=1)) ** 2
    f = np.fft.rfftfreq(N, 1.0 / sr)
    return {n: X[:, (f >= lo) & (f < hi)].sum(1) for n, (lo, hi, _) in BANDS.items()}, X.sum(1)

def measure(voc, rest, sr):
    n = min(np.shape(voc)[-1], np.shape(rest)[-1])
    pv, tv = _band_pow(np.asarray(voc)[..., :n], sr)
    pr, _ = _band_pow(np.asarray(rest)[..., :n], sr)
    k = min(len(tv), len(next(iter(pr.values()))))
    e = 10 * np.log10(tv[:k] + 1e-20)
    act = e > (np.percentile(e, 95) - 30.0)
    if act.sum() < 10:
        act = np.ones(k, bool)
    return {b: float(10 * np.log10((pv[b][:k][act].sum() + 1e-20) / (pr[b][:k][act].sum() + 1e-20))) for b in BANDS}

def plan(voc, rest, sr, margin_db=0.5, gain=0.85, cap_db=1.5):
    m = measure(voc, rest, sr)
    cuts = {}
    for b, (lo, hi, lim) in BANDS.items():
        over = m[b] - lim
        if over > margin_db:
            cuts[b] = -float(np.clip(gain * over, 0.0, cap_db))
    return m, cuts

def forward_guard(voc, rest, sr, **kw):
    a = np.asarray(voc, dtype=np.float32)
    m, cuts = plan(a, rest, sr, **kw)
    if not cuts:
        return a, {"measured": {k: round(v, 1) for k, v in m.items()}, "cuts_db": {}}
    sos = [_peak(float(np.sqrt(BANDS[b][0] * BANDS[b][1])), g, 1.0, sr) for b, g in cuts.items()]
    y = sg.sosfilt(np.vstack(sos), a, axis=-1).astype(np.float32)
    m2 = measure(y, rest, sr)
    return y, {"measured": {k: round(v, 1) for k, v in m.items()},
               "cuts_db": {k: round(v, 2) for k, v in cuts.items()},
               "after": {k: round(v, 1) for k, v in m2.items()}}


==============================================================================
FILE: fx-api/api/vocal_fx_detect.py   (298 lines)
==============================================================================

"""
vocal_fx_detect.py -- is this vocal ALREADY processed?  Stops the engine from stacking a second
reverb / delay / widening / tuning on top of effects the artist (or their DAW) already printed.

It measures; it does not guess.  Every effect needs SEVERAL independent signs before it is called, and
the engine is told to bypass only when the evidence is overwhelming.  Anything unclear = "normal":
the engine behaves exactly as it does today.

    a = analyze(voc, sr, bpm=None)       # voc: (N,) or (C,N) float
    a["reverb"]["confidence"], a["delay"]["delay_ms"], a["stereo"]["width"], a["pitch"]["tuned"] ...
    plan = engine_plan(a)                # what to change in the engine, per effect

    plan = {"reverb_send_scale": 0.0..1.0, "delay_send_scale": 0.0..1.0,
            "skip_adt": bool, "skip_widen": bool, "skip_pitch_correct": bool, "reasons": [...]}
      scale 1.0 = engine as usual, 0.0 = bypass (only when confidence >= BYPASS_AT),
      in between = reduce (confidence 0.55 .. BYPASS_AT).

numpy/scipy only, stateless, deterministic.
"""
import numpy as np
import scipy.signal as sg
from scipy import ndimage

HOP = 0.01
BYPASS_AT = 0.90          # bypass an effect only at/above this confidence
REDUCE_AT = 0.55          # start reducing the engine's own effect above this


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ shared front end
def _prep(voc, sr):
    x = np.asarray(voc, dtype=np.float64)
    st = x if x.ndim == 2 else np.stack([x, x])
    st = st[:2]
    m = st.mean(0)
    n = int(HOP * sr)
    sos = sg.butter(4, [150, 6000], btype="band", fs=sr, output="sos")
    b = sg.sosfilt(sos, m)
    k = len(b) // n
    e = 20 * np.log10(np.sqrt((b[: k * n].reshape(k, n) ** 2).mean(1)) + 1e-9)
    top = float(np.percentile(e, 95)); floor = float(np.percentile(e, 5))
    act = e > max(top - 32.0, floor + 12.0)
    act = ndimage.binary_closing(act, np.ones(8)); act = ndimage.binary_opening(act, np.ones(5))
    lab, nl = ndimage.label(act)
    runs = [(int(np.where(lab == j)[0][0]), int(np.where(lab == j)[0][-1])) for j in range(1, nl + 1)]
    return {"st": st, "m": m, "e": e, "act": act, "runs": runs, "top": top, "floor": floor, "sr": sr}


def _conf(*parts):
    p = np.clip(np.array(parts, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    return float(np.prod(p) ** (1.0 / len(p))) if (p > 0).all() else float(np.prod(p))


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ reverb
def detect_reverb(P):
    e, runs, floor = P["e"], P["runs"], P["floor"]
    rows = []
    for i, (a, z) in enumerate(runs):
        if z - a < 25:
            continue
        nxt = runs[i + 1][0] if i + 1 < len(runs) else len(e)
        if nxt - z < 90:                                   # need ~0.9 s of quiet to look at the tail
            continue
        loc = float(np.median(e[z + 62: nxt - 3])) if nxt - z > 70 else floor
        seg = e[z + 1: z + 55] - loc                       # tail above the local floor
        # monotone lower envelope: a delay's repeats put bumps on the tail, reverb fills the valleys between them
        seg = np.minimum.accumulate(ndimage.uniform_filter1d(seg, 5, mode="nearest"))
        a_ex = float(seg[11:25].mean()); b_ex = float(seg[25:45].mean())
        tt = np.arange(8, 45) * HOP; yy = seg[8:45]
        sl, ic = np.polyfit(tt, yy, 1)
        r2 = 1 - ((yy - (sl * tt + ic)) ** 2).sum() / (((yy - yy.mean()) ** 2).sum() + 1e-9)
        rt = float(np.clip(-60.0 / sl, 0.1, 5.0)) if sl < -1 else None
        ok = a_ex >= 9.0 and b_ex >= 3.0 and a_ex > b_ex and sl < -6.0 and r2 >= 0.55
        rows.append((a_ex, b_ex, sl, r2, rt, ok))
    n = len(rows)
    out = {"present": False, "confidence": 0.0, "phrases_checked": n, "rt60_s": None, "tail_db": None, "consistency": 0.0}
    if n < 3:
        out["why"] = "too few phrase endings with silence after them to judge"
        return out
    R = np.array([(r[0], r[1], r[2], r[3]) for r in rows]); okm = np.array([r[5] for r in rows])
    frac = float(okm.mean())
    good = R[okm] if okm.any() else R
    rts = [r[4] for r, k in zip(rows, okm) if k and r[4]]
    out.update(tail_db=round(float(np.median(R[:, 0])), 1), consistency=round(frac, 2),
               rt60_s=round(float(np.median(rts)), 2) if rts else None)
    c1 = (np.median(good[:, 0]) - 8.0) / 12.0          # how far the tail sits above the noise floor
    c2 = (frac - 0.35) / 0.40                          # how many phrase endings show it
    c3 = (np.median(good[:, 3]) - 0.5) / 0.35          # how clean the exponential decay is
    c4 = (okm.sum() - 2) / 4.0                         # how many independent examples
    out["confidence"] = round(_conf(c1, c2, c3, c4), 3) if okm.any() else 0.0
    out["present"] = out["confidence"] >= REDUCE_AT
    return out


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ delay / echo
def _lag_profile(P, hi_ms=1700):
    """Level-normalised autocorrelation of the band-limited vocal.  An echo y(t)=x(t)+g*x(t-D) leaves a sharp
    peak at D (and k*D for feedback); natural singing has no coherence at 60 ms..1.7 s."""
    sr = P["sr"]
    sos = sg.butter(4, [200, 5000], btype="band", fs=sr, output="sos")
    b = sg.sosfilt(sos, P["m"])
    n = int(0.02 * sr); k = len(b) // n
    fr = b[: k * n].reshape(k, n)
    g = np.sqrt((fr ** 2).mean(1, keepdims=True))
    fr = np.where(g > 1e-4, fr / (g + 1e-12), 0.0)          # every frame the same loudness, silence stays silent
    b = fr.reshape(-1)
    N = 1 << int(np.ceil(np.log2(len(b) * 2)))
    X = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    ac = np.fft.irfft(np.abs(X) ** 2)[: int(hi_ms / 1000 * sr)]
    return ac / (ac[0] + 1e-12)


def detect_delay(P, bpm=None):
    sr = P["sr"]
    ac = _lag_profile(P)
    lo = int(0.08 * sr); hi = len(ac) - int(0.05 * sr)      # keep away from both ends (edge artefacts)
    a = ac[lo:hi]
    w = int(0.06 * sr) | 1
    med = sg.medfilt(np.abs(a), w)
    mad = sg.medfilt(np.abs(np.abs(a) - med), w) + 1e-9
    z = (a - med) / (1.4826 * mad)
    idx = sg.find_peaks(z, height=6.0, distance=int(0.02 * sr))[0]
    idx = idx[a[idx] >= 0.02]
    out = {"present": False, "confidence": 0.0, "delay_ms": None, "peaks": len(idx)}
    if len(idx) == 0:
        out["why"] = "no echo peaks in the long-lag autocorrelation"
        return out
    lag = (idx + lo) / sr * 1000.0; amp = a[idx]; zz = z[idx]
    best = None
    for D in lag:                                            # try every peak as the fundamental delay time
        mem = [(i, kk) for i, l in enumerate(lag) for kk in range(1, 8) if abs(l - kk * D) <= max(8.0, 0.02 * kk * D)]
        if not mem:
            continue
        ii = sorted({i for i, _ in mem}); ks = sorted({kk for _, kk in mem})
        strong = float(amp[ii].max())
        score = (len(ks), strong, -D)
        if best is None or score > best[0]:
            best = (score, D, ii, ks)
    _, D, ii, ks = best
    nmem = len(ks); strong = float(amp[ii].max()); zs = float(np.median(zz[ii]))
    c_amp = (strong - 0.03) / 0.12                           # a clean echo is >0.1; chance is <0.05
    c_z = (zs - 5.0) / 8.0
    c_cnt = (nmem - 1) / 2.0 if nmem > 1 else (1.0 if strong >= 0.25 else 0.0)   # repeats, or one very strong tap
    conf = _conf(c_amp, c_z, c_cnt)
    ms = float(np.mean([lag[i] / kk for i, kk in [(i, kk) for i, kk in
               [(i, kk) for i in ii for kk in range(1, 8) if abs(lag[i] - kk * D) <= max(8.0, 0.02 * kk * D)]]]))
    synced = None
    if bpm and 50 <= bpm <= 210:
        beat = 60000.0 / bpm; fr = ms / beat
        synced = any(abs(fr - q) / q < 0.05 for q in (0.25, 1 / 3, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0))
    fb = None
    amps = {kk: float(amp[i]) for i in ii for kk in range(1, 8) if abs(lag[i] - kk * D) <= max(8.0, 0.02 * kk * D)}
    rat = [amps[k + 1] / amps[k] for k in amps if k + 1 in amps and amps[k] > 0 and amps[k + 1] < amps[k]]
    if rat: fb = round(float(np.median(rat)), 2)
    return {"present": conf >= REDUCE_AT, "confidence": round(conf, 3), "delay_ms": round(ms, 0), "peaks": len(idx),
            "repeats": nmem, "strongest_peak": round(strong, 3), "feedback_est": fb, "tempo_synced": synced}


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ stereo / doubles
def detect_stereo(P):
    st, act = P["st"], P["act"]
    sr = P["sr"]; n = int(HOP * sr)
    L, R = st[0], st[1]
    k = len(L) // n
    am = np.repeat(act[:k], n)[: k * n]
    l, r = L[: k * n][am], R[: k * n][am]
    if len(l) < sr:
        return {"present": False, "confidence": 0.0, "width": "unknown"}
    corr = float(np.corrcoef(l, r)[0, 1]) if l.std() > 0 and r.std() > 0 else 1.0
    M, S = (l + r) / 2, (l - r) / 2
    side_db = float(10 * np.log10((S ** 2).mean() / ((M ** 2).mean() + 1e-12) + 1e-12))
    seg = slice(0, min(len(L), 20 * sr))
    xc = sg.correlate(L[seg], R[seg], mode="full", method="fft"); mid = len(L[seg]) - 1
    W = int(0.04 * sr); win = xc[mid - W: mid + W + 1]
    lag_ms = float((np.argmax(np.abs(win)) - W) / sr * 1000.0)
    wide = corr < 0.985 or side_db > -22.0
    conf = _conf((0.995 - corr) / 0.08, 1.0) if wide else 0.0
    if side_db > -16.0:
        conf = max(conf, 0.95)
    return {"present": bool(wide and conf >= REDUCE_AT), "confidence": round(float(np.clip(conf, 0, 1)), 3),
            "correlation": round(corr, 3), "side_db": round(side_db, 1), "interchannel_lag_ms": round(lag_ms, 1),
            "width": "stereo" if wide else "mono/dual-mono"}


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ pitch correction
def _f0_track(P):
    m, sr, act = P["m"], P["sr"], P["act"]
    n = int(0.03 * sr); hop = int(HOP * sr)
    lo, hi = int(sr / 700), int(sr / 70)
    win = np.hanning(n)
    Wf = np.fft.rfft(win, 2 * n); aw = np.fft.irfft(np.abs(Wf) ** 2)[:n]; aw = aw / aw[0]   # window's own autocorrelation
    aw = np.maximum(aw, 0.05)
    f0 = []; cents = []
    for i in range(0, len(m) - n, hop):
        fi = i // hop
        if fi >= len(act) or not act[fi]:
            continue
        x = m[i:i + n] - m[i:i + n].mean()
        if np.abs(x).max() < 1e-4:
            continue
        X = np.fft.rfft(x * win, 2 * n)
        ac = np.fft.irfft(np.abs(X) ** 2)[:n]
        ac = ac / (ac[0] + 1e-12) / aw                   # undo the taper: a perfect periodic signal now scores ~1.0
        seg = ac[lo:hi]
        mx = float(seg.max())
        if mx < 0.88:
            continue
        k = int(np.argmax(seg >= 0.93 * mx))            # first strong peak, not the biggest (avoids octave-down errors)
        while k + 1 < len(seg) and seg[k + 1] > seg[k]:
            k += 1
        if k == 0 or k == len(seg) - 1:
            continue
        a_, b_, c_ = seg[k - 1], seg[k], seg[k + 1]
        den = a_ - 2 * b_ + c_
        kk = k + (0.5 * (a_ - c_) / den if abs(den) > 1e-12 else 0.0)
        f = sr / (lo + kk)
        f0.append((fi, f)); cents.append(1200 * np.log2(f / 440.0))
    return np.array(f0), np.array(cents)


def detect_pitch(P):
    f0, c = _f0_track(P)
    if len(c) < 200:
        return {"present": False, "confidence": 0.0, "tuned": False, "voiced_frames": int(len(c)), "why": "not enough clearly voiced frames"}
    best, bo = 0.0, 0.0
    for o in np.arange(-50, 50, 2.0):
        d = np.abs(((c - o + 50) % 100) - 50)
        fr = float((d <= 8).mean())
        if fr > best: best, bo = fr, float(o)
    fi = f0[:, 0]
    adj = np.diff(fi) == 1
    dc = np.abs(np.diff(c))
    flat = float((dc[adj] <= 1.5).mean()) if adj.any() else 0.0
    c1 = (best - 0.35) / 0.35          # random singing sits near 0.16-0.30
    c2 = (flat - 0.15) / 0.30
    conf = _conf(c1, c2)
    return {"present": conf >= REDUCE_AT, "tuned": conf >= REDUCE_AT, "confidence": round(conf, 3), "on_grid_fraction": round(best, 2),
            "flat_fraction": round(flat, 2), "voiced_frames": int(len(c)), "tuning_offset_cents": round(bo, 0)}


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ dynamics (information only)
def dynamics(P):
    m, sr, act = P["m"], P["sr"], P["act"]
    n = int(0.4 * sr); k = len(m) // n
    e = 20 * np.log10(np.sqrt((m[: k * n].reshape(k, n) ** 2).mean(1)) + 1e-9)
    ac = np.repeat(act, int(HOP * sr))[: k * n].reshape(k, n).mean(1) > 0.6
    v = e[ac]
    if len(v) < 10:
        return {"swing_db": None}
    swing = float(np.percentile(v, 95) - np.percentile(v, 10))
    h = int(HOP * sr)
    mask = np.zeros(len(m), dtype=bool)
    am = np.repeat(act, h)[: len(m)]
    mask[: len(am)] = am
    crest = float(20 * np.log10(np.abs(m).max() / (np.sqrt((m[mask] ** 2).mean()) + 1e-12)))
    return {"swing_db": round(swing, 1), "crest_db": round(crest, 1), "already_compressed": bool(swing <= 5.0 and crest <= 11.0)}


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ public API
def analyze(voc, sr, bpm=None):
    voc = np.asarray(voc)
    if voc.ndim == 2 and voc.shape[0] > voc.shape[1] and voc.shape[1] <= 8:
        voc = voc.T                                       # accept (N, C) as well as (C, N)
    if voc.ndim == 2 and voc.shape[0] == 1:
        voc = voc[0]
    x = voc
    if x.size == 0 or x.shape[-1] < sr * 12:
        return {"verdict": "unknown", "reasons": ["clip shorter than 12 s: not enough to judge"], "reverb": {"confidence": 0.0}, "delay": {"confidence": 0.0},
                "stereo": {"confidence": 0.0}, "pitch": {"confidence": 0.0}, "dynamics": {}}
    P = _prep(voc, sr)
    a = {"reverb": detect_reverb(P), "delay": detect_delay(P, bpm), "stereo": detect_stereo(P), "pitch": detect_pitch(P), "dynamics": dynamics(P)}
    hits = [k for k in ("reverb", "delay", "stereo", "pitch") if a[k].get("confidence", 0) >= BYPASS_AT]
    maybe = [k for k in ("reverb", "delay", "stereo", "pitch") if REDUCE_AT <= a[k].get("confidence", 0) < BYPASS_AT]
    a["verdict"] = ("processed: " + ", ".join(hits)) if hits else ("probably processed: " + ", ".join(maybe)) if maybe else "dry (no effects found)"
    a["reasons"] = []
    return a


def engine_plan(a):
    plan = {"reverb_send_scale": 1.0, "delay_send_scale": 1.0, "skip_adt": False, "skip_widen": False, "skip_pitch_correct": False, "reasons": []}

    def scale(c):
        if c >= BYPASS_AT: return 0.0
        if c >= REDUCE_AT: return float(np.clip(1.2 - 1.2 * c, 0.1, 1.0))
        return 1.0
    r, d, s, p = a["reverb"], a["delay"], a["stereo"], a["pitch"]
    plan["reverb_send_scale"] = scale(r.get("confidence", 0.0))
    plan["delay_send_scale"] = scale(d.get("confidence", 0.0))
    if r.get("confidence", 0) >= REDUCE_AT:
        plan["reasons"].append("reverb already in the vocal (tail %.0f dB above floor, RT60 ~%s s, confidence %.2f)" % (r.get("tail_db") or 0, r.get("rt60_s"), r["confidence"]))
    if d.get("confidence", 0) >= REDUCE_AT:
        plan["reasons"].append("delay already in the vocal (~%s ms, confidence %.2f)" % (d.get("delay_ms"), d["confidence"]))
    if s.get("confidence", 0) >= BYPASS_AT:
        plan["skip_adt"] = True; plan["skip_widen"] = True
        plan["reasons"].append("vocal is already stereo / doubled (correlation %.2f)" % s.get("correlation", 1.0))
    if p.get("confidence", 0) >= BYPASS_AT:
        plan["skip_pitch_correct"] = True
        plan["reasons"].append("pitch already corrected (%.0f%% of notes on the semitone grid)" % (100 * p.get("on_grid_fraction", 0)))
    return plan


==============================================================================
FILE: fx-api/api/vocal_polish.py   (226 lines)
==============================================================================

"""
vocal_polish.py  -  adaptive "expensive" polish that keeps the vocal clear (no smearing, no pumping)
"""
import os, time, shutil, tempfile, subprocess
import numpy as np
import scipy.signal as sg
import scipy.io.wavfile as wf
from scipy.ndimage import uniform_filter1d, maximum_filter1d

__all__ = ["polish", "ride_vocal", "clean_gaps", "carve_beat", "pad_tail", "fade_tail", "dither_int16", "mp3_safe_gain", "true_peak_db"]

GENRE_POLISH = {
    "afrobeats": dict(ride=1.0, up=3.0, down=3.5, breath=8.0, carve=2.0),
    "amapiano":  dict(ride=1.0, up=3.0, down=3.5, breath=8.0, carve=2.0),
    "hiphop":    dict(ride=1.1, up=3.5, down=4.0, breath=10.0, carve=2.0),
    "rnb":       dict(ride=0.8, up=2.5, down=3.0, breath=5.0, carve=1.5),
    "pop":       dict(ride=1.0, up=3.0, down=3.5, breath=8.0, carve=2.0),
    "dancehall": dict(ride=1.0, up=3.0, down=3.5, breath=8.0, carve=2.0),
    "gospel":    dict(ride=0.8, up=2.5, down=3.0, breath=5.0, carve=1.5),
    "other":     dict(ride=0.9, up=3.0, down=3.0, breath=6.0, carve=1.8),
}
_ALIAS = {"afro": "afrobeats", "afrobeat": "afrobeats", "afropop": "afrobeats", "rap": "hiphop", "trap": "hiphop",
          "drill": "hiphop", "rb": "rnb", "randb": "rnb", "piano": "amapiano"}

def _cfg(g):
    k = "".join(c for c in str(g or "other").lower() if c.isalnum())
    return dict(GENRE_POLISH[_ALIAS.get(k, k) if _ALIAS.get(k, k) in GENRE_POLISH else "other"])

def _bp(lo, hi, sr, order=2):
    return sg.butter(order, [lo, min(hi, sr / 2 * 0.95)], btype="band", fs=sr, output="sos")

def _mono(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, np.float64)
    return x if x.ndim == 1 else x.mean(axis=0)

def _lvl(x, sr, win_s, hop):
    p = uniform_filter1d(x.astype(np.float64) ** 2, size=max(1, int(win_s * sr)), mode="nearest")[::hop]
    return 10 * np.log10(p + 1e-12)

def _to_samples(c, hop, n):
    return np.interp(np.arange(n), (np.arange(len(c)) + 0.5) * hop, c)

def _thr(lvl):
    live = lvl[lvl > -70]
    return None if len(live) < 30 else max(np.percentile(live, 90) - 22.0, -60.0)

def _db(v):
    return 20.0 * np.log10(max(float(v), 1e-12))

def _rms(x):
    return float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(x, dtype=np.float64))) + 1e-12)

def ride_vocal(vocal, sr, genre="afrobeats", strength=None):
    x = np.asarray(vocal, np.float64)
    m = _mono(x)
    cfg = _cfg(genre)
    hop = int(0.010 * sr)
    hs = hop / sr
    lvl = _lvl(sg.sosfiltfilt(_bp(150, 5000, sr), m), sr, 0.040, hop)
    sib = _lvl(sg.sosfiltfilt(_bp(5000, 10000, sr), m), sr, 0.040, hop)
    thr = _thr(lvl)
    if thr is None: return x, {"skipped": "vocal silent / too short"}
    act = lvl > thr
    ok = act & ~(sib > lvl - 6.0)
    if ok.sum() < 30: return x, {"skipped": "not enough voiced material"}
    glob = float(np.median(lvl[ok]))
    step, half = 100, 300
    pts, vals = [], []
    for c in range(0, len(lvl), step):
        w = lvl[max(0, c - half): c + half][ok[max(0, c - half): c + half]]
        if len(w) > 20:
            pts.append(c); vals.append(np.median(w))
    local = np.interp(np.arange(len(lvl)), pts, vals) if pts else np.full(len(lvl), glob)
    target = 0.5 * glob + 0.5 * local
    spread = float(np.percentile(lvl[ok], 90) - np.percentile(lvl[ok], 10))
    s = float(np.clip((spread - 3.0) / 8.0, 0.15, 0.65) * cfg["ride"]) if strength is None else float(strength)
    raw = np.clip(s * (target - lvl), -cfg["down"], cfg["up"])
    ka, kr = np.exp(-hs / 0.025), np.exp(-hs / 0.120)
    g = np.zeros(len(lvl))
    cur = 0.0
    for i in range(len(lvl)):
        if ok[i]:
            t = raw[i]
            k = ka if t < cur else kr
            cur = k * cur + (1.0 - k) * t
        elif not act[i]:
            cur *= 0.98
        g[i] = cur
    g = np.r_[g[1:], g[-1]]
    gain = 10 ** (_to_samples(g, hop, x.shape[-1]) / 20.0)
    out = x * gain
    rg = g[act]
    return out, {"strength": round(s, 2), "target_db": round(glob, 1), "spread_db": round(spread, 1),
                 "ride_up_max_db": round(float(max(rg.max(), 0)), 1), "ride_down_max_db": round(float(min(rg.min(), 0)), 1),
                 "mean_abs_move_db": round(float(np.abs(rg).mean()), 2)}

def clean_gaps(vocal, sr, genre="afrobeats", depth_db=None):
    x = np.asarray(vocal, np.float64)
    m = _mono(x)
    cfg = _cfg(genre)
    hop = int(0.005 * sr)
    hs = hop / sr
    lvl = _lvl(m, sr, 0.020, hop)
    thr = _thr(lvl)
    if thr is None: return x, {"skipped": "vocal silent / too short"}
    act = lvl > thr
    gap = ~act
    gap = uniform_filter1d(gap.astype(float), size=int(0.100 / hs), mode="nearest") > 0.99
    after = maximum_filter1d(act.astype(np.uint8), size=int(0.200 / hs), origin=int(0.100 / hs) - 1).astype(bool)
    before = maximum_filter1d(act.astype(np.uint8), size=int(0.030 / hs), origin=-(int(0.015 / hs))).astype(bool)
    gap &= ~after & ~before
    if not gap.any(): return x, {"skipped": "no gaps found"}
    voice = float(np.median(lvl[act]))
    gap_lvl = float(np.percentile(lvl[gap], 90))
    rel = voice - gap_lvl
    depth = float((depth_db if depth_db is not None else cfg["breath"]) * np.clip((45.0 - rel) / 15.0, 0.25, 1.0))
    red = np.where(gap, depth, 0.0)
    ka, kr = np.exp(-hs / 0.030), np.exp(-hs / 0.006)
    cur = 0.0
    out_r = np.zeros(len(red))
    for i in range(len(red)):
        t = red[i]
        k = ka if t > cur else kr
        cur = k * cur + (1.0 - k) * t
        out_r[i] = cur
    gain = 10 ** (-_to_samples(out_r, hop, x.shape[-1]) / 20.0)
    return x * gain, {"depth_db": round(depth, 1), "gaps_pct": round(float(gap.mean() * 100), 1),
                      "gap_level_below_voice_db": round(rel, 1)}

_CARVE_BANDS = [
    (250, 500, -2.0, 0.75), (500, 1000, 2.0, 1.0), (1000, 2000, 3.0, 1.0), (2000, 4000, 2.0, 1.0)]

def carve_beat(beat, vocal, sr, genre="afrobeats", strength=1.0):
    b = np.asarray(beat, np.float64)
    if b.ndim == 1: b = np.stack([b, b])
    n = b.shape[1]
    vm = _mono(vocal)
    vm = vm[:n] if len(vm) >= n else np.pad(vm, (0, n - len(vm)))
    cfg = _cfg(genre)
    hop = int(0.010 * sr)
    hs = hop / sr
    vl = _lvl(vm, sr, 0.040, hop)
    thr = _thr(vl)
    if thr is None: return b, {"skipped": "vocal silent / too short"}
    act = vl > thr
    bm = b.mean(axis=0)
    out = b.copy()
    rep = {}
    ka, kr = np.exp(-hs / 0.015), np.exp(-hs / 0.180)
    for lo, hi, want, scale in _CARVE_BANDS:
        sos = _bp(lo, hi, sr)
        Lv = _lvl(sg.sosfiltfilt(sos, vm), sr, 0.050, hop)
        Lb = _lvl(sg.sosfiltfilt(sos, bm), sr, 0.050, hop)
        cap = cfg["carve"] * scale * float(np.clip(strength, 0.0, 1.0))
        need = np.where(act, np.clip(want - (Lv - Lb), 0.0, cap), 0.0)
        name = f"{lo}-{hi}"
        if need.max() < 0.1:
            rep[name] = {"avg_dip_db": 0.0, "max_dip_db": 0.0, "dipping_pct": 0.0}
            continue
        cur, d = 0.0, np.zeros(len(need))
        for i in range(len(need)):
            t = need[i]
            k = ka if t > cur else kr
            cur = k * cur + (1.0 - k) * t
            d[i] = cur
        g = 10 ** (-_to_samples(d, hop, n) / 20.0)
        for ch in range(2):
            band = sg.sosfiltfilt(sos, out[ch])
            out[ch] = out[ch] - (1.0 - g) * band
        rep[name] = {"avg_dip_db": round(float(d[act].mean()), 2), "max_dip_db": round(float(d.max()), 2),
                     "dipping_pct": round(float((d > 0.3).mean() * 100), 1)}
    lo_sos = sg.butter(2, 150, "low", fs=sr, output="sos")
    rep["beat_change_db"] = round(_db(_rms(out) / _rms(b)), 2)
    rep["beat_low_end_change_db"] = round(_db(_rms(sg.sosfiltfilt(lo_sos, out.mean(axis=0))) / _rms(sg.sosfiltfilt(lo_sos, bm))), 2)
    return out, rep

def polish(vocal, beat, sr, genre="afrobeats", ride=True, gaps=True, carve=True, carve_strength=1.0):
    t0 = time.time()
    rep = {"genre": genre}
    v = np.asarray(vocal, np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(beat, np.float64) if beat is not None else None
    if ride: v, rep["ride"] = ride_vocal(v, sr, genre)
    if gaps: v, rep["gaps"] = clean_gaps(v, sr, genre)
    if carve and b is not None: b, rep["carve"] = carve_beat(b, v, sr, genre, carve_strength)
    rep["seconds"] = round(time.time() - t0, 1)
    return v, b, rep

def pad_tail(x, sr, tail_s):
    x = np.asarray(x)
    pad = int(tail_s * sr)
    return np.pad(x, (0, pad)) if x.ndim == 1 else np.pad(x, ((0, 0), (0, pad)))

def fade_tail(y, sr, fade_s=0.25):
    y = np.array(y, np.float64)
    tail = y[..., -int(0.1 * sr):]
    if _db(_rms(tail)) < -55.0: return y, False
    k = int(fade_s * sr)
    y[..., -k:] *= 0.5 * (1 + np.cos(np.linspace(0, np.pi, k)))
    return y, True

def dither_int16(y, seed=1):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    q = np.asarray(y, np.float64) * 32767.0
    q = q + rng.random(q.shape) - rng.random(q.shape)
    return np.clip(np.round(q), -32768, 32767).astype(np.int16).T

def true_peak_db(x, sr):
    x = np.atleast_2d(np.asarray(x, np.float64))
    return _db(np.abs(sg.resample_poly(x, 4, 1, axis=-1)).max())

def mp3_safe_gain(y, sr, ceiling=-1.0, bitrate="320k", margin=0.02):
    if shutil.which("ffmpeg") is None: return 0.0, None, "ffmpeg not available"
    d = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    try:
        a = np.atleast_2d(np.asarray(y, np.float32))
        wf.write(os.path.join(d, "a.wav"), sr, a.T)
        run = lambda *c: subprocess.run(c, check=True, capture_output=True)
        run("ffmpeg", "-y", "-i", os.path.join(d, "a.wav"), "-b:a", bitrate, os.path.join(d, "a.mp3"))
        run("ffmpeg", "-y", "-i", os.path.join(d, "a.mp3"), "-f", "wav", "-acodec", "pcm_f32le", os.path.join(d, "b.wav"))
        _, dec = wf.read(os.path.join(d, "b.wav"))
        tp = true_peak_db(np.atleast_2d(dec.astype(np.float64).T), sr)
        return float(min(0.0, ceiling - tp - margin)), round(float(tp), 2), "ok"
    except Exception as e:
        return 0.0, None, f"encode check failed: {str(e)[:60]}"
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(d, ignore_errors=True)


==============================================================================
FILE: fx-api/api/vocal_sat.py   (206 lines)
==============================================================================

"""vocal_sat.py -- adaptive grade-1 VOCAL saturator (lead / backing / adlib).

Measures the vocal first (crest, brightness, sibilance, pitch, loudness), then plans the saturation from that:
  * 4x oversampled tube-style curve (asymmetric tanh: even + odd harmonics), DC-blocked, unity small-signal gain
  * works only on a band (about 1.6 x pitch up to 5-8 kHz): no low-mid mud, no saturated 's' sounds
  * drive is searched so the ADDED harmonic energy hits a target (about -30 dB for a lead), parallel mixed
  * guards: crest loss <= 1 dB, sibilance rise <= 1 dB, loudness matched, peaks held at original + 0.3 dB
Your engine's sat value (0.15 / 0.3 / 0.6 ...) maps to the target, so it stays a familiar control.

  process(voc, sr, role="lead", amount=0.3) -> (voc_out, report)
Pure numpy/scipy. voc: (n,) or (ch, n) float32.
"""
import numpy as np
import scipy.signal as sg

_OS = 4
ROLES = {
    "lead":    dict(hp=(150.0, 260.0), lp=7000.0, mix_max=0.50, amount=0.30),
    "backing": dict(hp=(220.0, 320.0), lp=5500.0, mix_max=0.50, amount=0.20),
    "adlib":   dict(hp=(200.0, 300.0), lp=6500.0, mix_max=0.50, amount=0.30),
}
LIMITS = dict(crest_drop_db=1.0, sib_rise_db=1.0, peak_rise_db=0.3)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------- helpers
def _mono(x):
    a = np.asarray(x, np.float32)
    return a.mean(axis=0) if a.ndim == 2 else a


def _sos(kind, f, sr, order=4):
    return sg.butter(order, f, kind, fs=sr, output="sos")


def _zp(x, sos):
    return sg.sosfiltfilt(sos, x).astype(np.float32)


def _db(v):
    return 20.0 * np.log10(np.asarray(v, np.float64) + 1e-12)


def _hfir(sr):
    return sg.firwin(255, min(21000.0, 0.46 * sr) / (sr * _OS / 2.0), window=("kaiser", 9.0))


def _frames(x, sr, hop_s=0.05):
    h = int(hop_s * sr); k = len(x) // h
    return 10 * np.log10((x[:k * h].reshape(k, h).astype(np.float64) ** 2).mean(1) + 1e-14)


def _f0_median(m, sr, frames=300):
    N = 2048; k = len(m) // N
    if k < 8: return None
    fr = m[:k * N].reshape(k, N); e = (fr ** 2).mean(1); idx = np.argsort(e)[::-1][:frames]
    lo, hi = int(sr / 500), int(sr / 70); out = []
    for i in idx:
        f = fr[i] * np.hanning(N); ac = np.fft.irfft(np.abs(np.fft.rfft(f, 2 * N)) ** 2)[:N]
        if ac[0] <= 0: continue
        ac = ac / ac[0]; lag = lo + int(np.argmax(ac[lo:hi]))
        if ac[lag] > 0.45: out.append(sr / lag)
    return float(np.median(out)) if len(out) >= 10 else None


# ----------------------------------------------------------------- 1. measure
def measure_vocal(v, sr):
    m = _mono(v)
    tot = _frames(m, sr); act = tot > (np.percentile(tot, 95) - 25.0)
    mid = _frames(_zp(m, _sos("bandpass", [250, 1000], sr, 2)), sr)
    hi = _frames(_zp(m, _sos("bandpass", [4000, 8000], sr, 2)), sr)
    air = _frames(_zp(m, _sos("bandpass", [8000, min(16000, 0.45 * sr)], sr, 2)), sr)
    sib = _frames(_zp(m, _sos("bandpass", [5000, 9000], sr, 2)), sr)
    pres = _frames(_zp(m, _sos("bandpass", [1500, 4000], sr, 2)), sr)
    w = int(0.4 * sr); k = len(m) // w
    fr = m[:k * w].reshape(k, w); r = np.sqrt((fr.astype(np.float64) ** 2).mean(1) + 1e-14); p = np.abs(fr).max(1)
    loud = _db(r) > np.percentile(_db(r), 50)
    crest = float(np.median((_db(p) - _db(r))[loud])) if loud.any() else 10.0
    ad = tot[act]
    return {"crest_short_db": round(crest, 2), "bright_db": round(float((hi - mid)[act].mean()), 1),
            "air_db": round(float((air - mid)[act].mean()), 1),
            "sib_p95_db": round(float(np.percentile((sib - pres)[act], 95)), 1),
            "active_rms_db": round(float(10 * np.log10((10 ** (ad / 10)).mean())), 2),
            "spread_db": round(float(np.percentile(ad, 90) - np.percentile(ad, 10)), 1),
            "f0": _f0_median(m, sr), "peak_db": round(float(_db(np.abs(np.asarray(v)).max())), 2)}


# ----------------------------------------------------------------- 2. plan
def plan(meas, role="lead", amount=None):
    R = ROLES.get(role, ROLES["lead"])
    amount = R["amount"] if amount is None else float(amount)
    target = float(np.interp(amount, [0, 0.15, 0.3, 0.6, 1.5], [-40, -34, -30, -26, -20]))
    target += float(np.interp(meas["crest_short_db"], [8, 14], [-3.0, 2.0]))   # squashed vocal: less, dynamic vocal: more
    lp, notes = R["lp"], []
    if meas["sib_p95_db"] > 5.0:
        lp = min(lp, 5000.0); target -= 1.5; notes.append("sibilant: saturation kept below 5 kHz")
    elif meas["sib_p95_db"] < 1.0 and meas["bright_db"] < -17.5:
        lp = max(lp, 8000.0); notes.append("dark vocal with soft s: harmonics allowed to reach the top")
    f0 = meas.get("f0") or 200.0
    hp = float(np.clip(1.6 * f0, *R["hp"]))
    bias = float(np.interp(meas["bright_db"], [-24, -14], [0.18, 0.08]))
    return {"role": role, "amount": amount, "target_db": round(float(np.clip(target, -42, -18)), 1), "hp": round(hp), "lp": round(lp),
            "bias": round(bias, 3), "mix_max": R["mix_max"], "notes": notes}


# ----------------------------------------------------------------- 3. saturator core
def _curve(x, drive, bias):
    t0 = np.tanh(bias)
    return (np.tanh(drive * x + bias) - t0) / (drive * (1.0 - t0 * t0))


def _sat_os(x, drive, bias, sr, chunk=441000, ctx=2048):
    H = _hfir(sr); n = len(x); out = np.empty(n, np.float32)
    for s in range(0, n, chunk):
        a0, b0 = max(0, s - ctx), min(n, s + chunk + ctx)
        seg = x[a0:b0].astype(np.float64)
        y = _curve(sg.resample_poly(seg, _OS, 1, window=H), drive, bias)
        dn = sg.resample_poly(y, 1, _OS, window=H)[:len(seg)]
        out[s:min(n, s + chunk)] = dn[s - a0: s - a0 + min(chunk, n - s)].astype(np.float32)
    return out


def _dcblock(x, sr):
    return sg.sosfilt(_sos("highpass", 15.0, sr, 2), x).astype(np.float32)


def _apply(x, sr, P, drive_db, mix):
    """one channel: band = [hp, lp] is saturated and mixed in parallel; everything else is untouched."""
    sos = _sos("bandpass", [P["hp"], min(P["lp"], 0.45 * sr)], sr, 3)
    band = _zp(x, sos); rest = x - band
    wet = _dcblock(_sat_os(band, 10 ** (drive_db / 20.0), P["bias"], sr), sr)
    return (rest + (1.0 - mix) * band + mix * wet).astype(np.float32)


def _peak_guard(x, ceiling, sr, look_ms=2.0):
    from scipy.ndimage import maximum_filter1d, uniform_filter1d
    a = np.asarray(x, np.float32); pk = np.abs(a).max(axis=0) if a.ndim == 2 else np.abs(a)
    L = max(3, int(look_ms / 1000.0 * sr)); env = maximum_filter1d(pk, size=2 * L + 1)
    g = np.minimum(1.0, ceiling / (env + 1e-12)).astype(np.float32); g = uniform_filter1d(g, size=2 * L + 1).astype(np.float32)
    g = np.minimum(g, np.minimum(1.0, ceiling / (pk + 1e-12)))
    return (a * g[None, :] if a.ndim == 2 else a * g).astype(np.float32)


def _band_energy_db(x, sr, lo, hi):
    m = _mono(x); N = 4096; k = (len(m) - N) // 2048 + 1
    idx = np.arange(N)[None, :] + 2048 * np.arange(k)[:, None]
    P = np.abs(np.fft.rfft(m[idx] * np.hanning(N), axis=1)) ** 2; f = np.fft.rfftfreq(N, 1 / sr)
    tot = 10 * np.log10(P[:, (f >= 100) & (f < 8000)].sum(1) + 1e-12); act = tot > np.percentile(tot, 95) - 25
    return float(10 * np.log10(P[act][:, (f >= lo) & (f < hi)].sum() + 1e-20))


# ----------------------------------------------------------------- 4. process
def process(voc, sr, role="lead", amount=None, mix=None):
    a = np.asarray(voc, np.float32)
    m = _mono(a)
    before = measure_vocal(a, sr)
    P = plan(before, role, amount)
    # probe = loudest 20 s
    w = int(20 * sr)
    if len(m) > w:
        step = int(sr * 2); starts = list(range(0, len(m) - w, step)); e = [float((m[s:s + w].astype(np.float64) ** 2).mean()) for s in starts]
        s0 = starts[int(np.argmax(e))]; probe = m[s0:s0 + w]
    else:
        probe = m
    sos = _sos("bandpass", [P["hp"], min(P["lp"], 0.45 * sr)], sr, 3)
    pband = _zp(probe, sos); pe = float((probe.astype(np.float64) ** 2).mean() + 1e-20)

    def added_db(drive_db, mx):
        wet = _sat_os(pband, 10 ** (drive_db / 20.0), P["bias"], sr)
        return float(10 * np.log10(((mx * (wet - pband)).astype(np.float64) ** 2).mean() / pe + 1e-20))

    def probe_stats(x):
        mm = measure_vocal(x, sr); return mm["crest_short_db"], mm["sib_p95_db"]
    c0, s0_ = probe_stats(probe)
    tries, mix_use, drive_use = [], 0.0, None
    cands = [mix] if mix is not None else [P["mix_max"] * f for f in (1.0, 0.7, 0.5, 0.35, 0.25)]
    for mx in cands:
        if mx <= 0: break
        lo, hi = -30.0, 24.0
        for _ in range(13):
            mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            if added_db(mid, mx) < P["target_db"]: lo = mid
            else: hi = mid
        dr = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        pr = _apply(probe, sr, P, dr, mx)
        pr = pr * np.float32(10 ** ((10 * np.log10(pe) - 10 * np.log10((pr.astype(np.float64) ** 2).mean() + 1e-20)) / 20.0))
        c1, s1 = probe_stats(pr)
        tries.append({"mix": round(mx, 2), "drive_db": round(dr, 1), "crest_drop_db": round(c0 - c1, 2), "sib_rise_db": round(s1 - s0_, 2)})
        if mix is not None or ((c0 - c1) <= LIMITS["crest_drop_db"] and (s1 - s0_) <= LIMITS["sib_rise_db"]):
            mix_use, drive_use = mx, dr; break
    if mix_use <= 0:
        rep = {"plan": P, "before": before, "applied": False, "note": "skipped: every amount cost crest or added sibilance beyond the limits", "mix_search": tries}
        return a, rep
    out = np.stack([_apply(c, sr, P, drive_use, mix_use) for c in a]) if a.ndim == 2 else _apply(a, sr, P, drive_use, mix_use)
    # level match on the vocal's own active level, then hold peaks
    mo = _mono(out)
    gain = float(np.clip(measure_vocal(a, sr)["active_rms_db"] - measure_vocal(mo, sr)["active_rms_db"], -1.0, 1.0))
    out = (out * np.float32(10 ** (gain / 20.0))).astype(np.float32)
    out = _peak_guard(out, float(np.abs(a).max()) * 10 ** (LIMITS["peak_rise_db"] / 20.0), sr)
    after = measure_vocal(out, sr)
    rep = {"plan": P, "applied": True, "drive_db": round(drive_use, 2), "mix": round(mix_use, 2), "mix_search": tries,
           "before": before, "after": after,
           "delta": {"active_rms_db": round(after["active_rms_db"] - before["active_rms_db"], 2), "peak_db": round(after["peak_db"] - before["peak_db"], 2),
                     "crest_short_db": round(after["crest_short_db"] - before["crest_short_db"], 2), "sib_p95_db": round(after["sib_p95_db"] - before["sib_p95_db"], 2),
                     "bright_db": round(after["bright_db"] - before["bright_db"], 2), "air_db": round(after["air_db"] - before["air_db"], 2)},
           "oversampling": _OS}
    return out, rep


==============================================================================
FILE: fx-api/api/vocal_space.py   (411 lines)
==============================================================================

"""
vocal_space.py  -  adaptive "pro touch" space for vocals: reverb + delay + early reflections + tiny room (+ optional beat room)

WHAT IT DOES (and why it sounds dry, clear and close)
  * Returns an ADDITIVE wet bus. The dry vocal and the beat are never modified:   mix = beat + vocal + bus
  * Sends are cleaned first: high-passed (no boom / plosives into the reverb) and de-essed (no sibilance smeared into the tail).
  * Pre-delay locked to the song tempo keeps the dry consonant in front of the reverb onset.
  * The wet bus is DUCKED by the dry vocal (sidechain): the space is quiet while words are sung and blooms in the gaps.
    That is the "dry, clear, no smearing, afloat in the mix" sound. Distance is controlled by the direct-to-reverb ratio,
    so the vocal stays close to the ear.
  * Delay is tempo-synced, filtered darker on every repeat, ping-pong wide, ducked, and by default only "thrown" off the
    ends of phrases, so repeats never land on top of the next word.
  * Early reflections (5 taps per side, 7-35 ms) + a 0.22 s room give the "in the room with the music" feel.
  * Optional beat room: a very quiet short room fed by the beat's top end puts vocal and beat in the same space.
    It is added on its own bus; the beat itself is untouched (report shows the measured change).

ADAPTIVE (per song, per user - nothing fixed):
  vocal density, phrase gaps, brightness, sibilance, tempo and genre set: reverb decay, pre-delay, send filters, wet level,
  duck depth/release, delay note value, feedback, throw-vs-full mode, de-ess amount.

USAGE
    bus, rep = vocal_space.build(lead_dry, sr, bpm=99.4, genre="afrobeats", role="lead", beat=beat_stereo, space=0.5)
    mix = beat + lead_dry + bus
    # backing vocals: duck their space from the LEAD so it never competes with it
    bus_b, rep_b = vocal_space.build(backing_dry, sr, bpm=99.4, role="backing", duck_from=lead_dry)

  space: 0..1 user slider (0.5 = neutral, +-4 dB of wet level). Genre values are engineering starting points, not measured.
"""
import time
import numpy as np
import scipy.signal as sg
from scipy.ndimage import uniform_filter1d

__all__ = ["build", "GENRE_SPACE"]

# starting values (typical engineering practice, NOT measured from reference records - tune by ear / report)
GENRE_SPACE = {
    # wet / dly = level of the plate / delay return with the duck OPEN, dB vs the dry vocal's RMS (ducking then pulls it down)
    #              plate decay  wet dB  delay dB  duck dB  early dB  room dB  feedback  delay style
    "afrobeats": dict(rt60=0.95, wet=-8.0, dly=-17.0, duck=8.0, early=-24.0, room=-27.0, fb=0.30, style="note"),
    "amapiano":  dict(rt60=1.20, wet=-7.0, dly=-18.0, duck=8.0, early=-24.0, room=-26.0, fb=0.32, style="note"),
    "hiphop":    dict(rt60=0.70, wet=-11.0, dly=-16.0, duck=10.0, early=-24.0, room=-28.0, fb=0.18, style="slap"),
    "rnb":       dict(rt60=1.30, wet=-6.0, dly=-17.0, duck=8.0, early=-23.0, room=-26.0, fb=0.34, style="note"),
    "pop":       dict(rt60=1.00, wet=-8.5, dly=-17.0, duck=8.0, early=-24.0, room=-27.0, fb=0.28, style="note"),
    "dancehall": dict(rt60=0.85, wet=-9.0, dly=-14.0, duck=9.0, early=-25.0, room=-28.0, fb=0.36, style="note"),
    "gospel":    dict(rt60=1.50, wet=-6.0, dly=-20.0, duck=7.0, early=-23.0, room=-25.0, fb=0.26, style="note"),
    "other":     dict(rt60=0.95, wet=-8.5, dly=-17.5, duck=8.0, early=-24.0, room=-27.0, fb=0.28, style="note"),
}
_ALIAS = {"afro": "afrobeats", "afrobeat": "afrobeats", "afropop": "afrobeats", "rap": "hiphop", "trap": "hiphop",
          "drill": "hiphop", "rb": "rnb", "randb": "rnb", "piano": "amapiano"}


def _genre(g):
    k = "".join(c for c in str(g or "other").lower() if c.isalnum())
    k = _ALIAS.get(k, k)
    return k if k in GENRE_SPACE else "other"


def _bp(lo, hi, sr, order=2):
    return sg.butter(order, [lo, min(hi, sr / 2 * 0.95)], btype="band", fs=sr, output="sos")


def _rms(x):
    return float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(x, dtype=np.float64))) + 1e-12)


def _db(v):
    return 20.0 * np.log10(max(float(v), 1e-12))


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ analysis
def _analyse(m, sr):
    hop = int(0.010 * sr)
    n = len(m) // hop
    fr = np.sqrt(np.mean(m[: n * hop].reshape(n, hop) ** 2, axis=1) + 1e-12)
    lvl = 20 * np.log10(fr)
    live = lvl[lvl > -70]
    if len(live) < 20:
        return None
    thr = max(np.percentile(live, 90) - 22.0, -60.0)
    act = lvl > thr
    # phrases: contiguous activity, gaps < 0.25 s merged, >= 0.4 s kept
    idx = np.flatnonzero(act)
    phr = []
    if len(idx):
        s = p = idx[0]
        for i in idx[1:]:
            if (i - p) * 0.010 > 0.25:
                phr.append((s * 0.010, (p + 1) * 0.010)); s = i
            p = i
        phr.append((s * 0.010, (p + 1) * 0.010))
    phr = [q for q in phr if q[1] - q[0] >= 0.4]
    gaps = [phr[i + 1][0] - phr[i][1] for i in range(len(phr) - 1)]
    sib = _rms(sg.sosfilt(_bp(5000, 9500, sr), m)[: n * hop].reshape(n, hop)[act])
    mid = _rms(sg.sosfilt(_bp(1000, 4000, sr), m)[: n * hop].reshape(n, hop)[act])
    top = _rms(sg.sosfilt(_bp(4000, 10000, sr), m)[: n * hop].reshape(n, hop)[act])
    body = _rms(sg.sosfilt(_bp(300, 4000, sr), m)[: n * hop].reshape(n, hop)[act])
    return {"active_ratio": float(act.mean()), "phrases": phr, "median_gap_s": float(np.median(gaps)) if gaps else 0.8,
            "sib_db": _db(sib / mid), "bright_db": _db(top / body), "thr_db": float(thr)}


def _activity(m, sr, thr_db, hop_s=0.002):
    """0..1 vocal activity at control rate (hop 2 ms, 8 ms window)."""
    hop = max(1, int(hop_s * sr))
    p = uniform_filter1d(m.astype(np.float64) ** 2, size=int(0.008 * sr), mode="nearest")[::hop]
    lvl = 10 * np.log10(p + 1e-12)
    return np.clip((lvl - (thr_db - 3.0)) / 6.0, 0.0, 1.0), hop


def _smooth(x, hop_s, atk_s, rel_s):
    ka, kr = np.exp(-hop_s / atk_s), np.exp(-hop_s / rel_s)
    y = np.empty_like(x)
    s = 0.0
    for i, v in enumerate(x):
        k = ka if v > s else kr
        s = k * s + (1.0 - k) * v
        y[i] = s
    return y


def _duck_curve(act, hop, n, hop_s, duck_db, atk_ms, rel_ms):
    a = _smooth(act, hop_s, atk_ms / 1000.0, rel_ms / 1000.0)
    g = 10 ** (-duck_db * a / 20.0)
    return np.interp(np.arange(n), (np.arange(len(g)) + 0.5) * hop, g).astype(np.float32)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ send cleaning
def _clean_send(m, sr, hpf, deess_db):
    """high-pass + de-ess so only clean vowel/body energy reaches the reverb and delay."""
    x = sg.sosfilt(sg.butter(2, hpf, "high", fs=sr, output="sos"), m)
    sb = sg.sosfiltfilt(_bp(5000, 9500, sr), x)
    env = uniform_filter1d(np.abs(sb), size=max(1, int(0.003 * sr)), mode="nearest")
    ref = np.median(env[env > np.percentile(env, 60)]) if np.any(env > np.percentile(env, 60)) else np.median(env)
    g = np.clip((1.5 * ref) / (env + 1e-12), 10 ** (-deess_db / 20.0), 1.0)
    g = uniform_filter1d(g, size=max(1, int(0.002 * sr)), mode="nearest")
    return x - (1.0 - g) * sb


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ impulse responses
def _plate_ir(sr, rt60, pre_ms, hpf, lpf, seed):
    """Stereo, decorrelated, 3-band decay (highs die first), soft build-up so the onset never smears consonants."""
    n = int(sr * (rt60 * 1.1 + pre_ms / 1000.0))
    t = np.arange(n) / sr
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    bands = [(150, 700, 1.15), (700, 3000, 1.0), (3000, max(lpf, 4500), 0.55)]
    ir = np.zeros((2, n))
    for ch in range(2):
        noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
        for lo, hi, mult in bands:
            ir[ch] += sg.sosfilt(_bp(lo, hi, sr), noise) * np.exp(-6.9078 * t / (rt60 * mult))
    ir *= (1.0 - np.exp(-t / 0.012))
    pre = int(pre_ms / 1000.0 * sr)
    ir = np.concatenate([np.zeros((2, pre)), ir[:, : n - pre]], axis=1)
    ir = sg.sosfilt(sg.butter(2, hpf, "high", fs=sr, output="sos"), ir, axis=-1)
    ir = sg.sosfilt(sg.butter(2, lpf, "low", fs=sr, output="sos"), ir, axis=-1)
    return ir / (np.sqrt((ir ** 2).sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)) + 1e-12)


def _early_ir(sr):
    taps = {0: [(7.0, .9), (11.5, -.7), (17.0, .6), (23.5, -.45), (31.0, .35)],
            1: [(9.0, .85), (14.0, -.65), (20.5, .55), (27.0, -.4), (35.0, .3)]}
    ir = np.zeros((2, int(0.045 * sr)))
    for ch, ts in taps.items():
        for ms, a in ts:
            ir[ch, int(ms / 1000.0 * sr)] += a
    ir = sg.sosfilt(sg.butter(2, 250, "high", fs=sr, output="sos"), ir, axis=-1)
    ir = sg.sosfilt(sg.butter(2, 7000, "low", fs=sr, output="sos"), ir, axis=-1)
    return ir / (np.sqrt((ir ** 2).sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)) + 1e-12)


def _conv(send, ir):
    return sg.oaconvolve(send.astype(np.float32)[None, :], ir.astype(np.float32), axes=-1)[:, : len(send)]


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ delay
def _delay(send, sr, d_s, fb, hpf, lpf, width):
    """Tempo-synced ping-pong. Each repeat is filtered again (darker, further back). Built echo by echo (vectorised)."""
    n, d = len(send), int(d_s * sr)
    lp = sg.butter(1, lpf, "low", fs=sr, output="sos")
    e = np.concatenate([np.zeros(d), sg.sosfilt(sg.butter(2, hpf, "high", fs=sr, output="sos"), send)])[:n]
    out = np.zeros((2, n), np.float32)
    amp, k = 1.0, 0
    while amp > 0.01 and k < 8 and d * (k + 1) < n:
        e = sg.sosfilt(lp, e)
        side = k % 2                                   # 1st echo left, 2nd right, ...
        out[side] += (amp * e).astype(np.float32)
        out[1 - side] += (amp * e * (1.0 - width)).astype(np.float32)
        e = np.concatenate([np.zeros(d), e])[:n]
        amp *= fb ** 1.0
        k += 1
    return out


def _throw_gate(phr, n, sr, tail_s):
    """1.0 only on the last tail_s of each phrase that is followed by a gap (or ends the song)."""
    g = np.zeros(n, np.float32)
    for i, (s, e) in enumerate(phr):
        gap = (phr[i + 1][0] - e) if i + 1 < len(phr) else 9.0
        if gap >= 0.30:
            a, b = int(max(s, e - tail_s) * sr), min(n, int((e + 0.06) * sr))
            g[a:b] = 1.0
    return uniform_filter1d(g, size=max(1, int(0.015 * sr)), mode="nearest")


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ word-tail "call and response" echo
def _syl_env(m, sr):
    """Voiced-band loudness curve (dB, 4 ms hop) used to find syllable peaks and the dips between them."""
    hop = int(0.004 * sr)
    x = sg.sosfiltfilt(_bp(250, 3000, sr), m)
    p = uniform_filter1d(x ** 2, size=int(0.015 * sr), mode="nearest")[::hop]
    e = 10 * np.log10(p + 1e-12)
    return uniform_filter1d(e, size=5, mode="nearest"), hop / sr


def _fragments(m, sr, phrases, n_syl=None, words=None, min_len=0.25, max_len=1.1):
    """For every phrase that is followed by a gap, find where its LAST WORD starts."""
    force = n_syl is not None
    if force:
        min_len = 0.12                                  # an explicitly requested single syllable may be short
    n_pref = 2 if n_syl is None else int(n_syl)
    env, hs = _syl_env(m, sr)
    pk, _ = sg.find_peaks(env, prominence=5.0, distance=max(1, int(0.11 / hs)))
    out = []
    for i, (s0, e0) in enumerate(phrases):
        nxt = phrases[i + 1][0] if i + 1 < len(phrases) else None
        if nxt is not None and nxt - e0 < 0.30:
            continue
        start, how = None, "syllables"
        if words:
            ws = [w for w in words if s0 - 0.05 <= w[0] < e0]
            if ws:
                start, how = max(ws[-1][0], e0 - max_len), "word timestamps"
        if start is None:
            p = pk[(pk * hs >= s0) & (pk * hs <= e0)]
            best = None
            for k in range(1, min(4, len(p)) + 1):
                if force and k != n_pref:
                    continue
                if k < len(p):
                    a, b = p[-k - 1], p[-k]
                    v = a + int(np.argmin(env[a:b + 1]))
                    rise = np.flatnonzero(env[v:b + 1] >= env[v] + 6.0)
                    cut = max(v * hs, (v + (int(rise[0]) if len(rise) else 0)) * hs - 0.02)
                    depth = min(env[a], env[b]) - env[v]
                else:
                    cut, depth = s0, 0.0                       # whole phrase is shorter than k syllables
                if not (min_len <= e0 - cut <= max_len):
                    continue
                score = depth - 1.5 * abs(k - n_pref)
                if best is None or score > best[0]:
                    best = (score, cut, k)
            start, how = (best[1], f"last {best[2]} syllables") if best else (max(s0, e0 - 0.5), "fallback 0.5 s")
        out.append({"start": float(start), "end": float(e0), "next": nxt, "how": how})
    return out


def _fragment_echoes(send, sr, frags, grid_s, repeats, decay_db, lpf0, width):
    """Repeat each last-word fragment as a clean, tempo-grid-aligned response AFTER the word ends."""
    n = len(send)
    out = np.zeros((2, n), np.float32)
    sched = []
    for f in frags:
        a, b = int(f["start"] * sr), min(n, int((f["end"] + 0.03) * sr))
        seg = send[a:b].copy()
        if len(seg) < int(0.05 * sr):
            continue
        fi, fo = min(int(0.006 * sr), len(seg) // 4), min(int(0.025 * sr), len(seg) // 3)
        seg[:fi] *= np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, fi)
        seg[-fo:] *= np.linspace(1.0, 0.0, fo)
        L = len(seg) / sr
        step = max(1, int(np.ceil((L + 0.03) / grid_s))) * grid_s
        for k in range(1, repeats + 1):
            st = f["start"] + k * step
            if f["next"] is not None and st >= f["next"] - 0.02:
                break
            e = sg.sosfilt(sg.butter(1, max(2200.0, lpf0 - (k - 1) * 900.0), "low", fs=sr, output="sos"), seg) * 10 ** (-decay_db * (k - 1) / 20.0)
            if f["next"] is not None and st + len(e) / sr > f["next"] - 0.02:
                e = e[: max(0, int((f["next"] - 0.02 - st) * sr))]
                if len(e) > 64:
                    e[-int(0.015 * sr):] *= np.linspace(1.0, 0.0, min(len(e), int(0.015 * sr)))
            i0 = int(st * sr)
            i1 = min(n, i0 + len(e))
            if i1 <= i0:
                continue
            side = (k - 1) % 2
            out[side, i0:i1] += e[: i1 - i0].astype(np.float32)
            out[1 - side, i0:i1] += (e[: i1 - i0] * (1.0 - width)).astype(np.float32)
            sched.append((round(f["start"], 2), k, round(st, 2)))
    return out, sched


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ main
def build(vocal, sr, bpm=None, genre="afrobeats", role="lead", beat=None, space=0.5, duck_from=None,
          delay=True, glue_db=-30.0, seed=7, return_parts=False,
          delay_mode=None, tail_syllables=None, words=None, echo_repeats=3):
    """vocal: dry vocal bus, (N,) or (2,N) float. Returns (bus (2,N) float32, report). Add bus to the mix."""
    t0 = time.time()
    v = np.asarray(vocal, dtype=np.float64)
    m = v if v.ndim == 1 else v.mean(axis=0)
    n = len(m)
    zeros = np.zeros((2, n), np.float32)
    an = _analyse(m, sr)
    if an is None:
        return zeros, {"skipped": "vocal silent / too short"}
    gcfg = dict(GENRE_SPACE[_genre(genre)])
    lead = role != "backing"
    bpm = float(bpm) if bpm else 95.0
    beat_s = 60.0 / bpm
    dens = float(np.clip((an["active_ratio"] - 0.30) / 0.45, 0.0, 1.0))        # 0 = sparse, 1 = dense
    user = (float(np.clip(space, 0, 1)) - 0.5) * 8.0                              # +-4 dB

    # ---- adaptive parameters
    rt60 = float(np.clip(gcfg["rt60"] * (1.18 - 0.55 * dens) * (1.0 if lead else 1.15), 0.45, 1.8))
    pre_ms = float(np.clip(60000.0 / bpm / 16.0, 18.0, 60.0) + (0 if lead else 8.0))
    hpf = 380.0 if lead else 300.0
    lpf = float(np.interp(an["bright_db"], [-18.0, -6.0], [8000.0, 5200.0]) - (0 if lead else 900.0))
    deess = float(np.interp(an["sib_db"], [-18.0, -8.0], [3.0, 9.0]))
    wet_db = gcfg["wet"] + 2.0 * (1.0 - dens) - 2.5 * dens + user + (3.0 if not lead else 0.0)
    duck_db = gcfg["duck"] + 4.0 * dens
    rel_ms = float(np.clip(60000.0 / bpm / 4.0, 80.0, 260.0))                       # 1/16 note release
    # ---- sidechain
    key = m if duck_from is None else (np.asarray(duck_from, np.float64).mean(axis=0) if np.ndim(duck_from) == 2 else np.asarray(duck_from, np.float64))
    key = key[:n] if len(key) >= n else np.pad(key, (0, n - len(key)))
    kan = an if duck_from is None else (_analyse(key, sr) or an)
    act, hop = _activity(key, sr, kan["thr_db"])
    hop_s = hop / sr
    duck_r = _duck_curve(act, hop, n, hop_s, duck_db, 4.0, rel_ms)

    send = _clean_send(m, sr, hpf, deess)
    d_rms = _rms(m)
    bus = np.zeros((2, n), np.float32)
    lv, parts = {}, {}

    lv_avg = {}

    def add(name, wet, rel_db, duck=None, ref=None):
        r = _rms(ref if ref is not None else wet)          # level is set with the duck/gate OPEN, like a console send
        if r < 1e-9:
            return
        wet = wet * (d_rms * 10 ** (rel_db / 20.0) / r)
        if duck is not None:
            wet = wet * duck[None, :]
        parts[name] = wet
        lv[name] = float(round(rel_db, 1))
        lv_avg[name] = float(round(_db(_rms(wet) / d_rms), 1))

    # plate (the "air" around the voice) - ducked
    add("plate", _conv(send, _plate_ir(sr, rt60, pre_ms, hpf, lpf, seed)), wet_db, duck_r)
    # early reflections + tiny room (the "in the room" feel) - not ducked, very low
    add("early", _conv(send, _early_ir(sr)), gcfg["early"] + user * 0.5)
    add("room", _conv(send, _plate_ir(sr, 0.22, 0.0, 200.0, 6500.0, seed + 1)), gcfg["room"] + user * 0.5)

    # delay: note value from the phrase gaps, throw off phrase ends when the vocal is dense
    dly_info = None
    if delay and lead:
        if gcfg["style"] == "slap":
            d_s, note = float(np.clip(beat_s * 0.25, 0.08, 0.16)), "1/16 slap"
        else:
            opts = [(1.0, "1/4"), (0.75, "dotted 1/8"), (0.5, "1/8")]
            lim = max(0.9 * an["median_gap_s"], 0.25)
