            pick = next(((f, nm) for f, nm in opts if 0.18 <= beat_s * f <= min(0.75, lim)), (0.5, "1/8"))
            d_s, note = float(np.clip(beat_s * pick[0], 0.18, 0.75)), pick[1]
        fb = float(np.clip(gcfg["fb"] + 0.10 * (1.0 - dens), 0.12, 0.45))
        mode = delay_mode or ("throw" if gcfg["style"] == "slap" and dens > 0.35 else "full" if gcfg["style"] == "slap" else "fragment")
        d_duck = _duck_curve(act, hop, n, hop_s, 6.0 + 3.0 * dens, 4.0, rel_ms)
        if mode == "fragment":
            frags = _fragments(m, sr, an["phrases"], tail_syllables, words, max_len=float(min(1.1, beat_s * 2.0)))
            grid = float(np.clip(beat_s * 0.5, 0.18, 0.5))                         # 1/8-note grid
            reps = int(echo_repeats if dens < 0.8 else min(echo_repeats, 2))
            wet, sched = _fragment_echoes(send, sr, frags, grid, reps, 6.0, 4800.0, 0.8)
            echo_db = gcfg["dly"] + 9.0 + user                                      # first echo vs the sung word itself
            wet = wet * (10 ** (echo_db / 20.0)) * d_duck[None, :]
            parts["delay"] = wet
            lv["delay"] = float(round(echo_db, 1)); lv_avg["delay"] = float(round(_db(_rms(wet) / d_rms), 1))
            dly_info = {"mode": "fragment (last word, answered on the 1/8 grid)", "grid_ms": round(grid * 1000, 1), "repeats": reps,
                        "decay_db_per_repeat": 6.0, "fragments": [{"from_s": round(f["start"], 2), "to_s": round(f["end"], 2), "found_by": f["how"]} for f in frags[:6]],
                        "echo_schedule_first": sched[:6]}
        else:
            dsend = send * (_throw_gate(an["phrases"], n, sr, float(np.clip(beat_s * 0.75, 0.25, 0.5))) if mode == "throw" else 1.0)
            add("delay", _delay(dsend, sr, d_s, fb, 420.0, 4800.0, 0.8), gcfg["dly"] + user, d_duck,
                ref=_delay(send, sr, d_s, fb, 420.0, 4800.0, 0.8))
            dly_info = {"ms": round(d_s * 1000, 1), "note": note, "feedback": round(fb, 2), "mode": mode}

    for p in parts.values():
        bus += p.astype(np.float32)

    # optional beat room: separate quiet bus fed by the beat's top end; beat itself is not touched
    glue = None
    if beat is not None and glue_db is not None and lead:
        b = np.asarray(beat, np.float64)
        bm = b.mean(axis=0) if b.ndim == 2 else b
        bm = bm[:n] if len(bm) >= n else np.pad(bm, (0, n - len(bm)))
        top = sg.sosfiltfilt(_bp(1800, 9000, sr), bm)
        w = _conv(top, _plate_ir(sr, 0.38, 0.0, 1500.0, 9000.0, seed + 2))
        w = w * (_rms(top) * 10 ** (glue_db / 20.0) / _rms(w))
        bus += w.astype(np.float32)
        glue = {"level_db_vs_beat_top": glue_db, "beat_top_change_db": round(_db(_rms(top + w.mean(axis=0)) / _rms(top)), 2)}

    act_mask = np.interp(np.arange(n), (np.arange(len(act)) + 0.5) * hop, act) > 0.5
    duck_meas = None                                   # mean gain reduction applied to the plate while singing / in gaps
    if act_mask.any() and (~act_mask).any():
        duck_meas = {"while_singing_db": round(-_db(float(duck_r[act_mask].mean())), 1),
                     "in_gaps_db": round(-_db(float(duck_r[~act_mask].mean())), 1)}
    rep = {
        "analysis": dict({k: (round(v, 2) if isinstance(v, float) else v) for k, v in an.items() if k != "phrases"},
                         phrases=len(an["phrases"]), density=round(dens, 2)),
        "genre": _genre(genre), "role": role, "bpm": bpm, "space": space,
        "plate": {"rt60_s": round(rt60, 2), "predelay_ms": round(pre_ms, 1), "hpf": hpf, "lpf": round(lpf), "deess_db": round(deess, 1),
                  "duck_db": round(duck_db, 1), "duck_release_ms": round(rel_ms), "duck_applied": duck_meas},
        "delay": dly_info, "beat_glue": glue, "open_levels_vs_dry_db": lv, "average_after_duck_db": lv_avg, "seconds": round(time.time() - t0, 1)}
    return (bus, rep, parts) if return_parts else (bus, rep)


==============================================================================
FILE: fx-api/api/wrap3d.py   (97 lines)
==============================================================================

"""3D Head-Wrap — spatial wrap for lead vocal. M/S, mono-safe."""
import numpy as np
import scipy.signal as signal


def _biquad_to_sos(b, a):
    return np.array([[b[0], b[1], b[2], a[0], a[1], a[2]]], dtype=np.float32)


def _soft_clip_knee(x, ceiling=0.98, threshold_db=-3.0):
    threshold = 10.0 ** (threshold_db / 20.0)
    out = x.copy()
    mask = np.abs(x) > threshold
    if np.any(mask):
        sgn = np.sign(x[mask])
        abs_x = np.abs(x[mask])
        scaled = (abs_x - threshold) / (ceiling - threshold)
        out[mask] = sgn * (threshold + (ceiling - threshold) * np.tanh(scaled))
    return np.clip(out, -ceiling, ceiling)


def _shelf_coeffs(freq_hz, gain_db, s=0.707, sr=44100):
    w0 = 2.0 * np.pi * freq_hz / sr
    A = 10.0 ** (gain_db / 40.0)
    alpha = np.sin(w0) / 2.0 * np.sqrt((A + 1.0 / A) * (1.0 / s - 1.0) + 2.0)
    cw = np.cos(w0)
    b = [A * ((A + 1.0) - (A - 1.0) * cw + 2.0 * np.sqrt(A) * alpha),
         2.0 * A * ((A - 1.0) - (A + 1.0) * cw),
         A * ((A + 1.0) - (A - 1.0) * cw - 2.0 * np.sqrt(A) * alpha)]
    a = [(A + 1.0) + (A - 1.0) * cw + 2.0 * np.sqrt(A) * alpha,
         -2.0 * ((A - 1.0) + (A + 1.0) * cw),
         (A + 1.0) + (A - 1.0) * cw - 2.0 * np.sqrt(A) * alpha]
    return b, a


def _peaking_coeffs(freq_hz, gain_db, q, sr=44100):
    w0 = 2.0 * np.pi * freq_hz / sr
    alpha = np.sin(w0) / (2.0 * q)
    A = 10.0 ** (gain_db / 40.0)
    b = [1.0 + alpha * A, -2.0 * np.cos(w0), 1.0 - alpha * A]
    a = [1.0 + alpha / A, -2.0 * np.cos(w0), 1.0 - alpha / A]
    return b, a


def apply_wrap(vocal_mono, sr=44100, instrumental_rms=-14.0):
    a = np.asarray(vocal_mono, dtype=np.float32)
    if a.ndim == 2:
        a = a.mean(axis=0)
    n = a.shape[0]
    if n < 64:
        return np.stack([a, a]).astype(np.float32)

    # Base path
    wrap = a.copy()
    sos_hp = signal.butter(4, 180.0 / (sr * 0.5), btype="highpass", output="sos")
    wrap = signal.sosfilt(sos_hp, wrap).astype(np.float32)

    b, aa = _peaking_coeffs(2500.0, -3.5, 1.4, sr)
    wrap = signal.sosfilt(_biquad_to_sos(b, aa), wrap).astype(np.float32)

    b, aa = _shelf_coeffs(11000.0, 2.0, 0.707, sr)
    wrap = signal.sosfilt(_biquad_to_sos(b, aa), wrap).astype(np.float32)

    # Haas delays
    dL = int(round(0.011 * sr))
    dR = int(round(0.023 * sr))
    wrap_L = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float32)
    wrap_R = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float32)
    if dL < n:
        wrap_L[dL:] = wrap[:-dL]
    if dR < n:
        wrap_R[dR:] = wrap[:-dR]

    # Head-shadow shelves
    b, aa = _shelf_coeffs(4000.0, -2.0, 0.707, sr)
    wrap_R = signal.sosfilt(_biquad_to_sos(b, aa), wrap_R).astype(np.float32)
    b, aa = _shelf_coeffs(4000.0, +2.0, 0.707, sr)
    wrap_L = signal.sosfilt(_biquad_to_sos(b, aa), wrap_L).astype(np.float32)

    # Adaptive gain: -14 dB base, +2 dB if dense
    gain = 0.2
    if instrumental_rms > -12.0:
        gain *= 10.0 ** (2.0 / 20.0)
    wrap_L = wrap_L * gain
    wrap_R = wrap_R * gain

    # M/S: keep half mid for mono depth
    wm = 0.5 * (wrap_L + wrap_R)
    ws = 0.5 * (wrap_L - wrap_R)
    out_L = a + 0.5 * wm + ws
    out_R = a + 0.5 * wm - ws

    # Knee soft clip
    out_L = _soft_clip_knee(out_L, 0.98, -3.0)
    out_R = _soft_clip_knee(out_R, 0.98, -3.0)

    return np.stack([out_L, out_R]).astype(np.float32)


==============================================================================
FILE: fx-api/api/wratty_fix_v2.py   (233 lines)
==============================================================================

"""
wratty_fix_v2.py  -  drop-in adaptive fixes for the wratty-mix engine
numpy + scipy only.  Every function measures its OWN input in its OWN units,
so there is no scale mismatch with the engine's internal metrics.
"""
import numpy as np
from scipy import signal

EPS = 1e-12

BANDS = [("sub", 20, 60), ("b60", 60, 120), ("lm", 120, 250), ("m250", 250, 500),
         ("m500", 500, 1000), ("m1k", 1000, 2000), ("p2k", 2000, 4000),
         ("p4k", 4000, 6000), ("t6k", 6000, 9000), ("a9k", 9000, 12000)]
MASTER_WIN = {"sub": (-9.1, -2.5), "b60": (-8.6, -4.4), "lm": (-10.2, -5.6),
              "m250": (-11.1, -7.4), "m500": (-12.5, -8.7), "m1k": (-16.9, -11.5),
              "p2k": (-22.1, -15.6), "p4k": (-24.5, -22.0), "t6k": (-25.6, -21.7),
              "a9k": (-30.7, -24.0)}
CAPS = {"m250": (-1.5, 1.5), "m500": (-1.5, 1.5), "m1k": (-1.5, 1.5),
        "p2k": (-2.0, 2.0), "p4k": (-2.0, 3.0), "t6k": (-2.0, 3.0), "a9k": (-2.0, 3.0)}
MARGIN_DB = 0.5
DEADBAND_DB = 0.25
SIB_TOLERANCE_DB = 1.5

def _mono(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, np.float32)
    if x.ndim == 1:
        return x
    if x.shape[0] <= 2 and x.shape[1] > 2:
        return x.mean(axis=0)  # (channels, samples)
    return x.mean(axis=1)      # (samples, channels)

def rel_profile(x, sr):
    fr, p = signal.welch(_mono(x), sr, nperseg=8192)
    tot = p.sum() + EPS
    out = []
    for _, lo, hi in BANDS:
        sel = (fr >= lo) & (fr < min(hi, sr / 2))
        out.append(10 * np.log10(p[sel].sum() / tot + EPS))
    return np.array(out)

def _predict(prof, g):
    lin = 10 ** (prof / 10)
    gl = 10 ** (g / 10)
    total_new = (lin * gl).sum() + max(0.0, 1.0 - lin.sum())
    return prof + g - 10 * np.log10(total_new)

def plan_tone(prof):
    g = np.zeros(len(BANDS))
    for _ in range(6):
        pred = _predict(prof, g)
        for i, (n, _, _) in enumerate(BANDS):
            if n not in CAPS:
                continue
            wlo, whi = MASTER_WIN[n]
            if pred[i] < wlo:
                need = (wlo + MARGIN_DB) - pred[i]
            elif pred[i] > whi:
                need = (whi - MARGIN_DB) - pred[i]
            else:
                need = 0.0
            cmin, cmax = CAPS[n]
            g[i] = np.clip(g[i] + 0.7 * need, cmin, cmax)
    g[np.abs(g) < DEADBAND_DB] = 0.0
    return g

def _fir(g_db, sr, taps=4097):
    names = [n for n, _, _ in BANDS]
    centers = [np.sqrt(lo * hi) for _, lo, hi in BANDS]
    idx = [i for i, n in enumerate(names) if n in CAPS]
    fx = np.array([100.0, 180.0] + [centers[i] for i in idx] + [sr / 2 * 0.999])
    gy = np.array([0.0, 0.0] + [g_db[i] for i in idx] + [g_db[idx[-1]]])
    f = np.geomspace(100, sr / 2 * 0.999, 400)
    gd = np.interp(np.log(f), np.log(fx), gy)
    f = np.concatenate(([0.0], f, [sr / 2]))
    amp = 10 ** (np.concatenate(([0.0], gd, [gd[-1]])) / 20)
    return signal.firwin2(taps, f / (sr / 2), amp)

def _apply_fir(x, h):
    x = np.asarray(x, np.float32)
    if x.ndim == 1:
        return signal.fftconvolve(x, h, mode="same").astype(np.float32)
    if x.shape[0] <= 2 and x.shape[1] > 2:
        # (channels, samples)
        return np.stack([signal.fftconvolve(x[c, :], h, mode="same")
                         for c in range(x.shape[0])], axis=0).astype(np.float32)
    # (samples, channels)
    return np.stack([signal.fftconvolve(x[:, c], h, mode="same")
                     for c in range(x.shape[1])], axis=1).astype(np.float32)

def sib_index(x, sr):
    m = _mono(x)
    a = signal.sosfilt(signal.butter(4, [5000, 9000], "band", fs=sr, output="sos"), m)
    b = signal.sosfilt(signal.butter(4, [1000, 4000], "band", fs=sr, output="sos"), m)
    w = int(0.02 * sr)
    n = len(m) // w
    ra = np.sqrt((a[:n * w].reshape(n, w) ** 2).mean(1))
    rb = np.sqrt((b[:n * w].reshape(n, w) ** 2).mean(1))
    r = 20 * np.log10((ra + 1e-9) / (rb + 1e-9))
    act = rb > np.percentile(rb, 50)
    return float(np.percentile(r[act], 95))

def master_tone_correct(x, sr):
    names = [n for n, _, _ in BANDS]
    prof = rel_profile(x, sr)
    g = plan_tone(prof)
    rep = {"before_rel_db": {n: float(v) for n, v in zip(names, np.round(prof, 1))},
           "gain_db": {n: float(v) for n, v in zip(names, np.round(g, 2))},
           "applied": False, "sib_guard": False, "at_cap": []}
    for i, n in enumerate(names):
        if n in CAPS and (abs(g[i] - CAPS[n][1]) < 1e-6 or abs(g[i] - CAPS[n][0]) < 1e-6):
            rep["at_cap"].append(n)
    if np.max(np.abs(g)) < DEADBAND_DB:
        return np.asarray(x, np.float32), rep
    y = _apply_fir(x, _fir(g, sr))
    s0, s1 = sib_index(x, sr), sib_index(y, sr)
    rep["sib_before"], rep["sib_after"] = round(s0, 1), round(s1, 1)
    if s1 - s0 > SIB_TOLERANCE_DB:
        g2 = g.copy()
        for i, n in enumerate(names):
            if n in ("p4k", "t6k", "a9k") and g2[i] > 0:
                g2[i] *= 0.5
        y = _apply_fir(x, _fir(g2, sr))
        rep["sib_guard"] = True
        rep["gain_db"] = dict(zip(names, np.round(g2, 2)))
        rep["sib_after"] = round(float(sib_index(y, sr)), 1)
    rep["applied"] = True
    rep["after_rel_db"] = {n: float(v) for n, v in zip(names, np.round(rel_profile(y, sr), 1))}
    return y, rep

CARVE = [("lowmid", 150, 350, -8.0, 3.0, 1.0),
         ("mid",    500, 2000, 0.0, 1.5, 1.0),
         ("pres",  2000, 4500, -3.0, 3.0, 1.5),
         ("top",   4500, 8000, -3.0, 2.0, 0.5)]

def _frame_db(x, w):
    n = len(x) // w
    return 10 * np.log10((x[:n * w].reshape(n, w) ** 2).mean(1) + 1e-12)

def _smooth_env(db, hop_s, atk_ms=10.0, rel_ms=150.0):
    ka = np.exp(-hop_s * 1000.0 / atk_ms)
    kr = np.exp(-hop_s * 1000.0 / rel_ms)
    out = np.empty_like(db)
    y = db[0]
    for i, v in enumerate(db):
        k = ka if v > y else kr
        y = k * y + (1 - k) * v
        out[i] = y
    return out

def carve_beat(beat, vocal, sr, fallback=False):
    b = np.asarray(beat, np.float32)
    bm, v = _mono(b), _mono(vocal)
    n = min(len(bm), len(v))
    w = int(0.05 * sr)
    gate = signal.sosfiltfilt(signal.butter(4, [300, 3000], "band", fs=sr, output="sos"), v[:n])
    gdb = _frame_db(gate, w)
    act = gdb > (np.percentile(gdb, 95) - 30)
    out = b.copy()
    rep = {}
    hop = int(0.005 * sr)
    for name, lo, hi, floor, cap, fb in CARVE:
        sos = signal.butter(4, [lo, hi], "band", fs=sr, output="sos")
        vb = signal.sosfiltfilt(sos, v[:n])
        bb = signal.sosfiltfilt(sos, bm[:n])
        vp = (vb[:len(act) * w].reshape(len(act), w) ** 2).mean(1)
        bp = (bb[:len(act) * w].reshape(len(act), w) ** 2).mean(1)
        C = 10 * np.log10(vp[act].mean() + EPS) - 10 * np.log10(bp[act].mean() + EPS)
        depth = min(cap, 0.6 * (floor - C)) if C < floor else 0.0
        if fallback:
            depth = max(depth, fb)
        rep[name] = {"C_db": round(float(C), 1), "floor_db": float(floor),
                     "depth_db": round(float(depth), 2), "at_cap": bool(depth >= cap - 1e-6)}
        if depth < 0.1:
            continue
        env = _smooth_env(_frame_db(vb, hop), hop / sr)
        thr = np.percentile(env, 95) - 30.0
        a = np.clip((env - thr) / 12.0, 0.0, 1.0)
        centers = (np.arange(len(a)) + 0.5) * hop
        gain_db = np.interp(np.arange(len(b)), centers, -depth * a)
        g_lin = (10 ** (gain_db / 20) - 1.0).astype(np.float32)
        if out.ndim == 1:
            out += signal.sosfiltfilt(sos, b).astype(np.float32) * g_lin
        else:
            for c in range(out.shape[1]):
                out[:, c] += signal.sosfiltfilt(sos, b[:, c]).astype(np.float32) * g_lin
    return out, rep

def qa(x, sr):
    x = np.asarray(x, np.float32)
    names = [n for n, _, _ in BANDS]
    prof = rel_profile(x, sr)
    fr, p = signal.welch(_mono(x), sr, nperseg=8192)
    bp = lambda lo, hi: 10 * np.log10(p[(fr >= lo) & (fr < hi)].sum() + EPS)
    top_minus_pres = bp(5500, 10000) - bp(2000, 5500)
    res = {"rel_db": {n: float(v) for n, v in zip(names, np.round(prof, 1))},
           "top_minus_presence_db": round(float(top_minus_pres), 1),
           "sib_index": round(float(sib_index(x, sr)), 1)}
    if x.ndim == 2:
        L, R = x[:, 0], x[:, 1]
        M, S = (L + R) / 2, (L - R) / 2
        res["side_minus_mid_db"] = round(float(10 * np.log10((S ** 2).mean() / ((M ** 2).mean() + EPS) + EPS)), 1)
        res["mono_loss_db"] = round(float(20 * np.log10(np.sqrt((M ** 2).mean()) / np.sqrt((x ** 2).mean() + EPS) + EPS)), 2)
    m = _mono(x)
    res["crest_db"] = round(float(20 * np.log10(np.abs(x).max() / (np.sqrt((m ** 2).mean()) + EPS))), 1)
    res["clipped_samples"] = int((np.abs(x) >= 0.999).sum())
    fails = []
    for i, n in enumerate(names):
        if n in CAPS:
            lo, hi = MASTER_WIN[n]
            if prof[i] < lo - 1.0 or prof[i] > hi + 1.0:
                fails.append(f"{n} {prof[i]:.1f} outside {lo}..{hi} (+/-1)")
    if res.get("mono_loss_db", 0) < -0.5:
        fails.append("mono loss > 0.5 dB")
    if res["clipped_samples"] > 0:
        fails.append("clipped samples")
    res["fails"], res["pass"] = fails, not fails
    return res

def needs_declip(x, thr=0.999, min_run=3, min_runs=20):
    a = np.abs(np.asarray(x, np.float32))
    if a.ndim == 2:
        a = a.max(axis=1)
    hit = np.concatenate(([0], (a >= thr).astype(np.int8), [0]))
    d = np.diff(hit)
    starts, ends = np.where(d == 1)[0], np.where(d == -1)[0]
    return int(((ends - starts) >= min_run).sum()) > min_runs

def tail_warning(stem, keep_samples, floor_db=-50.0):
    tail = _mono(stem)[keep_samples:]
    if len(tail) == 0:
        return False, -120.0
    lvl = 20 * np.log10(np.sqrt((tail.astype(np.float64) ** 2).mean()) + 1e-9)
    return bool(lvl > floor_db), float(lvl)

import os
import hmac
import modal

app = modal.App("wratty-cleanup")
SECRET = modal.Secret.from_name("wratty-mix")
mixvol = modal.Volume.from_name("wratty-mixes", create_if_missing=True)


@app.function(
    image=modal.Image.debian_slim().pip_install("fastapi[standard]"),
    secrets=[SECRET],
    volumes={"/mixes": mixvol},
)
@modal.asgi_app()
def api():
    from fastapi import FastAPI, Request
    from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse

    web = FastAPI()

    @web.post("/delete")
    async def delete(request: Request):
        key = os.environ.get("FX_INTERNAL_SECRET", "")
        auth = request.headers.get("authorization", "")
        if not key or not hmac.compare_digest(auth, "Bearer " + key):
            return JSONResponse({"error": "unauthorized"}, status_code=401)
        body = await request.json()
        keys = body.get("keys") or []
        deleted, missing, bad = [], [], []
        for k in keys:
            k = str(k)
            if not (k.startswith("fx/") and (k.endswith(".mp3") or k.endswith(".flac") or k.endswith(".wav"))):
                bad.append(k)
                continue
            try:
                os.remove(os.path.join("/mixes", k))
                deleted.append(k)
            except FileNotFoundError:
                missing.append(k)
            except Exception:
                bad.append(k)
        if deleted:
            mixvol.commit()
        return JSONResponse({"ok": True, "deleted": deleted, "missing": missing, "bad": bad})

    return web


# Runs daily at 03:00 UTC. Starts in DRY-RUN: it only logs what it WOULD
# delete, so the policy can be reviewed before anything is removed.
# Flip `{"live": True}` to enable real deletion.
@app.function(
    image=modal.Image.debian_slim(),
    secrets=[SECRET],
    schedule=modal.Cron("0 3 * * *"),
    timeout=600,
)
def retention_sweep():
    import json
    import os
    import urllib.request

    app_url = (os.environ.get("APP_URL") or "").rstrip("/")
    key = os.environ.get("FX_INTERNAL_SECRET", "")
    if not app_url or not key:
        print("retention: APP_URL or FX_INTERNAL_SECRET missing", flush=True)
        return

    body = json.dumps({"live": True}).encode()    # LIVE
    req = urllib.request.Request(
        app_url + "/api/retention/sweep",
        data=body,
        headers={"Content-Type": "application/json",
                 "Authorization": "Bearer " + key},
        method="POST",
    )
    try:
        # 180s: the Render service may be cold-starting after 15 min idle.
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=180) as r:
            print("retention:", r.status, r.read()[:600].decode(), flush=True)
    except Exception as e:
        print("retention failed:", e, flush=True)

FILE: modal_files.py   (169 lines)

"""Serves - and now accepts - files on the wratty-mixes volume."""
import hashlib
import hmac
import os
import pathlib
import time
from urllib.parse import quote

import modal

VOL = "wratty-mixes"
MOUNT = "/mixes"
FILES_BASE = "https://wrattyg--wratty-files-web.modal.run"
MAX_UPLOAD = 512 * 1024 * 1024      # per-file cap
SECRET_NAME = "wratty-mix"          # reuses FX_INTERNAL_SECRET

app = modal.App("wratty-files")
vol = modal.Volume.from_name(VOL, create_if_missing=True)

image = modal.Image.debian_slim().pip_install("fastapi[standard]")

MEDIA = {".mp3": "audio/mpeg", ".flac": "audio/flac",
         ".wav": "audio/wav", ".m4a": "audio/mp4"}
HDRS = {"cache-control": "public, max-age=31536000, immutable",
        "access-control-allow-origin": "*"}


@app.function(image=image, volumes={MOUNT: vol},
              secrets=[modal.Secret.from_name(SECRET_NAME)],
              max_containers=4, timeout=300)
@modal.asgi_app()
def web():
    import threading
    from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request
    from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
    from fastapi.responses import FileResponse

    api = FastAPI()
    api.add_middleware(CORSMiddleware, allow_origins=["*"],
                       allow_methods=["GET", "PUT", "OPTIONS"],
                       allow_headers=["*"], max_age=86400)

    ROOT = pathlib.Path(MOUNT).resolve()
    LOCK = threading.Lock()
    KEY = os.environ.get("FX_INTERNAL_SECRET", "")

    def resolve(key):
        p = (ROOT / key).resolve()
        if not str(p).startswith(str(ROOT)):
            return None
        return p

    def ticket_ok(req, scope="stem-upload"):
        """Verifies the short-lived ticket minted by the Next.js app.
        The signing secret itself never leaves the two servers.

        Reads from the x-fx-ticket header (server-to-server) OR the ?t=
        query param (browser downloads, which cannot add custom headers)."""
        if not KEY:
            return False
        tok = req.headers.get("x-fx-ticket", "") or req.query_params.get("t", "")
        try:
            exp_s, sig = tok.split(".", 1)
            exp = int(exp_s)
        except Exception:
            return False
        if abs(time.time() - exp) > 900:      # +-15 min, tolerant of clock skew
            return False
        want = hmac.new(KEY.encode(), ("%s|%d" % (scope, exp)).encode(),
                        hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()
        return hmac.compare_digest(want, sig)

    def listing(prefix=""):
        out = []
        if ROOT.is_dir():
            for p in ROOT.rglob("*"):
                if p.is_file() and not p.name.endswith(".part"):
                    k = str(p.relative_to(ROOT))
                    if k.startswith(prefix):
                        out.append(k)
        return sorted(out)

    @api.get("/")
    def index():
        return {"ok": True, "service": "wratty-files",
                "mounted": ROOT.is_dir(), "base": "/f/<key>"}

    @api.get("/health")
    def health():
        return {"ok": True, "mounted": ROOT.is_dir(), "mount": MOUNT}

    @api.get("/ls")
    def ls(request: Request, prefix: str = ""):
        # Was public - it listed every render on the volume.
        if not ticket_ok(request, "read"):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="bad or expired ticket")
        if not ROOT.is_dir():
            return {"count": 0, "files": [], "note": "volume not mounted"}
        ks = listing(prefix)
        return {"count": len(ks), "files": ks[:200]}

    @api.put("/u/{key:path}")
    async def upload(key: str, request: Request):
        if not ticket_ok(request):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="bad or expired ticket")
        p = resolve(key)
        if p is None or key.startswith("/") or ".." in key:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="bad key")
        if p.exists():
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="already exists: " + key)

        p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        tmp = p.with_suffix(p.suffix + ".part")
        n = 0
        with open(tmp, "wb") as fh:
            async for chunk in request.stream():
                n += len(chunk)
                if n > MAX_UPLOAD:
                    fh.close()
                    tmp.unlink(missing_ok=True)
                    raise HTTPException(status_code=413, detail="too large")
                fh.write(chunk)
        if n == 0:
            tmp.unlink(missing_ok=True)
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="empty body")
        os.replace(tmp, p)                   # atomic - never a half file
        try:
            vol.commit()                     # publish before we say OK
        except Exception as e:
            print("upload commit failed: %s" % e, flush=True)
        return {"key": key, "bytes": n,
                "url": "%s/f/%s" % (FILES_BASE, quote(key))}

    @api.get("/f/{key:path}")
    def get_file(key: str, request: Request):
        # Was fully public: anyone with the hostname could pull any render.
        if not ticket_ok(request, "read"):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="bad or expired ticket")
        p = resolve(key)
        if p is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="bad key")
        if not p.is_file():
            # Miss: another container may have just committed. reload() only
            # here - it errors "volume busy" while files are open, and shows
            # the volume empty for a moment, so never on the hot path.
            with LOCK:
                if not p.is_file():
                    try:
                        vol.reload()
                    except Exception as e:
                        print("reload failed: %s" % e, flush=True)
                        time.sleep(0.5)
            p = resolve(key)
        if p is None or not p.is_file():
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="not found: " + key,
                                headers={"cache-control": "no-store"})
        hdr = dict(HDRS)
        dl_name = request.query_params.get("name")
        if dl_name:
            safe = "".join(ch for ch in dl_name if 32 <= ord(ch) < 127) or "download"
            hdr["content-disposition"] = 'attachment; filename="%s"' % safe.replace('"', "")
            hdr["cache-control"] = "private, no-store"
        return FileResponse(
            p,
            media_type=MEDIA.get(p.suffix.lower(), "application/octet-stream"),
            headers=hdr,
        )

    return api

import uuid
import time

import modal

app = modal.App("wratty-mix")

image = (
    modal.Image.debian_slim(python_version="3.12")
    .apt_install("libsndfile1", "ca-certificates")
    .pip_install("numpy", "scipy", "numba", "pedalboard>=0.9.25", "requests", "fastapi", "soundfile", "boto3", "urllib3", "certifi")
    .add_local_dir("fx-api/api", remote_path="/root/api")
)

SECRET = modal.Secret.from_name("wratty-mix")
try:
    R2_SECRET = modal.Secret.from_name("wratty-r2")
except Exception:
    R2_SECRET = None
jobs = modal.Dict.from_name("wratty-mix-jobs", create_if_missing=True)

# Mix renders are written here and served by the separate wratty-files app.
mixvol = modal.Volume.from_name("wratty-mixes", create_if_missing=True)


@app.function(image=image, cpu=2.0, memory=8192, timeout=3600, secrets=[SECRET] + ([R2_SECRET] if R2_SECRET else []),
              volumes={"/mixes": mixvol})
def run_mix(job_id: str, stems: list, loudness: str, preset: str = "neutral", max_seconds: float = 0):
    import sys
    import traceback
    import os
    sys.path.insert(0, "/root/api")
    sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "fx-api/api"))
    import mix
    jobs[job_id] = {"status": "running"}
    try:
        res = mix.do_mix(stems, loudness, job_id, max_seconds, preset)
        try:
            mixvol.commit()      # publish the render to wratty-files
        except Exception as _e:
            print("volume commit failed: %s" % _e, flush=True)
        try:
            import json as _j
            _safe = {}
            for _k, _v in (res or {}).items():
                try:
                    _j.dumps(_v); _safe[_k] = _v
                except Exception:
                    _safe[_k] = str(_v)[:200]
            _rpt = _j.dumps(_safe, default=str)
            print("MIXREPORT " + _rpt[:6000], flush=True)
            try:
                _rp = os.path.join("/mixes", "reports", "%s.json" % job_id)
                os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(_rp), exist_ok=True)
                with open(_rp, "w") as _f:
                    _f.write(_rpt)
                print("REPORT_SAVED " + _rp, flush=True)
                try:
                    mixvol.commit()
                except Exception as _ce:
                    print("report commit failed: %s" % _ce, flush=True)
            except Exception as _re:
                print("REPORT_SAVE_ERR " + str(_re)[:200], flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass
        import math as _m
        def _clean(o):
            if isinstance(o, float) and (_m.isnan(o) or _m.isinf(o)):
                return None
            if isinstance(o, dict):
                return {k: _clean(v) for k, v in o.items()}
            if isinstance(o, (list, tuple)):
                return [_clean(v) for v in o]
            return o
        jobs[job_id] = {
            "status": "done",
            "url": res.get("url", ""),
            "result": _clean(res),
        }
    except Exception as e:
        traceback.print_exc()
        jobs[job_id] = {"status": "failed", "error": str(e)[:300]}
    finally:
        pass


@app.function(
    image=image,
    cpu=4.0,
    memory=8192,
    timeout=600,
    scaledown_window=300,
    secrets=[SECRET],
)
@modal.asgi_app()
def api():
    print("Forcing clear re-sync validation channel: 1791533703")
    import os

    from fastapi import FastAPI, Request
    from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse

    web = FastAPI()
    key = os.environ.get("FX_INTERNAL_SECRET", "")

    @web.get("/")
    async def status(request: Request):
        jid = request.query_params.get("id")
        if not jid:
            return {"ok": True}
        if not key or request.headers.get("authorization") != "Bearer " + key:
            return JSONResponse({"error": "unauthorized"}, status_code=401)
        import math as _m
        def _cl(o):
            if isinstance(o, float) and (_m.isnan(o) or _m.isinf(o)):
                return None
            if isinstance(o, dict):
                return {k: _cl(v) for k, v in o.items()}
            if isinstance(o, (list, tuple)):
                return [_cl(v) for v in o]
            return o
        return JSONResponse(_cl((await jobs.get.aio(jid)) or {"status": "none"}))

    @web.post("/")
    async def start(request: Request):
        if not key or request.headers.get("authorization") != "Bearer " + key:
            return JSONResponse({"error": "unauthorized"}, status_code=401)
        try:
            body = await request.json()
        except Exception:
            return JSONResponse({"error": "bad json"}, status_code=400)

        stems = body.get("stems")
        if not isinstance(stems, list) or not stems:
            return JSONResponse({"error": "stems[] required"}, status_code=400)
        if len(stems) > 12:
            return JSONResponse({"error": "max 12 stems"}, status_code=400)
        for s in stems:
            if not str(s.get("url") or "").startswith("https://"):
                return JSONResponse(
                    {"error": "each stem needs an https url"}, status_code=400
                )
        # NO global lock: every render runs in its own Modal container, so
        # parallel mixes are fine. The Postgres queue is the ONLY authority
        # on how many run at once.
        jid = uuid.uuid4().hex
        await jobs.put.aio(jid, {"status": "queued"})
        await run_mix.spawn.aio(
            jid,
            stems,
            str(body.get("loudness") or "MEDIUM"),
            str(body.get("preset") or "neutral"),
            float(body.get("maxSeconds") or 0),
        )
        return {"job": jid}

    return web

@app.function(image=image, cpu=0.5, memory=1024, timeout=180)
def engine_probe():
    import numpy as np
    import sys, os
    sys.path.insert(0, "/root/api")
    import dsp_delay
    import dsp_pro

    sr = 44100
    dur = 5.0
    n = int(sr * dur)
    t = np.arange(n) / sr
    x = (0.3 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 220 * t) * (0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 2 * t))).astype(np.float32)
    xs = np.stack([x, x])

    out = {}
    wet, info = dsp_delay.delay_render(xs, sr, bpm=99.4, note_div=0.25)
    dry_rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(xs ** 2)))
    wet_rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(wet ** 2)))
    out["delay_dry_rms"]   = round(dry_rms, 6)
    out["delay_wet_rms"]   = round(wet_rms, 6)
    out["delay_rel_db"]    = round(20.0 * np.log10(wet_rms / dry_rms + 1e-12), 2)
    out["delay_info"]      = info

    plate = dsp_pro.plate_wet(xs, sr, mix=1.0, duck_depth=0.25)
    if plate is None:
        out["plate"] = "returned None"
    else:
        p_rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(plate ** 2)))
        out["plate_dry_rms"] = round(dry_rms, 6)
        out["plate_wet_rms"] = round(p_rms, 6)
        out["plate_rel_db"]  = round(20.0 * np.log10(p_rms / dry_rms + 1e-12), 2)
    return out

FILE: modal_tune.py   (113 lines)

import os
import modal
import uuid
import numpy as np
import soundfile as sf

app = modal.App("wratty-autotune")
volume = modal.Volume.from_name("wratty-mixes", create_if_missing=True)

image = (modal.Image.debian_slim(python_version="3.11")
         .pip_install("numpy", "scipy", "soundfile", "requests", "fastapi[standard]")
         .add_local_python_source("vocaltune"))

NOTE_NAMES = ["C", "C#", "D", "D#", "E", "F", "F#", "G", "G#", "A", "A#", "B"]

def midi_to_name(m):
    return f"{NOTE_NAMES[int(m) % 12]}{int(m) // 12 - 1}"

@app.function(image=image, volumes={"/mixes": volume}, timeout=600, cpu=2.0, memory=4096)
def tune_job(job_id: str, vocal_url: str, beat_url: str = None, preset: str = None, manual_key: str = None):
    progress_dict = modal.Dict.from_name("tuning-progress", create_if_missing=True)
    progress_dict[job_id] = {"status": "running", "pct": 10, "stage": "Initializing..."}

    try:
        from vocaltune.io_utils import read_audio, write_audio
        from vocaltune.engine import VocalTuner
        
        progress_dict[job_id] = {"status": "running", "pct": 15, "stage": "Downloading audio files..."}

        import requests
        from io import BytesIO
        vocal_res = requests.get(vocal_url)
        vocal_res.raise_for_status()
        vocal_data, sr = sf.read(BytesIO(vocal_res.content))
        if vocal_data.ndim > 1: vocal_data = np.mean(vocal_data, axis=1)

        beat_data = None
        beat_sr = None
        if beat_url:
            progress_dict[job_id] = {"status": "running", "pct": 20, "stage": "Downloading beat..."}
            beat_res = requests.get(beat_url)
            beat_res.raise_for_status()
            beat_data, beat_sr = sf.read(BytesIO(beat_res.content))
            if beat_data.ndim > 1: beat_data = np.mean(beat_data, axis=1)

        progress_dict[job_id] = {"status": "running", "pct": 40, "stage": f"Tuning ({preset})..."}

        tuner = VocalTuner()
        result = tuner.process(
            vocal_data, 
            sr, 
            genre=preset or "afrobeats", 
            beat=beat_data, 
            beat_sr=beat_sr,
            key=manual_key
        )

        progress_dict[job_id] = {"status": "running", "pct": 85, "stage": "Rendering..."}

        os.makedirs("/mixes/fx", exist_ok=True)
        out = f"/mixes/fx/tuned-{job_id}.wav"
        write_audio(out, result.audio, sr, bits=24)
        volume.commit()

        notes_list = []
        for n in result.notes:
            st = float(n.start * result.correction.hop_s)
            et = float(n.end * result.correction.hop_s)
            pm = int(n.target_midi)
            notes_list.append({
                "startTime": round(st, 4),
                "endTime": round(et, 4),
                "duration": round(et - st, 4),
                "pitchMidi": pm,
                "noteName": midi_to_name(pm)
            })

        payload = {
            "status": "done",
            "pct": 100,
            "stage": "Done",
            "url": f"https://wrattyg--wratty-files-web.modal.run/f/fx/tuned-{job_id}.wav",
            "detectedKey": result.key,
            "pianoRoll": notes_list,
            "notes": len(notes_list)
        }
        progress_dict[job_id] = payload
        return payload

    except Exception as e:
        import traceback
        traceback.print_exc()
        err = {"status": "error", "error": str(e)[:300]}
        progress_dict[job_id] = err
        raise e

@app.function(image=image, volumes={"/mixes": volume})
@modal.fastapi_endpoint(method="POST")
def start(body: dict):
    url = body.get("url")
    if not url:
        return {"error": "Missing url"}
    job_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
    progress_dict = modal.Dict.from_name("tuning-progress", create_if_missing=True)
    progress_dict[job_id] = {"status": "queued", "pct": 0, "stage": "Queued"}
    tune_job.spawn(job_id, url, body.get("beatUrl"), body.get("preset"), body.get("key"))
    return {"jobId": job_id}

@app.function(image=image, volumes={"/mixes": volume})
@modal.fastapi_endpoint(method="GET")
def status(job_id: str):
    progress_dict = modal.Dict.from_name("tuning-progress", create_if_missing=True)
    return progress_dict.get(job_id) or {"status": "unknown"}
